### [1] [Emotional Modulation in Swarm Decision Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09963)
*David Freire-Obregón*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 扩展蜂群方程为基于智能体的情感调节模型，研究情感效价和唤醒度如何通过调节交互速率影响集体决策的共识形成和收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的蜂群决策理论（蜂群方程）与情感和社会建模相结合，研究情感因素如何调节集体决策过程。传统模型主要关注简单的交互规则，而本研究探索情感维度（效价和唤醒度）如何作为交互速率的调节器，影响共识形成。

Method: 将蜂群方程扩展为基于智能体的模型，其中情感效价（正负）和唤醒度（高低）作为交互速率的调节器，改变招募和交叉抑制参数。智能体显示从其情感状态映射的模拟面部表情，研究情感传染在共识形成中的作用。探索三种场景：1) 效价和唤醒度对共识结果和速度的联合影响；2) 效价匹配时唤醒度打破平局的作用；3) 超过中间支持阈值后共识加速的"雪球效应"。

Result: 情感调节能够通过改变有效招募和抑制速率来偏置决策结果和改变收敛时间。同时，内在的非线性放大即使在完全对称的情感条件下也能产生决定性胜利。具体发现包括：情感不对称性影响决策结果，唤醒度在效价匹配时能打破平局，以及存在共识加速的"雪球效应"。

Conclusion: 该研究将经典蜂群决策理论与情感社会建模联系起来，强调情感不对称性和结构性临界点如何共同塑造集体结果。提出的框架为研究自然和人工系统中集体选择的情感维度提供了灵活工具，揭示了情感因素在集体决策中的重要作用。

Abstract: Collective decision-making in biological and human groups often emerges from simple interaction rules that amplify minor differences into consensus. The bee equation, developed initially to describe nest-site selection in honeybee swarms, captures this dynamic through recruitment and inhibition processes. Here, we extend the bee equation into an agent-based model in which emotional valence (positive-negative) and arousal (low-high) act as modulators of interaction rates, effectively altering the recruitment and cross-inhibition parameters. Agents display simulated facial expressions mapped from their valence-arousal states, allowing the study of emotional contagion in consensus formation.
  Three scenarios are explored: (1) the joint effect of valence and arousal on consensus outcomes and speed, (2) the role of arousal in breaking ties when valence is matched, and (3) the "snowball effect" in which consensus accelerates after surpassing intermediate support thresholds. Results show that emotional modulation can bias decision outcomes and alter convergence times by shifting effective recruitment and inhibition rates. At the same time, intrinsic non-linear amplification can produce decisive wins even in fully symmetric emotional conditions.
  These findings link classical swarm decision theory with affective and social modelling, highlighting how both emotional asymmetries and structural tipping points shape collective outcomes. The proposed framework offers a flexible tool for studying the emotional dimensions of collective choice in both natural and artificial systems.

</details>

### [2] [Predictive Control with Indirect Adaptive Laws for Payload Transportation by Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08831)
*Leila Amanzadeh,Taizoon Chunawala,Randall T. Fawcett,Alexander Leonessa,Kaveh Akbari Hamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于四足机器人鲁棒负载运输的分层规划与控制框架，将模型预测控制与基于梯度下降的自适应更新律相结合，实现了对未知负载参数的自适应估计和稳定轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在运输未知或变化负载时面临模型不确定性和稳定性挑战，特别是在复杂地形上。现有方法难以实时适应负载变化并保证系统稳定性，需要开发能够自适应估计未知参数并确保稳定性的分层控制框架。

Method: 采用分层框架：高层使用间接自适应律估计降阶运动模型的未知参数，结合模型预测控制进行实时轨迹规划，并在MPC约束中加入凸稳定性准则保证估计误差稳定性；低层使用非线性全身控制器跟踪高层生成的优化轨迹。

Result: 数值仿真验证了框架能力：机器人能运输未建模的静态负载（平坦地形109%，粗糙地形91%）和动态负载（粗糙地形73%）。与普通MPC和L1 MPC相比性能显著提升。硬件实验在室内外粗糙地形上验证了方法的有效性，能应对负载变化、推力扰动和障碍物等不确定性。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应MPC框架成功解决了四足机器人运输未知负载的挑战，通过分层设计和稳定性约束实现了鲁棒性能，在复杂地形和动态负载条件下表现出色，为机器人自主运输任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper formally develops a novel hierarchical planning and control framework for robust payload transportation by quadrupedal robots, integrating a model predictive control (MPC) algorithm with a gradient-descent-based adaptive updating law. At the framework's high level, an indirect adaptive law estimates the unknown parameters of the reduced-order (template) locomotion model under varying payloads. These estimated parameters feed into an MPC algorithm for real-time trajectory planning, incorporating a convex stability criterion within the MPC constraints to ensure the stability of the template model's estimation error. The optimal reduced-order trajectories generated by the high-level adaptive MPC (AMPC) are then passed to a low-level nonlinear whole-body controller (WBC) for tracking. Extensive numerical investigations validate the framework's capabilities, showcasing the robot's proficiency in transporting unmodeled, unknown static payloads up to 109% in experiments on flat terrains and 91% on rough experimental terrains. The robot also successfully manages dynamic payloads with 73% of its mass on rough terrains. Performance comparisons with a normal MPC and an L1 MPC indicate a significant improvement. Furthermore, comprehensive hardware experiments conducted in indoor and outdoor environments confirm the method's efficacy on rough terrains despite uncertainties such as payload variations, push disturbances, and obstacles.

</details>


### [3] [Age-Related Differences in the Perception of Eye-Gaze from a Social Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08810)
*Lucas Morillo-Mendez,Martien G. S. Schrooten,Oscar Martinez Mozos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究探索社交机器人辅助老年人日常任务时指点性注视的益处，以及年龄相关差异如何影响社交感知，为设计适应性非语言线索提供参考


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着社交机器人辅助老年人日常任务的兴趣增加，非语言线索如指点性注视在人机交互中很重要。然而，对指点性注视的敏感性随年龄自然下降，导致社交感知减少。因此需要探索社交机器人指点性注视对老年人的益处，以及年龄差异如何影响社交感知

Method: 通过对比研究探索社交机器人辅助日常任务时指点性注视的益处，分析年龄相关差异对社交感知的影响，对比老年人和年轻人群体的不同反应

Result: 研究发现指点性注视在社交机器人辅助老年人日常任务中具有益处，但年龄相关差异显著影响社交感知，老年人与年轻人群体的反应存在明显差异

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计适应性年龄相关非语言线索提供了重要参考，有助于优化人机交互中社交机器人对老年人的辅助效果，提升老年人的社交感知和交互体验

Abstract: There is an increasing interest in social robots assisting older adults during daily life tasks. In this context, non-verbal cues such as deictic gaze are important in natural communication in human-robot interaction. However, the sensibility to deictic-gaze declines naturally with age and results in a reduction in social perception. Therefore, this work explores the benefits of deictic gaze from social robots assisting older adults during daily life tasks, and how age-related differences may influence their social perception in contrast to younger populations. This may help on the design of adaptive age-related non-verbal cues in the Human-Robot Interaction context.

</details>


### [4] [SEP-NMPC: Safety Enhanced Passivity-Based Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for a UAV Slung Payload System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08860)
*Seyedreza Rezaei,Junjie Kang,Amaldev Haridevan,Jinjun Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SEP-NMPC：一种结合严格无源性不等式和高阶控制屏障函数的安全增强型无源性非线性模型预测控制框架，为四旋翼吊挂负载运输提供稳定性和安全性保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼飞行器在吊挂负载穿越复杂环境时，同时保证稳定性和无碰撞飞行具有挑战性。传统MPC方法难以在负载摆动情况下提供形式化的稳定性和安全性保证。

Method: 提出SEP-NMPC框架：1）通过基于自适应阻尼的整形能量存储函数推导严格无源性不等式，嵌入NMPC确保稳定性；2）使用高阶控制屏障函数(HOCBFs)保证用户定义的间隙集前向不变，确保四旋翼和摆动负载与静态/动态障碍物保持分离；3）优化问题保持二次规划兼容性，在线实时求解，无需增益调度或启发式切换。

Result: 大量仿真和真实世界实验验证了：1）稳定的负载运输；2）无碰撞轨迹；3）所有测试场景下的实时可行性。框架成功统一了基于无源性的闭环稳定性和基于HOCBF的安全性保证。

Conclusion: SEP-NMPC框架为无人机吊挂负载运输提供了形式化的稳定性和安全性保证，通过无源性不等式确保渐近收敛，通过HOCBFs确保障碍物分离，实现了在复杂环境中的安全稳定飞行。

Abstract: Model Predictive Control (MPC) is widely adopted for agile multirotor vehicles, yet achieving both stability and obstacle-free flight is particularly challenging when a payload is suspended beneath the airframe. This paper introduces a Safety Enhanced Passivity-Based Nonlinear MPC (SEP-NMPC) that provides formal guarantees of stability and safety for a quadrotor transporting a slung payload through cluttered environments. Stability is enforced by embedding a strict passivity inequality, which is derived from a shaped energy storage function with adaptive damping, directly into the NMPC. This formulation dissipates excess energy and ensures asymptotic convergence despite payload swings. Safety is guaranteed through high-order control barrier functions (HOCBFs) that render user-defined clearance sets forward-invariant, obliging both the quadrotor and the swinging payload to maintain separation while interacting with static and dynamic obstacles. The optimization remains quadratic-program compatible and is solved online at each sampling time without gain scheduling or heuristic switching. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments confirm stable payload transport, collision-free trajectories, and real-time feasibility across all tested scenarios. The SEP-NMPC framework therefore unifies passivity-based closed-loop stability with HOCBF-based safety guarantees for UAV slung-payload transportation.

</details>


### [5] [Scale-Plan: Scalable Language-Enabled Task Planning for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08814)
*Piyush Gupta,Sangjae Bae,Jiachen Li,David Isele*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Scale-Plan是一个可扩展的LLM辅助框架，通过构建动作图和使用浅层LLM推理来指导结构化图搜索，生成紧凑的任务相关表示，从而在对象丰富的环境中实现高效的多机器人长时程任务规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多机器人系统的长时程任务规划在实际部署中面临挑战，主要因为大量感知信息中大部分与任务目标无关，给规划带来负担。传统符号规划器依赖手动构建的问题规范，可扩展性和适应性有限；而基于大语言模型的方法在对象丰富的环境中容易出现幻觉和弱接地问题。

Method: 给定PDDL领域规范，Scale-Plan构建捕获领域结构的动作图，使用浅层LLM推理指导结构化图搜索，识别相关动作和对象的最小子集。通过在规划前过滤无关信息，实现高效的任务分解、分配和长时程计划生成。

Result: Scale-Plan在复杂多智能体任务上评估，并引入了MAT2-THOR基准（基于AI2-THOR构建）。Scale-Plan在所有指标上优于纯LLM和混合LLM-PDDL基线方法，显著提高了可扩展性和可靠性。

Conclusion: Scale-Plan通过结合LLM推理和结构化图搜索，在规划前过滤无关信息，有效解决了多机器人长时程规划中的可扩展性和接地问题，为实际环境中的协作团队部署提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon task planning for heterogeneous multi-robot systems is essential for deploying collaborative teams in real-world environments; yet, it remains challenging due to the large volume of perceptual information, much of which is irrelevant to task objectives and burdens planning. Traditional symbolic planners rely on manually constructed problem specifications, limiting scalability and adaptability, while recent large language model (LLM)-based approaches often suffer from hallucinations and weak grounding-i.e., poor alignment between generated plans and actual environmental objects and constraints-in object-rich settings. We present Scale-Plan, a scalable LLM-assisted framework that generates compact, task-relevant problem representations from natural language instructions. Given a PDDL domain specification, Scale-Plan constructs an action graph capturing domain structure and uses shallow LLM reasoning to guide a structured graph search that identifies a minimal subset of relevant actions and objects. By filtering irrelevant information prior to planning, Scale-Plan enables efficient decomposition, allocation, and long-horizon plan generation. We evaluate our approach on complex multi-agent tasks and introduce MAT2-THOR, a cleaned benchmark built on AI2-THOR for reliable evaluation of multi-robot planning systems. Scale-Plan outperforms pure LLM and hybrid LLM-PDDL baselines across all metrics, improving scalability and reliability.

</details>


### [6] [HMR-1: Hierarchical Massage Robot with Vision-Language-Model for Embodied Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08817)
*Rongtao Xu,Mingming Yu,Xiaofeng Han,Yu Zhang,Kaiyi Hu,Zhe Feng,Zenghuang Fu,Changwei Wang,Weiliang Meng,Xiaopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文构建了MedMassage-12K多模态数据集并提出分层式具身按摩框架，以解决医疗具身智能在物理治疗中缺乏标准化评估基准和开源数据集的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能在医疗健康领域（特别是物理治疗和康复）具有变革性潜力，但面临两大关键挑战：缺乏标准化评估基准和开源多模态穴位按摩数据集。

Method: 1. 构建MedMassage-12K数据集（12,190张图像，174,177个QA对）；2. 提出分层式具身按摩框架，包括高层穴位定位模块（使用MLLM理解语言并识别穴位位置）和底层控制模块（提供规划轨迹）；3. 基于此框架评估现有MLLM并建立基准；4. 微调Qwen-VL模型验证框架有效性。

Result: 1. 创建了覆盖多样光照条件和背景的大规模多模态数据集；2. 建立了具身按摩任务的评估基准；3. 通过微调Qwen-VL模型证明了框架的有效性；4. 物理实验进一步验证了框架的实际适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过构建数据集和分层框架，解决了医疗具身智能在物理治疗领域的标准化评估和数据稀缺问题，为具身按摩系统的发展提供了重要基础，并通过实验验证了其实际应用价值。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Embodied Intelligence has opened transformative opportunities in healthcare, particularly in physical therapy and rehabilitation. However, critical challenges remain in developing robust embodied healthcare solutions, such as the lack of standardized evaluation benchmarks and the scarcity of open-source multimodal acupoint massage datasets. To address these gaps, we construct MedMassage-12K - a multimodal dataset containing 12,190 images with 174,177 QA pairs, covering diverse lighting conditions and backgrounds. Furthermore, we propose a hierarchical embodied massage framework, which includes a high-level acupoint grounding module and a low-level control module. The high-level acupoint grounding module uses multimodal large language models to understand human language and identify acupoint locations, while the low-level control module provides the planned trajectory. Based on this, we evaluate existing MLLMs and establish a benchmark for embodied massage tasks. Additionally, we fine-tune the Qwen-VL model, demonstrating the framework's effectiveness. Physical experiments further confirm the practical applicability of the framework.Our dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/Xiaofeng-Han-Res/HMR-1.

</details>


### [7] [Formation-Aware Adaptive Conformalized Perception for Safe Leader-Follower Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08958)
*Richie R. Suganda,Bin Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分布式、感知安全的领导者-跟随者编队控制方法，通过自适应保形预测量化感知不确定性，并集成到CBF-QP控制器中，在保证可见性约束的同时提高编队成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式视觉编队中，每个机器人使用机载感知估计相对状态、跟踪期望设定点并保持领导者在相机视野内。由于异方差感知误差以及编队机动与可见性约束之间的耦合，安全控制具有挑战性。

Method: 提出基于Risk-Aware Mondrian CP的分布式、编队感知自适应保形预测方法，生成编队条件不确定性分位数。将这些边界集成到具有平滑裕度的编队感知保形CBF-QP中，以强制执行可见性约束，同时保持可行性和跟踪性能。

Result: Gazebo仿真显示，相比忽略编队依赖可见性风险的非自适应（全局）CP基线，该方法提高了编队成功率和跟踪精度，同时保持了有限样本概率安全保证。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了分布式视觉编队中的感知安全问题，通过自适应不确定性量化在保证安全的同时提升了控制性能。

Abstract: This paper considers the perception safety problem in distributed vision-based leader-follower formations, where each robot uses onboard perception to estimate relative states, track desired setpoints, and keep the leader within its camera field of view (FOV). Safety is challenging due to heteroscedastic perception errors and the coupling between formation maneuvers and visibility constraints. We propose a distributed, formation-aware adaptive conformal prediction method based on Risk-Aware Mondrian CP to produce formation-conditioned uncertainty quantiles. The resulting bounds tighten in high-risk configurations (near FOV limits) and relax in safer regions. We integrate these bounds into a Formation-Aware Conformal CBF-QP with a smooth margin to enforce visibility while maintaining feasibility and tracking performance. Gazebo simulations show improved formation success rates and tracking accuracy over non-adaptive (global) CP baselines that ignore formation-dependent visibility risk, while preserving finite-sample probabilistic safety guarantees. The experimental videos are available on the \href{https://nail-uh.github.io/iros2026.github.io/}{project website}\footnote{Project Website: https://nail-uh.github.io/iros2026.github.io/}.

</details>


### [8] [Latent World Models for Automated Driving: A Unified Taxonomy, Evaluation Framework, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09086)
*Rongxiang Zeng,Yongqi Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一潜在空间框架，整合自动驾驶世界模型进展，通过潜在表示形式和结构先验组织设计空间，阐述五个跨领域内部机制，提出具体评估方案和研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型和视觉-语言-动作系统正在重塑自动驾驶，但缺乏统一的框架来整合不同方法。潜在表示作为核心计算基板，需要系统化的设计原则和评估标准来推动领域发展。

Method: 提出统一潜在空间框架，按潜在表示目标（潜在世界、潜在动作、潜在生成器）和形式（连续状态、离散标记、混合）组织设计空间，引入几何、拓扑和语义的结构先验，阐述五个内部机制。

Result: 建立了自动驾驶世界模型的分类框架，明确了设计选择与鲁棒性、泛化性和可部署性的联系，提出了包含闭环指标套件和资源感知思考成本的评估方案，识别了具体研究方向。

Conclusion: 统一潜在空间框架为自动驾驶世界模型提供了系统化的设计原则和评估标准，有助于推动决策就绪、可验证和资源高效的自动驾驶系统发展，需要关注内部机制与性能指标的对应关系。

Abstract: Emerging generative world models and vision-language-action (VLA) systems are rapidly reshaping automated driving by enabling scalable simulation, long-horizon forecasting, and capability-rich decision making. Across these directions, latent representations serve as the central computational substrate: they compress high-dimensional multi-sensor observations, enable temporally coherent rollouts, and provide interfaces for planning, reasoning, and controllable generation. This paper proposes a unifying latent-space framework that synthesizes recent progress in world models for automated driving. The framework organizes the design space by the target and form of latent representations (latent worlds, latent actions, latent generators; continuous states, discrete tokens, and hybrids) and by structural priors for geometry, topology, and semantics. Building on this taxonomy, the paper articulates five cross-cutting internal mechanics (i.e, structural isomorphism, long-horizon temporal stability, semantic and reasoning alignment, value-aligned objectives and post-training, as well as adaptive computation and deliberation) and connects these design choices to robustness, generalization, and deployability. The work also proposes concrete evaluation prescriptions, including a closed-loop metric suite and a resource-aware deliberation cost, designed to reduce the open-loop / closed-loop mismatch. Finally, the paper identifies actionable research directions toward advancing latent world model for decision-ready, verifiable, and resource-efficient automated driving.

</details>


### [9] [Impact of Different Failures on a Robot's Perceived Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08821)
*Andrew Violette,Zhanxin Wu,Haruki Nishimura,Masha Itkina,Leticia Priebe Rocha,Mark Zolotas,Guy Hoffman,Hadas Kress-Gazit*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究机器人不同类型故障（失误、冻结、错误）对感知可靠性的影响，以及如何通过成功执行恢复可靠性，无需显式社交修复


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人故障可能导致其感知可靠性下降，进而影响可信度。需要了解不同类型故障对感知可靠性的差异化影响，以及如何通过成功执行自然恢复可靠性，为机器人故障修复策略提供指导

Method: 采用预注册的在线视频研究，参与者观看机器人执行拾取放置任务的视频，包括五种故障类型（失误、冻结、三种错误）、故障后成功执行、纯成功执行三种情况。通过参与者的投注模式（选择机器人或抛硬币）和问卷调查分析感知可靠性变化

Result: 错误类故障对感知可靠性的损害小于失误或冻结故障，某些错误甚至被感知为成功；故障后立即成功执行与无故障的成功执行对感知可靠性的影响相同；成功执行能够有效恢复故障后的感知可靠性

Conclusion: 失误和冻结类故障比错误类故障更需要修复；机器人成功执行能够自然恢复感知可靠性，无需显式社交修复行为；研究结果为机器人故障修复策略提供了重要指导

Abstract: Robots fail, potentially leading to a loss in the robot's perceived reliability (PR), a measure correlated with trustworthiness. In this study we examine how various kinds of failures affect the PR of the robot differently, and how this measure recovers without explicit social repair actions by the robot. In a preregistered and controlled online video study, participants were asked to predict a robot's success in a pick-and-place task. We examined manipulation failures (slips), freezing (lapses), and three types of incorrect picked objects or place goals (mistakes). Participants were shown one of 11 videos -- one of five types of failure, one of five types of failure followed by a successful execution in the same video, or a successful execution video. This was followed by two additional successful execution videos. Participants bet money either on the robot or on a coin toss after each video. People's betting patterns along with a qualitative analysis of their survey responses highlight that mistakes are less damaging to PR than slips or lapses, and some mistakes are even perceived as successes. We also see that successes immediately following a failure have the same effect on PR as successes without a preceding failure. Finally, we show that successful executions recover PR after a failure. Our findings highlight which robot failures are in higher need of repair in a human-robot interaction, and how trust could be recovered by robot successes.

</details>


### [10] [Lightweight 3D LiDAR-Based UAV Tracking: An Adaptive Extended Kalman Filtering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09783)
*Nivand Khosravi,Meysam Basiri,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 轻量级LiDAR无人机跟踪系统，采用自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波器处理稀疏点云数据，在GPS拒止环境下实现可靠相对定位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机群需要精确的相对定位以实现协调飞行和避撞。现有视觉跟踪方法受光照条件影响，而传统LiDAR系统通常笨重且功耗高，不适用于小型无人机。需要一种轻量级、鲁棒的LiDAR跟踪方案来解决稀疏、噪声和非均匀点云数据的挑战。

Method: 提出基于自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波器(AEKF)的轻量级LiDAR无人机跟踪系统。该方法动态调整噪声协方差矩阵（使用创新和残差统计），增强真实条件下的跟踪精度。包含恢复机制处理临时检测失败（由散射LiDAR返回或遮挡引起）。使用Livox Mid-360 LiDAR在DJI F550无人机上进行实验验证。

Result: 在真实飞行场景中，该方法在稀疏LiDAR返回和间歇检测条件下表现出鲁棒的无人机跟踪性能。在激进机动过程中，始终优于标准卡尔曼滤波和粒子滤波方法。实现了GPS拒止环境下可靠的相对定位，无需多传感器阵列或外部基础设施。

Conclusion: 该轻量级LiDAR跟踪系统结合自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波器，有效解决了非重复扫描3D LiDAR产生的稀疏、噪声和非均匀点云数据的挑战。系统适合具有严格载荷限制的小型无人机，在GPS拒止环境中实现了可靠的相对定位，为无人机群协调飞行提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate relative positioning is crucial for swarm aerial robotics, enabling coordinated flight and collision avoidance. Although vision-based tracking has been extensively studied, 3D LiDAR-based methods remain underutilized despite their robustness under varying lighting conditions. Existing systems often rely on bulky, power-intensive sensors, making them impractical for small UAVs with strict payload and energy constraints. This paper presents a lightweight LiDAR-based UAV tracking system incorporating an Adaptive Extended Kalman Filter (AEKF) framework. Our approach effectively addresses the challenges posed by sparse, noisy, and nonuniform point cloud data generated by non-repetitive scanning 3D LiDARs, ensuring reliable tracking while remaining suitable for small drones with strict payload constraints. Unlike conventional filtering techniques, the proposed method dynamically adjusts the noise covariance matrices using innovation and residual statistics, thereby enhancing tracking accuracy under real-world conditions. Additionally, a recovery mechanism ensures continuity of tracking during temporary detection failures caused by scattered LiDAR returns or occlusions. Experimental validation was performed using a Livox Mid-360 LiDAR mounted on a DJI F550 UAV in real-world flight scenarios. The proposed method demonstrated robust UAV tracking performance under sparse LiDAR returns and intermittent detections, consistently outperforming both standard Kalman filtering and particle filtering approaches during aggressive maneuvers. These results confirm that the framework enables reliable relative positioning in GPS-denied environments without the need for multi-sensor arrays or external infrastructure.

</details>


### [11] [APPLV: Adaptive Planner Parameter Learning from Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08862)
*Yuanjie Lu,Beichen Wang,Zhengqi Wu,Yang Li,Xiaomin Lin,Chengzhi Mao,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: APPLE-V提出了一种从视觉-语言-动作模型学习自适应规划器参数的方法，通过预测经典规划器的参数配置，在约束环境中实现更好的导航性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高度约束环境中，传统导航方法需要环境特定的参数调优，端到端学习方法难以实现精确控制，而现有的VLA模型在导航任务中面临精确控制和推理延迟的挑战。

Method: APPLE-V利用预训练的视觉语言模型，通过回归头预测经典规划器的参数配置，开发了两种训练策略：基于收集的导航轨迹的监督学习微调和进一步优化导航性能的强化学习微调。

Result: 在模拟的BARN数据集和物理机器人实验中，APPLE-V在导航性能和泛化到未见环境方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: APPLE-V通过将VLA模型与经典规划器参数预测相结合，在保持安全性的同时实现了更好的导航性能和泛化能力，为约束环境中的自主导航提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in highly constrained environments remains challenging for mobile robots. Classical navigation approaches offer safety assurances but require environment-specific parameter tuning; end-to-end learning bypasses parameter tuning but struggles with precise control in constrained spaces. To this end, recent robot learning approaches automate parameter tuning while retaining classical systems' safety, yet still face challenges in generalizing to unseen environments. Recently, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promise by leveraging foundation models' scene understanding capabilities, but still struggle with precise control and inference latency in navigation tasks. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Planner Parameter Learning from Vision-Language-Action Model (\textsc{applv}). Unlike traditional VLA models that directly output actions, \textsc{applv} leverages pre-trained vision-language models with a regression head to predict planner parameters that configure classical planners. We develop two training strategies: supervised learning fine-tuning from collected navigation trajectories and reinforcement learning fine-tuning to further optimize navigation performance. We evaluate \textsc{applv} across multiple motion planners on the simulated Benchmark Autonomous Robot Navigation (BARN) dataset and in physical robot experiments. Results demonstrate that \textsc{applv} outperforms existing methods in both navigation performance and generalization to unseen environments.

</details>


### [12] [NanoBench: A Multi-Task Benchmark Dataset for Nano-Quadrotor System Identification, Control, and State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09908)
*Syed Izzat Ullah,Jose Baca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NanoBench是首个针对27克纳米四旋翼飞行器（Crazyflie 2.1）的开源多任务基准数据集，提供执行器级信号、控制器内部状态和毫米级精度的地面真值数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中机器人基准主要针对百克至千克级飞行器，仅提供高层状态数据，忽略了纳米级四旋翼所需的执行器级信号。纳米四旋翼的低雷诺数空气动力学、无芯直流电机非线性和严重计算约束使得针对大型飞行器开发的模型和控制器失效，需要专门的数据集进行研究。

Method: 在Vicon运动捕捉环境中使用Crazyflie 2.1纳米四旋翼（起飞重量27克）收集超过170条飞行轨迹，包括悬停、多频激励、标准跟踪和激进机动等多种速度状态。数据集以100Hz频率提供同步的Vicon地面真值、原始IMU数据、机载扩展卡尔曼滤波器估计、PID控制器内部状态和电机PWM指令，以及10Hz的电池遥测数据，对齐一致性优于0.5毫秒。

Result: 创建了首个公开数据集，联合提供执行器指令、控制器内部状态和估计器输出，并在商用纳米级空中平台上实现毫米级精度地面真值。定义了标准化评估协议、训练/测试分割和开源基线，支持三个任务：非线性系统辨识、闭环控制器基准测试和机载状态估计评估。

Conclusion: NanoBench填补了纳米四旋翼研究领域的数据空白，为研究低雷诺数空气动力学、电机非线性和计算约束下的控制问题提供了首个全面的基准数据集，将促进纳米级空中机器人系统辨识、控制和状态估计算法的发展。

Abstract: Existing aerial-robotics benchmarks target vehicles from hundreds of grams to several kilograms and typically expose only high-level state data. They omit the actuator-level signals required to study nano-scale quadrotors, where low-Reynolds number aerodynamics, coreless DC motor nonlinearities, and severe computational constraints invalidate models and controllers developed for larger vehicles. We introduce NanoBench, an open-source multi-task benchmark collected on the commercially available Crazyflie 2.1 nano-quadrotor (takeoff weight 27 g) in a Vicon motion capture arena. The dataset contains over 170 flight trajectories spanning hover, multi-frequency excitation, standard tracking, and aggressive maneuvers across multiple speed regimes. Each trajectory provides synchronized Vicon ground truth, raw IMU data, onboard extended Kalman filter estimates, PID controller internals, and motor PWM commands at 100 Hz, alongside battery telemetry at 10 Hz, aligned with sub-0.5 ms consistency. NanoBench defines standardized evaluation protocols, train/test splits, and open-source baselines for three tasks: nonlinear system identification, closed-loop controller benchmarking, and onboard state estimation assessment. To our knowledge, it is the first public dataset to jointly provide actuator commands, controller internals, and estimator outputs with millimeter-accurate ground truth on a commercially available nano-scale aerial platform.

</details>


### [13] [Adaptive SINDy: Residual Force System Identification Based UAV Disturbance Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08863)
*Fawad Mehboob,Amir Atef Habel,Roohan Ahmed Khan,Mikhail Derevianchenko,Clement Fortin,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将SINDy数据驱动系统辨识与RLS自适应控制相结合的方法，用于无人机在湍流环境中的扰动抑制和轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在湍流环境中的稳定控制是一个重要问题。传统方法如扰动观测器和经典自适应控制对复杂环境适应性有限，而基于学习的方法虽然被越来越多地用于建模残余力和扰动抑制，但其泛化性和可解释性存在问题。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的集成方法：使用稀疏辨识非线性动力学（SINDy）进行数据驱动的系统辨识，并结合递归最小二乘（RLS）自适应控制来适应和抑制湍流环境中的风扰动。

Result: 在Gazebo谐波环境和真实飞行中进行了测试验证，风速高达2 m/s，来自四个方向，创建了高度动态的湍流环境。自适应SINDy在多个轨迹跟踪误差指标上优于基线PID和INDI控制器，且未发生坠机。在圆形和双纽线轨迹上分别实现了高达12.2 cm和17.6 cm的RMSE，以及13.7 cm和10.5 cm的MAE。

Conclusion: 该方法在高度动态环境下对复杂轨迹跟踪进行了验证，使用非常轻量级的Crazyflie无人机，证明了SINDy与RLS自适应控制集成的有效性，能够显著提高无人机在湍流环境中的轨迹跟踪性能。

Abstract: The stability and control of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) in a turbulent environment is a matter of great concern. Devising a robust control algorithm to reject disturbances is challenging due to the highly nonlinear nature of wind dynamics, and modeling the dynamics using analytical techniques is not straightforward. While traditional techniques using disturbance observers and classical adaptive control have shown some progress, they are mostly limited to relatively non-complex environments. On the other hand, learning based approaches are increasingly being used for modeling of residual forces and disturbance rejection; however, their generalization and interpretability is a factor of concern. To this end, we propose a novel integration of data-driven system identification using Sparse Identification of Non-Linear Dynamics (SINDy) with a Recursive Least Square (RLS) adaptive control to adapt and reject wind disturbances in a turbulent environment. We tested and validated our approach on Gazebo harmonic environment and on real flights with wind speeds of up to 2 m/s from four directions, creating a highly dynamic and turbulent environment. Adaptive SINDy outperformed the baseline PID and INDI controllers on several trajectory tracking error metrics without crashing. A root mean square error (RMSE) of up to 12.2 cm and 17.6 cm, and a mean absolute error (MAE) of 13.7 cm and 10.5 cm were achieved on circular and lemniscate trajectories, respectively. The validation was performed on a very lightweight Crazyflie drone under a highly dynamic environment for complex trajectory tracking.

</details>


### [14] [Proprioceptive Safe Active Navigation and Exploration for Planetary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08905)
*Matthew Y. Jiang,Feifei Qian,Shipeng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PSANE框架利用腿式机器人的本体感知信息进行未知可变形地形中的安全导航与探索，通过高斯过程回归学习可通行性模型，实现安全区域认证和探索边界识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形颗粒地形在行星探测中带来显著的运动和固定风险，且难以通过遥感（如视觉）检测。腿式机器人可通过腿-地形交互感知地形特性，但如何系统利用这种交互信息进行导航规划仍待探索。

Method: 提出PSANE框架：1) 通过高斯过程回归学习可通行性模型，在线估计和认证安全区域；2) 识别探索边界；3) 将估计结果与反应式控制器集成实现实时导航；4) 边界选择采用多目标优化，平衡安全集扩展概率和目标导向成本；5) 通过标量化在帕累托最优边界集中选择子目标。

Result: PSANE能够仅使用本体感知估计的可通行性安全探索未知颗粒地形并到达指定目标，相比基线方法实现了性能提升。

Conclusion: PSANE框架成功解决了利用腿-地形交互信息进行可变形环境中导航规划的问题，为腿式机器人在未知可变形地形中的安全自主导航提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Deformable granular terrains introduce significant locomotion and immobilization risks in planetary exploration and are difficult to detect via remote sensing (e.g., vision). Legged robots can sense terrain properties through leg-terrain interactions during locomotion, offering a direct means to assess traversability in deformable environments. How to systematically exploit this interaction-derived information for navigation planning, however, remains underexplored. We address this gap by presenting PSANE, a Proprioceptive Safe Active Navigation and Exploration framework that leverages leg-terrain interaction measurements for safe navigation and exploration in unknown deformable environments. PSANE learns a traversability model via Gaussian Process regression to estimate and certify safe regions and identify exploration frontiers online, and integrates these estimates with a reactive controller for real-time navigation. Frontier selection is formulated as a multi-objective optimization that balances safe-set expansion probability and goal-directed cost, with subgoals selected via scalarization over the Pareto-optimal frontier set. PSANE safely explores unknown granular terrain and reaches specified goals using only proprioceptively estimated traversability, while achieving performance improvements over baseline methods.

</details>


### [15] [FAME: Force-Adaptive RL for Expanding the Manipulation Envelope of a Full-Scale Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08961)
*Niraj Pudasaini,Yutong Zhang,Jensen Lavering,Alessandro Roncone,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAME是一个基于强化学习的力自适应框架，用于增强人形机器人在双手机械臂操作扰动下的站立平衡能力，通过隐式编码上体关节配置和双手交互力来实现在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人双手机械臂操作时，外部手部力会通过运动链传播并约束可行的操作包络，需要一种能够适应操作引发扰动的平衡控制方法。

Method: 提出FAME框架：1) 训练阶段在仿真中对每只手施加多样化的球形采样3D力扰动，配合上体姿态课程学习；2) 学习一个隐式上下文编码器，编码上体关节配置和双手交互力；3) 部署时从机器人动力学估计交互力并输入编码器，实现无腕部力/力矩传感器的在线适应。

Result: 仿真实验：在五个固定臂配置下，FAME将平均站立成功率提升至73.84%，而仅课程学习基线为51.40%，基础策略为29.44%。实物部署：在Unitree H12人形机器人上验证了在非对称单臂负载和对称双臂负载场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FAME通过力自适应强化学习框架有效提升了人形机器人在双手机械臂操作扰动下的平衡鲁棒性，无需额外力传感器即可实现在线适应，为动态操作环境中的平衡控制提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Maintaining balance under external hand forces is critical for humanoid bimanual manipulation, where interaction forces propagate through the kinematic chain and constrain the feasible manipulation envelope. We propose \textbf{FAME}, a force-adaptive reinforcement learning framework that conditions a standing policy on a learned latent context encoding upper-body joint configuration and bimanual interaction forces. During training, we apply diverse, spherically sampled 3D forces on each hand to inject disturbances in simulation together with an upper-body pose curriculum, exposing the policy to manipulation-induced perturbations across continuously varying arm configurations. At deployment, interaction forces are estimated from the robot dynamics and fed to the same encoder, enabling online adaptation without wrist force/torque sensors. In simulation across five fixed arm configurations with randomized hand forces and commanded base heights, FAME improves mean standing success to 73.84%, compared to 51.40% for the curriculum-only baseline and 29.44% for the base policy. We further deploy the learned policy on a full-scale Unitree H12 humanoid and evaluate robustness in representative load-interaction scenarios, including asymmetric single-arm load and symmetric bimanual load. Code and videos are available on https://fame10.github.io/Fame/

</details>


### [16] [SurgCalib: Gaussian Splatting-Based Hand-Eye Calibration for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08983)
*Zijian Wu,Shuojue Yang,Yu Chung Lee,Eitan Prisman,Yueming Jin,Septimiu E. Salcudean*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于高斯泼溅的达芬奇手术机器人手眼标定框架SurgCalib，无需标记物，通过两阶段优化在RCM约束下实现自动标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人手眼标定面临独特挑战：编码器因线缆拉伸和回差导致测量不准确，传统基于标记物的方法违反无菌协议且干扰手术流程，需要无标记的自动标定方案。

Method: SurgCalib框架：首先使用原始运动学测量初始化手术器械位姿，然后在高斯泼溅可微分渲染管道中，在RCM约束下进行两阶段优化程序细化位姿。

Result: 在公开dVRK基准SurgPose上评估：左器械2D尖端重投影误差12.24px(2.06mm)，右器械11.33px(1.9mm)；左器械3D尖端欧氏距离误差5.98mm，右器械4.75mm。

Conclusion: SurgCalib实现了无需标记物的自动手眼标定，在手术室环境中具有应用潜力，为电缆驱动手术机器人提供了有效的标定解决方案。

Abstract: We present a Gaussian Splatting-based framework for hand-eye calibration of the da Vinci surgical robot. In a vision-guided robotic system, accurate estimation of the rigid transformation between the robot base and the camera frame is essential for reliable closed-loop control. For cable-driven surgical robots, this task faces unique challenges. The encoders of surgical instruments often produce inaccurate proprioceptive measurements due to cable stretch and backlash. Conventional hand-eye calibration approaches typically rely on known fiducial patterns and solve the AX = XB formulation. While effective, introducing additional markers into the operating room (OR) environment can violate sterility protocols and disrupt surgical workflows. In this study, we propose SurgCalib, an automatic, markerless framework that has the potential to be used in the OR. SurgCalib first initializes the pose of the surgical instrument using raw kinematic measurements and subsequently refines this pose through a two-phase optimization procedure under the RCM constraint within a Gaussian Splatting-based differentiable rendering pipeline. We evaluate the proposed method on the public dVRK benchmark, SurgPose. The results demonstrate average 2D tool-tip reprojection errors of 12.24 px (2.06 mm) and 11.33 px (1.9 mm), and 3D tool-tip Euclidean distance errors of 5.98 mm and 4.75 mm, for the left and right instruments, respectively.

</details>


### [17] [Characterization, Analytical Planning, and Hybrid Force Control for the Inspire RH56DFX Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08988)
*Xuan Tan,William Xie,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对Inspire RH56DFX灵巧手提出三项改进：硬件表征、MuJoCo仿真模型验证、混合力速抓取控制器，将其从黑盒设备转变为研究工具


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业灵巧手如Inspire RH56DFX存在未校准本体感知信息、高速接触行为不可靠、欠驱动耦合连杆导致对握抓取困难等问题，难以作为科学仪器使用

Method: 1) 硬件表征（力校准、延迟和超调）；2) sim2real验证的MuJoCo模型用于宽度到抓取规划；3) 混合闭环速度-力抓取控制器

Result: 在孔插入任务中达到65%成功率（优于腕力基线10%）；在15个物理多样性物体上300次抓取达到87%成功率，优于无规划抓取和学习抓取

Conclusion: 该方法模块化设计，兼容外部物体检测器和视觉语言模型，为Inspire RH56DFX手提供了可解释且可立即部署的灵巧操作接口

Abstract: Commercially accessible dexterous robot hands are increasingly prevalent, but many remain difficult to use as scientific instruments. For example, the Inspire RH56DFX hand exposes only uncalibrated proprioceptive information and shows unreliable contact behavior at high speed (up to 1618% force limit overshoot). Furthermore, its underactuated, coupled finger linkages make antipodal grasps non-trivial. We contribute three improvements to the Inspire RH56DFX to transform it from a black-box device to a research tool: (1) hardware characterization (force calibration, latency, and overshoot), (2) a sim2real validated MuJoCo model for analytical width-to-grasp planning, and (3) a hybrid, closed-loop speed-force grasp controller. We validate these components on peg-in-hole insertion, achieving 65% success and outperforming a wrist-force-only baseline of 10% and on 300 grasps across 15 physically diverse objects, achieving 87% success and outperforming plan-free grasps and learned grasps. Our approach is modular, designed for compatibility with external object detectors and vision-language models for width & force estimation and high-level planning, and provides an interpretable and immediately deployable interface for dexterous manipulation with the Inspire RH56DFX hand, open-sourced at this website https://correlllab.github.io/rh56dfx.html.

</details>


### [18] [Improving through Interaction: Searching Behavioral Representation Spaces with CMA-ES-IG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09011)
*Nathaniel Dennler,Zhonghao Shi,Yiran Tao,Andreea Bobu,Stefanos Nikolaidis,Maja Matarić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CMA-ES-IG算法，在机器人偏好学习中同时考虑学习效果和用户体验，通过生成感知上可区分且信息丰富的轨迹供用户排序


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人偏好学习方法主要关注学习效果（如样本效率、估计精度），但忽视了用户在提供排序时的体验期望，这会影响用户对机器人系统的接受度。需要开发既优化学习效果又考虑用户体验的偏好学习方法。

Method: 提出CMA-ES-IG算法，将协方差矩阵自适应进化策略（CMA-ES）与信息增益（IG）结合，生成感知上可区分且信息丰富的轨迹供用户排序，在偏好学习过程中显式纳入用户体验考量。

Result: 相比现有方法，CMA-ES-IG：(1) 能更有效地扩展到高维偏好空间；(2) 对高维问题保持计算可行性；(3) 对噪声或不一致的用户反馈具有鲁棒性；(4) 非专业用户更倾向于使用它来识别偏好的机器人行为。

Conclusion: CMA-ES-IG算法成功地将用户体验考量纳入机器人偏好学习过程，通过模拟研究和真实机器人实验验证了其优势，为开发更人性化的机器人交互系统提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Robots that interact with humans must adapt to individual users' preferences to operate effectively in human-centered environments. An intuitive and effective technique to learn non-expert users' preferences is through rankings of robot behaviors, e.g., trajectories, gestures, or voices. Existing techniques primarily focus on generating queries that optimize preference learning outcomes, such as sample efficiency or final preference estimation accuracy. However, the focus on outcome overlooks key user expectations in the process of providing these rankings, which can negatively impact users' adoption of robotic systems. This work proposes the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategies with Information Gain (CMA-ES-IG) algorithm. CMA-ES-IG explicitly incorporates user experience considerations into the preference learning process by suggesting perceptually distinct and informative trajectories for users to rank. We demonstrate these benefits through both simulated studies and real-robot experiments. CMA-ES-IG, compared to state-of-the-art alternatives, (1) scales more effectively to higher-dimensional preference spaces, (2) maintains computational tractability for high-dimensional problems, (3) is robust to noisy or inconsistent user feedback, and (4) is preferred by non-expert users in identifying their preferred robot behaviors. This project's code is available at github.com/interaction-lab/CMA-ES-IG

</details>


### [19] [PlayWorld: Learning Robot World Models from Autonomous Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09030)
*Tenny Yin,Zhiting Mei,Zhonghe Zheng,Miyu Yamane,David Wang,Jade Sceats,Samuel M. Bateman,Lihan Zha,Apurva Badithela,Ola Shorinwa,Anirudha Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PlayWorld：一种从无监督机器人自玩中学习高质量视频世界模拟器的自主流水线，能够生成物理一致的交互预测并显著提升策略学习效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于动作的视频模型在预测物理一致的机器人-物体交互方面存在困难，这些交互对机器人操作至关重要。现有方法依赖人类演示数据存在偏差且难以捕捉复杂的长尾物理交互。

Method: 提出PlayWorld系统，这是一个简单、可扩展、完全自主的流水线，能够从无监督机器人自玩中学习，无需依赖成功偏差的人类演示数据，从而自然实现可扩展的数据收集并捕捉复杂的物理交互。

Result: PlayWorld能够生成高质量、物理一致的接触丰富交互预测，在多种操作任务中表现优于基于人类收集数据训练的世界模型。在细粒度失败预测和策略评估方面提升达40%，在真实世界部署中通过世界模型强化学习使策略成功率提升65%。

Conclusion: PlayWorld通过无监督机器人自玩学习，能够构建高质量的视频世界模拟器，显著提升物理交互预测的保真度，并为策略学习和评估提供强大支持，展示了自主数据收集在构建机器人模拟器中的优势。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video models offer a promising path to building general-purpose robot simulators that can improve directly from data. Yet, despite training on large-scale robot datasets, current state-of-the-art video models still struggle to predict physically consistent robot-object interactions that are crucial in robotic manipulation. To close this gap, we present PlayWorld, a simple, scalable, and fully autonomous pipeline for training high-fidelity video world simulators from interaction experience. In contrast to prior approaches that rely on success-biased human demonstrations, PlayWorld is the first system capable of learning entirely from unsupervised robot self-play, enabling naturally scalable data collection while capturing complex, long-tailed physical interactions essential for modeling realistic object dynamics. Experiments across diverse manipulation tasks show that PlayWorld generates high-quality, physically consistent predictions for contact-rich interactions that are not captured by world models trained on human-collected data.We further demonstrate the versatility of PlayWorld in enabling fine-grained failure prediction and policy evaluation, with up to 40% improvements over human-collected data. Finally, we demonstrate how PlayWorld enables reinforcement learning in the world model, improving policy performance by 65% in success rates when deployed in the real world.

</details>


### [20] [ImpedanceDiffusion: Diffusion-Based Global Path Planning for UAV Swarm Navigation with Generative Impedance Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09031)
*Faryal Batool,Yasheerah Yaqoot,Muhammad Ahsan Mustafa,Roohan Ahmed Khan,Aleksey Fedoseev,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ImpedanceDiffusion：用于无人机集群在杂乱室内环境导航的分层框架，结合扩散模型全局路径规划、APF跟踪和语义感知可变阻抗控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人机集群在杂乱室内环境中导航的挑战，需要长时程规划、反应式避障和自适应柔顺性控制，传统方法难以同时满足这些需求

Method: 分层框架：1) 基于图像条件扩散模型的全局路径规划器（顶视图长时程和FPV短时程两种变体）；2) APF反应式跟踪层；3) VLM-RAG语义障碍物分类模块（90%检索准确率）用于自适应阻抗参数调整

Result: 100%轨迹生成率，92%总体成功率，无飞行碰撞；顶视图规划器产生更平滑轨迹（硬障碍附近1.0-1.2 m/s，软障碍附近0.6-1.0 m/s）；FPV规划器产生更大局部间隙轨迹（硬障碍附近1.4-2.0 m/s，软障碍附近最高1.6 m/s）

Conclusion: ImpedanceDiffusion框架通过结合扩散规划、APF跟踪和语义感知阻抗控制，实现了无人机集群在杂乱室内环境中的可靠自适应导航，展示了零样本仿真到真实部署的有效性

Abstract: Safe swarm navigation in cluttered indoor environment requires long-horizon planning, reactive obstacle avoidance, and adaptive compliance. We propose ImpedanceDiffusion, a hierarchical framework that leverages image-conditioned diffusion-based global path planning with Artificial Potential Field (APF) tracking and semantic-aware variable impedance control for aerial drone swarms.
  The diffusion model generates geometric global trajectories directly from RGB images without explicit map construction. These trajectories are tracked by an APF-based reactive layer, while a VLM-RAG module performs semantic obstacle classification with 90% retrieval accuracy to adapt impedance parameters for mixed obstacle environments during execution.
  Two diffusion planners are evaluated: (i) a top-view long-horizon planner using single-pass inference and (ii) a first-person-view (FPV) short-horizon planner deployed via a two-stage inference pipeline. Both planners achieve a 100% trajectory generation rate across twenty static and dynamic experimental configurations and are validated via zero-shot sim-to-real deployment on Crazyflie 2.1 drones through the hierarchical APF-impedance control stack. The top-view planner produces smoother trajectories that yield conservative tracking speeds of 1.0-1.2 m/s near hard obstacles and 0.6-1.0 m/s near soft obstacles. In contrast, the FPV planner generates trajectories with greater local clearance and typically higher speeds, reaching 1.4-2.0 m/s near hard obstacles and up to 1.6 m/s near soft obstacles. Across 20 experimental configurations (100 total runs), the framework achieved a 92% success rate while maintaining stable impedance-based formation control with bounded oscillations and no in-flight collisions, demonstrating reliable and adaptive swarm navigation in cluttered indoor environments.

</details>


### [21] [Cutting the Cord: System Architecture for Low-Cost, GPU-Accelerated Bimanual Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09051)
*Artemis Shaw,Chen Liu,Justin Costa,Rane Gray,Alina Skowronek,Kevin Diaz,Nam Bui,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开源XLeRobot基础上构建的双臂移动机械臂，集成板载计算，成本低于1300美元，具备远程操作、自主SLAM导航和视觉操控能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为机器人和机器人学习的研究与教育提供一个低成本、无外部依赖的移动操作平台，解决现有系统成本高、依赖外部基础设施的问题

Method: 基于开源XLeRobot构建，采用优化机械设计最大化刚度重量比，Tri-Bus电源拓扑隔离计算与电机电压瞬变，使用NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano实现嵌入式自主性

Result: 开发出成本低于1300美元的双臂移动机械臂平台，具备远程操作、自主SLAM导航和基于视觉的操控能力，无需外部依赖即可运行

Conclusion: 该平台为机器人和机器人学习的研究与教育提供了一个经济实惠、功能完整的移动操作解决方案，填补了低成本自主机器人平台的空白

Abstract: We present a bimanual mobile manipulator built on the open-source XLeRobot with integrated onboard compute for less than \$1300. Key contributions include: (1) optimized mechanical design maximizing stiffness-to-weight ratio, (2) a Tri-Bus power topology isolating compute from motor-induced voltage transients, and (3) embedded autonomy using NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano for untethered operation. The platform enables teleoperation, autonomous SLAM navigation, and vision-based manipulation without external dependencies, providing a low-cost alternative for research and education in robotics and robot learning.

</details>


### [22] [Quality over Quantity: Demonstration Curation via Influence Functions for Data-Centric Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09056)
*Haeone Lee,Taywon Min,Junsu Kim,Sinjae Kang,Fangchen Liu,Lerrel Pinto,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出QoQ方法，通过影响函数评估训练样本对验证集损失的贡献，系统识别高质量机器人演示数据，提升策略性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于演示的机器人端到端控制依赖大规模数据集，但人类遥操作收集的演示数据存在噪声和次优行为，数据质量成为瓶颈。现有数据筛选方法多为手动启发式，缺乏系统性

Method: 提出QoQ方法：1) 将数据质量定义为训练样本对验证集损失的贡献；2) 使用影响函数高效估计个体样本对模型性能的影响；3) 引入两项关键技术：采用验证样本最大影响捕捉最相关状态-动作对，以及聚合同一轨迹内状态-动作对的影响分数以减少噪声并提高数据覆盖

Result: 在仿真和真实环境实验中，QoQ方法相比现有数据选择方法能持续提升策略性能

Conclusion: QoQ提供了一种基于理论的数据质量评估框架，通过系统识别高质量演示数据，有效解决了机器人学习中的数据质量瓶颈问题

Abstract: Learning from demonstrations has emerged as a promising paradigm for end-to-end robot control, particularly when scaled to diverse and large datasets. However, the quality of demonstration data, often collected through human teleoperation, remains a critical bottleneck for effective data-driven robot learning. Human errors, operational constraints, and teleoperator variability introduce noise and suboptimal behaviors, making data curation essential yet largely manual and heuristic-driven. In this work, we propose Quality over Quantity (QoQ), a grounded and systematic approach to identifying high-quality data by defining data quality as the contribution of each training sample to reducing loss on validation demonstrations. To efficiently estimate this contribution, we leverage influence functions, which quantify the impact of individual training samples on model performance. We further introduce two key techniques to adapt influence functions for robot demonstrations: (i) using maximum influence across validation samples to capture the most relevant state-action pairs, and (ii) aggregating influence scores of state-action pairs within the same trajectory to reduce noise and improve data coverage. Experiments in both simulated and real-world settings show that QoQ consistently improves policy performances over prior data selection methods.

</details>


### [23] [High-Slip-Ratio Control for Peak Tire-Road Friction Estimation Using Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09073)
*Zhaohui Liang,Hang Zhou,Heye Huanh,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种高滑移率控制框架，使自动驾驶车辆在空载运行时主动激发峰值摩擦区域，通过约束最优控制和统计投影方法实现安全、准确的轮胎-路面摩擦系数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轮胎-路面摩擦系数估计方法主要依赖常规车辆的自然驾驶数据，这些数据通常在温和加减速条件下获得，滑移激励不足，对峰值摩擦系数的可观测性有限，难以准确估计恶劣路况下的摩擦特性。

Method: 采用简化的Magic Formula轮胎模型表示非线性滑移-力动力学，通过重复高滑移测量进行局部拟合；设计约束最优控制策略平衡滑移激励、轨迹跟踪和防碰撞；引入基于分箱的统计投影方法在噪声和局部稀疏条件下鲁棒估计峰值摩擦系数。

Result: 通过闭环仿真和实车实验验证了该框架的准确性、安全性和可行性，表明其适用于可扩展、经济高效的道路摩擦筛查。

Conclusion: 提出的高滑移率控制框架能够使自动驾驶车辆安全地主动激发峰值摩擦区域，结合统计投影方法实现了准确、鲁棒的轮胎-路面摩擦系数估计，为道路安全监测提供了有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of the tire-road friction coefficient (TRFC) is critical for ensuring safe vehicle control, especially under adverse road conditions. However, most existing methods rely on naturalistic driving data from regular vehicles, which typically operate under mild acceleration and braking. As a result, the data provide insufficient slip excitation and offer limited observability of the peak TRFC. This paper presents a high-slip-ratio control framework that enables automated vehicles (AVs) to actively excite the peak friction region during empty-haul operations while maintaining operational safety. A simplified Magic Formula tire model is adopted to represent nonlinear slip-force dynamics and is locally fitted using repeated high-slip measurements. To support safe execution in car-following scenarios, we formulate a constrained optimal control strategy that balances slip excitation, trajectory tracking, and collision avoidance. In parallel, a binning-based statistical projection method is introduced to robustly estimate peak TRFC under noise and local sparsity. The framework is validated through both closed-loop simulations and real-vehicle experiments, demonstrating its accuracy, safety, and feasibility for scalable, cost-effective roadway friction screening.

</details>


### [24] [Provably Safe Trajectory Generation for Manipulators Under Motion and Environmental Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09083)
*Fei Meng,Zijiang Yang,Xinyu Mao,Haobo Liang,Max Q. -H. Meng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于机器人机械臂在不确定和非凸环境中的风险有界运动规划框架，结合深度随机Koopman算子模型、分层验证方法和MPPI控制器，实现安全高效的运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理复杂几何形状和非高斯不确定性时，难以提供高效且形式化认证的碰撞风险保证。机器人机械臂在不确定和非凸环境中的运动规划需要更可靠的解决方案。

Method: 1) 使用刚性机械臂深度随机Koopman算子(RM-DeSKO)模型预测机器人状态分布；2) 提出分层验证方法，结合可并行物理仿真和平方和(SOS)编程进行细粒度形式化碰撞风险认证；3) 将SOS分解的二进制碰撞信息嵌入模型预测路径积分(MPPI)控制器改进策略。

Result: 在两个典型机器人机械臂上通过大量仿真和真实世界实验验证了框架有效性，包括具有挑战性的人机协作场景。展示了学习模型的仿真到真实转移能力，以及在复杂不确定环境中生成安全高效轨迹的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架为机器人机械臂在不确定和非凸环境中提供了形式化认证的风险有界运动规划解决方案，实现了安全性和效率的平衡，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Robot manipulators operating in uncertain and non-convex environments present significant challenges for safe and optimal motion planning. Existing methods often struggle to provide efficient and formally certified collision risk guarantees, particularly when dealing with complex geometries and non-Gaussian uncertainties. This article proposes a novel risk-bounded motion planning framework to address this unmet need. Our approach integrates a rigid manipulator deep stochastic Koopman operator (RM-DeSKO) model to robustly predict the robot's state distribution under motion uncertainty. We then introduce an efficient, hierarchical verification method that combines parallelizable physics simulations with sum-of-squares (SOS) programming as a filter for fine-grained, formal certification of collision risk. This method is embedded within a Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller that uniquely utilizes binary collision information from SOS decomposition to improve its policy. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated on two typical robot manipulators through extensive simulations and real-world experiments, including a challenging human-robot collaboration scenario, demonstrating sim-to-real transfer of the learned model and its ability to generate safe and efficient trajectories in complex, uncertain settings.

</details>


### [25] [PM-Nav: Priori-Map Guided Embodied Navigation in Functional Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09113)
*Jiang Gao,Xiangyu Dong,Haozhou Li,Haoran Zhao,Yaoming Zhou,Xiaoguang Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PM-Nav：一种基于先验地图引导的具身导航框架，通过语义先验地图、分层思维链提示和多模型协作机制，在功能建筑中实现高精度导航


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言驱动的具身导航范式在功能建筑中面临挑战，因为功能建筑具有高度相似的特征，缺乏有效利用先验空间知识的能力

Method: 1) 将环境地图转换为导航友好的语义先验地图；2) 设计带有注释先验地图的分层思维链提示模板进行精确路径规划；3) 构建多模型协作动作输出机制，完成导航规划的定位决策和执行控制

Result: 在自制功能建筑数据集上的综合测试显示，PM-Nav在仿真和现实环境中分别比SG-Nav和InstructNav平均提升511%/1175%和650%/400%，表现出巨大性能提升

Conclusion: PM-Nav作为功能建筑的骨干导航框架具有巨大潜力，其性能提升证明了利用先验地图指导具身导航的有效性

Abstract: Existing language-driven embodied navigation paradigms face challenges in functional buildings (FBs) with highly similar features, as they lack the ability to effectively utilize priori spatial knowledge. To tackle this issue, we propose a Priori-Map Guided Embodied Navigation (PM-Nav), wherein environmental maps are transformed into navigation-friendly semantic priori-maps, a hierarchical chain-of-thought prompt template with an annotation priori-map is designed to enable precise path planning, and a multi-model collaborative action output mechanism is built to accomplish positioning decisions and execution control for navigation planning. Comprehensive tests using a home-made FB dataset show that the PM-Nav obtains average improvements of 511\% and 1175\%, and 650\% and 400\% over the SG-Nav and the InstructNav in simulation and real-world, respectively. These tremendous boosts elucidate the great potential of using the PM-Nav as a backbone navigation framework for FBs.

</details>


### [26] [DexHiL: A Human-in-the-Loop Framework for Vision-Language-Action Model Post-Training in Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09121)
*Yifan Han,Zhongxi Chen,Yuxuan Zhao,Congsheng Xu,Yanming Shao,Yichuan Peng,Yao Mu,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexHiL：首个集成臂-手人机协同框架，用于灵巧VLA模型的后训练，通过干预感知数据采样和轻量遥操作界面提升灵巧操作性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然VLA模型在机器人操作中展现出良好的泛化能力，但在特定复杂下游任务部署时仍需有效后训练。同时，人机协同学习已被证明是优化机器人策略的强大机制，但将其扩展到灵巧操作仍具挑战性：多指控制是高维、接触密集的，且执行分布与标准臂部运动显著不同，导致现有灵巧VLA系统在可靠性和适应性方面受限

Method: DexHiL提出首个集成臂-手人机协同框架，包含：1）干预感知数据采样策略，优先选择校正段进行后训练；2）轻量遥操作界面，支持执行过程中的即时人工校正。该框架在单一系统中实现对臂和灵巧手的协调干预

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，DexHiL作为有效的后训练框架，带来显著的性能提升，在多个不同任务中平均成功率比标准离线微调基线高出25%

Conclusion: DexHiL成功解决了灵巧VLA模型的人机协同后训练挑战，通过集成臂-手协同干预机制和智能数据采样策略，显著提升了灵巧操作任务的可靠性和适应性

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising generalization capabilities in robotic manipulation, deploying them on specific and complex downstream tasks still demands effective post-training. In parallel, Human-in-the-Loop (HiL) learning has proven to be a powerful mechanism for refining robot policies. However, extending this paradigm to dexterous manipulation remains challenging: multi-finger control is high-dimensional, contact-intensive, and exhibits execution distributions that differ markedly from standard arm motions, leaving existing dexterous VLA systems limited in reliability and adaptability. We present DexHiL, the first integrated arm-hand human-in-the-loop framework for dexterous VLA models, enabling coordinated interventions over the arm and the dexterous hand within a single system. DexHiL introduces an intervention-aware data sampling strategy that prioritizes corrective segments for post-training, alongside a lightweight teleoperation interface that supports instantaneous human corrections during execution. Real-robot experiments demonstrate that DexHiL serves as an effective post-training framework, yielding a substantial performance leap, outperforming standard offline-only fine-tuning baselines by an average of 25% in success rates across distinct tasks.
  Project page: https://chenzhongxi-sjtu.github.io/dexhil/

</details>


### [27] [SPAN-Nav: Generalized Spatial Awareness for Versatile Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09163)
*Jiahang Liu,Tianyu Xu,Jiawei Chen,Lu Yue,Jiazhao Zhang,Zhiyong Wang,Minghan Li,Qisheng Zhao,Anqi Li,Qi Su,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPAN-Nav：一种通过占用预测任务从RGB视频流中提取通用3D空间先验的端到端基础模型，使用单个空间token注入空间线索，在多个导航基准上实现SOTA性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的具身导航方法在复杂环境中路径规划不可靠，主要原因是空间感知能力不足，需要增强通用3D空间意识

Method: 1) 通过室内外环境的大规模占用预测任务提取空间先验；2) 引入紧凑的空间表示，发现单个token足以封装导航所需的粗粒度线索；3) 受思维链启发，使用单个空间token在端到端框架中显式注入空间线索到动作推理；4) 多任务协同训练从广义空间先验中捕获任务自适应线索

Result: 1) 构建了包含420万占用标注的大规模数据集，覆盖室内外场景和多类型导航任务；2) 在三个基准测试中实现了最先进的性能，涵盖多样化场景和导航任务；3) 真实世界实验验证了在复杂物理场景中的鲁棒泛化能力和实际可靠性

Conclusion: SPAN-Nav通过紧凑的空间表示和端到端框架成功将通用3D空间意识注入具身导航，即使在没有显式空间监督的任务中也能实现鲁棒的空间感知和泛化能力

Abstract: Recent embodied navigation approaches leveraging Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate strong generalization in versatile Vision-Language Navigation (VLN). However, reliable path planning in complex environments remains challenging due to insufficient spatial awareness. In this work, we introduce SPAN-Nav, an end-to-end foundation model designed to infuse embodied navigation with universal 3D spatial awareness using RGB video streams. SPAN-Nav extracts spatial priors across diverse scenes through an occupancy prediction task on extensive indoor and outdoor environments. To mitigate the computational burden, we introduce a compact representation for spatial priors, finding that a single token is sufficient to encapsulate the coarse-grained cues essential for navigation tasks. Furthermore, inspired by the Chain-of-Thought (CoT) mechanism, SPAN-Nav utilizes this single spatial token to explicitly inject spatial cues into action reasoning through an end-to end framework. Leveraging multi-task co-training, SPAN-Nav captures task-adaptive cues from generalized spatial priors, enabling robust spatial awareness to generalize even to the task lacking explicit spatial supervision. To support comprehensive spatial learning, we present a massive dataset of 4.2 million occupancy annotations that covers both indoor and outdoor scenes across multi-type navigation tasks. SPAN-Nav achieves state-of-the-art performance across three benchmarks spanning diverse scenarios and varied navigation tasks. Finally, real-world experiments validate the robust generalization and practical reliability of our approach across complex physical scenarios.

</details>


### [28] [ZeroWBC: Learning Natural Visuomotor Humanoid Control Directly from Human Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09170)
*Haoran Yang,Jiacheng Bao,Yucheng Xin,Haoming Song,Yuyang Tian,Bin Zhao,Dong Wang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZeroWBC：从人类第一人称视频直接学习自然人形机器人全身控制策略，无需大规模机器人遥操作数据，实现自然的人机场景交互


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人形机器人场景交互控制存在局限性：1）现有方法局限于刚性运动模式；2）缺乏执行坐姿、踢腿等更自然人类行为的能力；3）获取真实机器人遥操作数据成本高昂且耗时。需要一种无需大规模遥操作数据就能实现自然全身控制的方法。

Method: 提出ZeroWBC框架：1）微调视觉语言模型（VLM），根据文本指令和第一人称视觉上下文预测未来全身人体运动；2）将生成的运动重定向到真实机器人关节；3）通过鲁棒的通用运动跟踪策略执行，实现人形机器人全身控制。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上的广泛实验表明，该方法在运动自然性和多功能性方面优于基线方法，成功建立了无需遥操作数据收集的全身控制流程，为通用人形机器人全身控制提供了可扩展且高效的范式。

Conclusion: ZeroWBC通过直接从人类第一人称视频学习，消除了大规模机器人遥操作数据的需求，实现了自然的人形机器人场景交互控制，为人形机器人全身控制提供了可扩展且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Achieving versatile and naturalistic whole-body control for humanoid robot scene-interaction remains a significant challenge. While some recent works have demonstrated autonomous humanoid interactive control, they are constrained to rigid locomotion patterns and expensive teleoperation data collection, lacking the versatility to execute more human-like natural behaviors such as sitting or kicking. Furthermore, acquiring the necessary real robot teleoperation data is prohibitively expensive and time-consuming. To address these limitations, we introduce ZeroWBC, a novel framework that learns a natural humanoid visuomotor control policy directly from human egocentric videos, eliminating the need for large-scale robot teleoperation data and enabling natural humanoid robot scene-interaction control. Specifically, our approach first fine-tunes a Vision-Language Model (VLM) to predict future whole-body human motions based on text instructions and egocentric visual context, then these generated motions are retargeted to real robot joints and executed via our robust general motion tracking policy for humanoid whole-body control. Extensive experiments on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot demonstrate that our method outperforms baseline approaches in motion naturalness and versatility, successfully establishing a pipeline that eliminates teleoperation data collection overhead for whole-body humanoid control, offering a scalable and efficient paradigm for general humanoid whole-body control.

</details>


### [29] [STONE Dataset: A Scalable Multi-Modal Surround-View 3D Traversability Dataset for Off-Road Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09175)
*Konyul Park,Daehun Kim,Jiyong Oh,Seunghoon Yu,Junseo Park,Jaehyun Park,Hongjae Shin,Hyungchan Cho,Jungho Kim,Jun Won Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STONE是一个用于越野导航的大规模多模态数据集，提供轨迹引导的3D可通行性地图和全面的环绕感知数据，包括128通道LiDAR、6个RGB相机和3个4D成像雷达，覆盖多种环境和条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集缺乏可扩展性和多模态性，限制了3D可通行性预测的研究进展。越野导航需要准确估计可通行区域并在多样化地形和感知条件下实现鲁棒感知。

Method: 开发了全自动、无需标注的流水线生成轨迹引导的3D可通行性地图：从LiDAR扫描重建密集地形表面，提取坡度、高程、粗糙度等几何属性，使用基于马氏距离的标准为机器人轨迹之外区域分配可通行性标签。

Result: 创建了STONE数据集，包含广泛的环境和条件（昼夜、草地、农田、建筑工地、湖泊等），建立了体素级3D可通行性预测基准，并在单模态和多模态设置下提供了强基线模型。

Conclusion: STONE通过自动标注流水线实现了可扩展的、几何感知的地面真值构建，为越野导航研究提供了大规模多模态数据集和基准，解决了现有数据集的局限性。

Abstract: Reliable off-road navigation requires accurate estimation of traversable regions and robust perception under diverse terrain and sensing conditions. However, existing datasets lack both scalability and multi-modality, which limits progress in 3D traversability prediction. In this work, we introduce STONE, a large-scale multi-modal dataset for off-road navigation. STONE provides (1) trajectory-guided 3D traversability maps generated by a fully automated, annotation-free pipeline, and (2) comprehensive surround-view sensing with synchronized 128-channel LiDAR, six RGB cameras, and three 4D imaging radars. The dataset covers a wide range of environments and conditions, including day and night, grasslands, farmlands, construction sites, and lakes. Our auto-labeling pipeline reconstructs dense terrain surfaces from LiDAR scans, extracts geometric attributes such as slope, elevation, and roughness, and assigns traversability labels beyond the robot's trajectory using a Mahalanobis-distance-based criterion. This design enables scalable, geometry-aware ground-truth construction without manual annotation. Finally, we establish a benchmark for voxel-level 3D traversability prediction and provide strong baselines under both single-modal and multi-modal settings. STONE is available at: https://konyul.github.io/STONE-dataset/

</details>


### [30] [Robust Spatiotemporal Motion Planning for Multi-Agent Autonomous Racing via Topological Gap Identification and Accelerated MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09188)
*Mingyi Zhang,Cheng Hu,Yiqin Wang,Haotong Qin,Hongye Su,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种拓扑间隙识别与加速MPC框架，用于高速多智能体自主赛车，通过预测对手行为、构建动态占用走廊选择最优超车间隙，并使用定制PTC求解器确保运动学可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高速多智能体自主赛车方法存在两个主要问题：要么过度简化智能体间交互，要么放弃严格的运动学约束。这限制了在严格计算限制下实现鲁棒的时空规划和精确控制。

Method: 1. 使用SGPs预测对手行为；2. 构建动态占用走廊；3. 通过拓扑间隙识别选择最优超车间隙；4. 采用线性时变MPC确保严格运动学可行性；5. 使用定制伪瞬态连续(PTC)求解器实现高频执行。

Result: 在F1TENTH平台上的实验结果显示：1. 在顺序场景中总机动时间减少51.6%；2. 在密集瓶颈场景中超车成功率始终超过81%；3. 平均计算延迟降低20.3%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合拓扑间隙识别和加速MPC框架，在保持严格运动学约束的同时实现了鲁棒的交互处理，显著提升了高速自主赛车性能，推动了安全高速自主赛车的边界。

Abstract: High-speed multi-agent autonomous racing demands robust spatiotemporal planning and precise control under strict computational limits. Current methods often oversimplify interactions or abandon strict kinematic constraints. We resolve this by proposing a Topological Gap Identification and Accelerated MPC framework. By predicting opponent behaviors via SGPs, our method constructs dynamic occupancy corridors to robustly select optimal overtaking gaps. We ensure strict kinematic feasibility using a Linear Time-Varying MPC powered by a customized Pseudo-Transient Continuation (PTC) solver for high-frequency execution. Experimental results on the F1TENTH platform show that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines: it reduces total maneuver time by 51.6% in sequential scenarios, consistently maintains an overtaking success rate exceeding 81% in dense bottlenecks, and lowers average computational latency by 20.3%, pushing the boundaries of safe and high-speed autonomous racing.

</details>


### [31] [WESPR: Wind-adaptive Energy-Efficient Safe Perception & Planning for Robust Flight with Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09194)
*Khuzema Habib,Pranav Deshakulkarni Manjunath,Kasra Torshizi,Troi Williams,Pratap Tokekar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WESPR框架通过几何感知和局部天气数据快速预测环境几何对局部风况的影响，实现主动路径规划和控制调整，相比风无关自适应控制器显著减少轨迹偏差并提高稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部风况严重影响无人机性能，现有自适应控制器虽能缓解湍流但无法感知产生湍流的周围几何结构，而依赖流体动力学模拟的现有方法计算成本高，难以实时适应新环境和条件。

Method: WESPR是一个轻量级框架，集成几何感知和局部天气数据来估计风场、计算成本效益路径并调整控制策略，整个流程在10秒内完成，使用Crazyflie无人机在湍流障碍课程中进行验证。

Result: 相比风无关自适应控制器，WESPR实现了12.5-58.7%的最大轨迹偏差减少和24.6%的稳定性提升，验证了框架在实时风场预测和主动控制调整方面的有效性。

Conclusion: WESPR通过快速预测环境几何对风况的影响，实现了主动路径规划和控制适应，为无人机在复杂风环境中的导航提供了实用解决方案，弥补了现有方法的实时性不足。

Abstract: Local wind conditions strongly influence drone performance: headwinds increase flight time, crosswinds and wind shear hinder agility in cluttered spaces, while tailwinds reduce travel time. Although adaptive controllers can mitigate turbulence, they remain unaware of the surrounding geometry that generates it, preventing proactive avoidance. Existing methods that model how wind interacts with the environment typically rely on computationally expensive fluid dynamics simulations, limiting real-time adaptation to new environments and conditions. To bridge this gap, we present WESPR, a fast framework that predicts how environmental geometry affects local wind conditions, enabling proactive path planning and control adaptation. Our lightweight pipeline integrates geometric perception and local weather data to estimate wind fields, compute cost-efficient paths, and adjust control strategies-all within 10 seconds. We validate WESPR on a Crazyflie drone navigating turbulent obstacle courses. Our results show a 12.5-58.7% reduction in maximum trajectory deviation and a 24.6% improvement in stability compared to a wind-agnostic adaptive controller.

</details>


### [32] [TRIP-Bag: A Portable Teleoperation System for Plug-and-Play Robotic Arms and Leaders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09226)
*Noboru Myers,Sankalp Yamsani,Obin Kwon,Joohyung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRIP-Bag是一个便携式遥操作系统，用于在多样环境中收集高质量机器人操作数据，解决了现有数据收集方法中的体现差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模、多样化的机器人操作演示数据获取仍然是一个主要挑战。现有的野外数据收集方法通常依赖于基于视觉的手持夹爪或手套姿态估计，这引入了收集平台与目标机器人之间的体现差距。遥操作系统虽然消除了体现差距，但通常难以在实验室环境外部署。

Method: 提出了TRIP-Bag（便携包中的遥操作、记录和智能），这是一个完全包含在商业手提箱中的便携式木偶式遥操作系统。系统设置时间不到5分钟，采用直接关节到关节的遥操作方式，能够在任何环境中实现快速可靠的数据收集。

Result: 通过非专家用户的实验验证了TRIP-Bag的可用性，表明系统直观易用。通过训练基准操作策略确认了收集数据的质量，证明了其作为机器人学习实用资源的价值。

Conclusion: TRIP-Bag为在不同环境中收集高保真操作数据提供了一个实用的解决方案，解决了现有数据收集方法中的体现差距和部署限制问题，为机器人学习提供了高质量的数据资源。

Abstract: Large scale, diverse demonstration data for manipulation tasks remains a major challenge in learning-based robot policies. Existing in-the-wild data collection approaches often rely on vision-based pose estimation of hand-held grippers or gloves, which introduces an embodiment gap between the collection platform and the target robot. Teleoperation systems eliminate the embodiment gap, but are typically impractical to deploy outside the laboratory environment. We propose TRIP-Bag (Teleoperation, Recording, Intelligence in a Portable Bag), a portable, puppeteer-style teleoperation system fully contained within a commercial suitcase, as a practical solution for collecting high-fidelity manipulation data across varied settings. With a setup time of under five minutes and direct joint-to-joint teleoperation, TRIP-Bag enables rapid and reliable data collection in any environment. We validated TRIP-Bag's usability through experiments with non-expert users, showing that the system is intuitive and easy to operate. Furthermore, we confirmed the quality of the collected data by training benchmark manipulation policies, demonstrating its value as a practical resource for robot learning.

</details>


### [33] [MO-Playground: Massively Parallelized Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09237)
*Neil Janwani,Ellen Novoseller,Vernon J. Lawhern,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MORLAX（GPU原生快速多目标强化学习算法）和MO-Playground（GPU加速多目标环境库），实现25-270倍加速，解决MORL算法并行化不足问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MORL算法无法有效利用大规模并行化（如同时模拟数千个环境），导致计算时间大幅增加，限制了MORL在复杂多目标机器人问题中的应用

Method: 1) 开发MORLAX：GPU原生的快速MORL算法；2) 创建MO-Playground：pip可安装的GPU加速多目标环境库；3) 在BRUCE人形机器人环境中验证，学习跨越6个现实目标的帕累托最优策略

Result: 在几分钟内近似帕累托集，相比传统CPU方法实现25-270倍加速，同时获得更优的帕累托前沿超体积；成功为BRUCE人形机器人学习到跨越平滑度、效率、手臂摆动等6个目标的帕累托最优运动策略

Conclusion: MORLAX和MO-Playground的组合显著加速了多目标强化学习，使复杂机器人问题的帕累托最优策略学习变得可行，为MORL的实际应用开辟了新途径

Abstract: Multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL) is a powerful tool to learn Pareto-optimal policy families across conflicting objectives. However, unlike traditional RL algorithms, existing MORL algorithms do not effectively leverage large-scale parallelization to concurrently simulate thousands of environments, resulting in vastly increased computation time. Ultimately, this has limited MORL's application towards complex multi-objective robotics problems. To address these challenges, we present 1) MORLAX, a new GPU-native, fast MORL algorithm, and 2) MO-Playground, a pip-installable playground of GPU-accelerated multi-objective environments. Together, MORLAX and MO-Playground approximate Pareto sets within minutes, offering 25-270x speed-ups compared to legacy CPU-based approaches whilst achieving superior Pareto front hypervolumes. We demonstrate the versatility of our approach by implementing a custom BRUCE humanoid robot environment using MO-Playground and learning Pareto-optimal locomotion policies across 6 realistic objectives for BRUCE, such as smoothness, efficiency and arm swinging.

</details>


### [34] [See, Plan, Rewind: Progress-Aware Vision-Language-Action Models for Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09292)
*Tingjun Dai,Mingfei Han,Tingwen Du,Zhiheng Liu,Zhihui Li,Salman Khan,Jun Yu,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPR是一个进度感知的视觉-语言-动作框架，通过"看-规划-回滚"循环将语言指令动态分解为空间子目标序列，实现鲁棒的机器人操作和错误恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过明确的、可操作的里程碑来测量任务进度对于鲁棒的机器人操作至关重要。进度感知能力使模型能够理解当前任务状态、预测可验证的中间状态，并在进度停滞时检测和恢复故障。

Method: SPR框架通过连续的"看-规划-回滚"核心循环运行：1) 看：观察当前状态和即将到来的里程碑；2) 规划：规划到下一个2D路径点的轨迹；3) 回滚：通过监控与预期序列的进度，在失败时回滚到可恢复状态。这种闭环方法无需额外训练数据或辅助模型即可实现鲁棒的错误纠正。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，SPR比MolmoAct基线高出5%。在具有未见指令和初始状态的挑战性LIBERO-Plus基准测试中，SPR实现了最先进的鲁棒性，性能下降最小，超越了OpenVLA-OFT和UniVLA，展示了卓越的分布外鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SPR框架通过进度感知和动态子目标分解，实现了鲁棒的机器人操作和错误恢复，在多个基准测试中表现出优异的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Measurement of task progress through explicit, actionable milestones is critical for robust robotic manipulation. This progress awareness enables a model to ground its current task status, anticipate verifiable intermediate states, and detect and recover from failures when progress stalls. To embody this capability, we introduce See, Plan, Rewind (SPR), a progress-aware vision-language-action framework that dynamically grounds language instructions into a sequence of spatial subgoals. SPR operates through a continuous core cycle, Seeing the current state and upcoming milestone, Planning a trajectory towards the next 2D waypoint, and Rewinding to a recoverable state upon failure by monitoring progress against the expected sequence. This closed-loop approach enables robust error correction without requiring additional training data or auxiliary models. Extensive experiments demonstrate the framework's effectiveness, generalization and robustness: SPR outperforms the MolmoAct baseline by 5\% on the LIBERO benchmark. On the challenging LIBERO-Plus benchmark with unseen instructions and initial states, SPR achieves state-of-the-art robustness with the smallest performance drop, surpassing OpenVLA-OFT and UniVLA, demonstrating superior out-of-distribution robustness.

</details>


### [35] [NLiPsCalib: An Efficient Calibration Framework for High-Fidelity 3D Reconstruction of Curved Visuotactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09319)
*Xuhao Qin,Feiyu Zhao,Yatao Leng,Runze Hu,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NLiPsCalib：一种用于曲面视觉触觉传感器的物理一致高效校准框架，通过集成可控近场光源和近光光度立体视觉简化校准过程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物仿生曲面视觉触觉传感器虽然能增强接触适应性，但非均匀照明会降低感知质量，现有校准方法依赖定制压头和专用设备，成本高且劳动密集

Method: 提出NLiPsCalib框架，集成可控近场光源，利用近光光度立体视觉估计接触几何，仅需与日常物体的简单接触即可完成校准；开发NLiPsTac传感器验证框架

Result: 实验证明该方法能实现高保真3D重建，适用于多种曲面形态，校准过程简单，降低了开发定制化视觉触觉传感器的门槛

Conclusion: 该研究提出的校准框架使视觉触觉传感更易普及，为开发各种几何形状的定制传感器提供了可行方案

Abstract: Recent advances in visuotactile sensors increasingly employ biomimetic curved surfaces to enhance sensorimotor capabilities. Although such curved visuotactile sensors enable more conformal object contact, their perceptual quality is often degraded by non-uniform illumination, which reduces reconstruction accuracy and typically necessitates calibration. Existing calibration methods commonly rely on customized indenters and specialized devices to collect large-scale photometric data, but these processes are expensive and labor-intensive. To overcome these calibration challenges, we present NLiPsCalib, a physics-consistent and efficient calibration framework for curved visuotactile sensors. NLiPsCalib integrates controllable near-field light sources and leverages Near-Light Photometric Stereo (NLiPs) to estimate contact geometry, simplifying calibration to just a few simple contacts with everyday objects. We further introduce NLiPsTac, a controllable-light-source tactile sensor developed to validate our framework. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach enables high-fidelity 3D reconstruction across diverse curved form factors with a simple calibration procedure. We emphasize that our approach lowers the barrier to developing customized visuotactile sensors of diverse geometries, thereby making visuotactile sensing more accessible to the broader community.

</details>


### [36] [From Flow to One Step: Real-Time Multi-Modal Trajectory Policies via Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation-based Distribution Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09415)
*Ju Dong,Liding Zhang,Lei Zhang,Yu Fu,Kaixin Bai,Zoltan-Csaba Marton,Zhenshan Bing,Zhaopeng Chen,Alois Christian Knoll,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通过隐式最大似然估计将条件流匹配专家蒸馏为快速单步学生模型的框架，解决扩散模型在机器人控制中延迟高的问题，同时保持多模态动作分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散和流匹配的生成策略在机器人操作中表现出色，但依赖迭代ODE积分导致高延迟，限制了高频闭环控制。现有单步加速方法存在分布坍缩问题，产生平均化轨迹而无法执行连贯操作策略。

Method: 通过隐式最大似然估计将条件流匹配专家蒸馏为单步学生模型；使用双向Chamfer距离作为集合级目标，促进模态覆盖和保真度；统一感知编码器集成多视角RGB、深度、点云和本体感知为几何感知表示。

Result: 实现高频控制，支持实时滚动时域重规划和动态扰动下的鲁棒性提升，在单次前向传播中保持教师模型的多模态动作分布。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了生成策略在机器人控制中的延迟问题，通过有效的蒸馏方法在保持多模态分布的同时实现实时控制，为动态环境中的鲁棒操作提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching achieve strong performance in robotic manipulation by modeling multi-modal human demonstrations. However, their reliance on iterative Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) integration introduces substantial latency, limiting high-frequency closed-loop control. Recent single-step acceleration methods alleviate this overhead but often exhibit distributional collapse, producing averaged trajectories that fail to execute coherent manipulation strategies. We propose a framework that distills a Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) expert into a fast single-step student via Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (IMLE). A bi-directional Chamfer distance provides a set-level objective that promotes both mode coverage and fidelity, enabling preservation of the teacher multi-modal action distribution in a single forward pass. A unified perception encoder further integrates multi-view RGB, depth, point clouds, and proprioception into a geometry-aware representation. The resulting high-frequency control supports real-time receding-horizon re-planning and improved robustness under dynamic disturbances.

</details>


### [37] [Stein Variational Ergodic Surface Coverage with SE(3) Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09458)
*Jiayun Li,Yufeng Jin,Sangli Teng,Dejian Gong,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于预条件SE(3) Stein变分梯度下降的采样优化方法，用于生成机器人表面覆盖的遍历轨迹，解决了现有方法在处理点云目标和SE(3)约束方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遍历轨迹优化方法在覆盖任务中表现良好，但在处理点云目标时面临非凸优化景观和SE(3)约束处理不足的问题，特别是在采样优化技术中。

Method: 提出预条件SE(3) Stein变分梯度下降方法：1) 将点云遍历覆盖重新表述为流形感知采样问题；2) 推导SE(3)特定的SVGD粒子更新；3) 开发预条件器加速轨迹优化收敛。

Result: 在3D点云表面覆盖基准测试和机器人表面绘制任务中，该方法相比现有轨迹优化和采样优化基线实现了更优的覆盖质量，计算效率可接受，并在真实机器人实验中验证。

Conclusion: 提出的采样框架能够一致地找到比现有方法更优的局部最优解，同时保持SE(3)几何结构，为机器人表面操作任务提供了有效的轨迹生成方法。

Abstract: Surface manipulation tasks require robots to generate trajectories that comprehensively cover complex 3D surfaces while maintaining precise end-effector poses. Existing ergodic trajectory optimization (TO) methods demonstrate success in coverage tasks, while struggling with point-cloud targets due to the nonconvex optimization landscapes and the inadequate handling of SE(3) constraints in sampling-as-optimization (SAO) techniques. In this work, we introduce a preconditioned SE(3) Stein Variational Gradient Descent (SVGD) approach for SAO ergodic trajectory generation. Our proposed approach comprises multiple innovations. First, we reformulate point-cloud ergodic coverage as a manifold-aware sampling problem. Second, we derive SE(3)-specific SVGD particle updates, and, third, we develop a preconditioner to accelerate TO convergence. Our sampling-based framework consistently identifies superior local optima compared to strong optimization-based and SAO baselines while preserving the SE(3) geometric structure. Experiments on a 3D point-cloud surface coverage benchmark and robotic surface drawing tasks demonstrate that our method achieves superior coverage quality with tractable computation in our setting relative to existing TO and SAO approaches, and is validated in real-world robot experiments.

</details>


### [38] [SEA-Nav: Efficient Policy Learning for Safe and Agile Quadruped Navigation in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09460)
*Shiyi Chen,Mingye Yang,Haiyan Mao,Jiaqi Zhang,Haiyi Liu,Shuheng He,Debing Zhang,Zihao Qiu,Chun Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SEA-Nav强化学习框架，在密集障碍环境中实现四足机器人安全、高效、敏捷的导航，仅需分钟级训练时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人导航方法存在局限性：要么在简单障碍分布中缺乏安全性和敏捷性，要么在复杂环境中运动缓慢且需要过长的训练时间

Method: 1. 使用基于可微控制屏障函数(CBF)的防护层约束导航策略输出安全速度命令；2. 引入自适应碰撞回放机制和危险探索奖励，增加从关键经验中学习的概率；3. 结合运动学动作约束确保安全速度命令

Result: 首次在现实世界中实现具有挑战性的四足机器人导航，且仅需分钟级训练时间

Conclusion: SEA-Nav框架能够在密集障碍环境中实现安全、高效、敏捷的四足机器人导航，显著缩短训练时间并成功部署到物理系统

Abstract: Efficiently training quadruped robot navigation in densely cluttered environments remains a significant challenge. Existing methods are either limited by a lack of safety and agility in simple obstacle distributions or suffer from slow locomotion in complex environments, often requiring excessively long training phases. To this end, we propose SEA-Nav (Safe, Efficient, and Agile Navigation), a reinforcement learning framework for quadruped navigation.
