Within diverse and dense obstacle environments, a differentiable control barrier function (CBF)-based shield constraints the navigation policy to output safe velocity commands. An adaptive collision replay mechanism and hazardous exploration rewards are introduced to increase the probability of learning from critical experiences, guiding efficient exploration and exploitation. Finally, kinematic action constraints are incorporated to ensure safe velocity commands, facilitating successful physical deployment. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first approach that achieves highly challenging quadruped navigation in the real world with minute-level training time.

</details>


### [39] [Receptogenesis in a Vascularized Robotic Embodiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09473)
*Kadri-Ann Pankratov,Leonid Zinatullin,Hans Priks,Adele Metsniit,Urmas Johanson,Tarmo Tamm,Alvo Aabloo,Edoardo Sinibaldi,Indrek Must*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种通过流体血管系统实现机器人原位材料合成与硬件重构的方法，使机器人能够在运行过程中自主生成传感器等硬件组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模块化机器人系统依赖于部署前后的离散组件集成，缺乏真正的物理适应性。受生物循环系统启发，研究者希望开发能够通过动态材料重构实现物理适应和发展的机器人系统，这种能力目前在机器人领域尚未实现。

Method: 采用血管化机器人复合材料，通过协调流体前体运输与外部局部UV照射，驱动原位光聚合反应，从内向外化学重构血管系统。该反应将含有光潜伏引发剂的前体转化为UV敏感的聚吡咯固体分散体，建立传感模式。

Result: 成功实现了"受体生成"——基于环境线索从内部流体储备按需构建传感器。新合成的传感器通过特征性电阻抗降低得到验证，并成功闭合了蛾类仿生机器人翅膀拍动的控制回路，实时提升了机器人能力。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了一个基于材料的构成演化框架，使机器人能够在复杂环境中物理生长支持新兴行为所需的硬件，为自主系统生成神经血管系统等专门特征提供了途径。

Abstract: Equipping robotic systems with the capacity to generate $\textit{ex novo}$ hardware during operation extends control of physical adaptability. Unlike modular systems that rely on discrete component integration pre- or post-deployment, we envision the possibility that physical adaptation and development emerge from dynamic material restructuring to shape the body's intrinsic functions. Drawing inspiration from circulatory systems that redistribute mass and function in biological organisms, we utilize fluidics to restructure the material interface, a capability currently unpaired in robotics. Here, we realize this synthetic growth capability through a vascularized robotic composite designed for programmable material synthesis, demonstrated via receptogenesis - the on-demand construction of sensors from internal fluid reserves based on environmental cues. By coordinating the fluidic transport of precursors with external localized UV irradiation, we drive an $\textit{in situ}$ photopolymerization that chemically reconstructs the vasculature from the inside out. This reaction converts precursors with photolatent initiator into a solid dispersion of UV-sensitive polypyrrole, establishing a sensing modality validated by a characteristic decrease in electrical impedance. The newly synthesized sensor closed a control loop to regulate wing flapping in a moth-inspired robotic demonstrator. This physical update increased the robot's capability in real time. This work establishes a materials-based framework for constitutive evolution, enabling robots to physically grow the hardware needed to support emerging behaviors in a complex environment; for example, suggesting a pathway toward autonomous systems capable of generating specialized features, such as neurovascular systems in situated robotics.

</details>


### [40] [StyleVLA: Driving Style-Aware Vision Language Action Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09482)
*Yuan Gao,Dengyuan Hua,Mattia Piccinini,Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Korbinian Moller,Lin Li,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StyleVLA是一个物理感知的视觉语言动作模型框架，用于生成多样且物理可行的自动驾驶行为，通过混合损失函数和大规模指令数据集训练，在驾驶风格适应性和轨迹可行性方面优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言动作模型主要生成通用的无碰撞轨迹，但缺乏对多样化驾驶风格的适应能力，且将轨迹生成视为简单的token预测可能导致运动学不可行的动作。需要解决个性化驾驶和物理可行性问题。

Method: 提出StyleVLA框架，基于Qwen3-VL-4B构建，引入混合损失函数结合运动学一致性约束和连续回归头来提升轨迹可行性。构建大规模指令数据集，包含1.2k场景、76k鸟瞰图和42k第一人称视角样本，涵盖五种驾驶风格和自然语言指令。

Result: StyleVLA在综合驾驶评分（成功率、物理可行性、风格一致性）上显著优于专有模型和SOTA VLA模型：BEV评分0.55 vs Gemini-3-Pro的0.32，FPV评分0.51 vs 0.35。4B参数模型在特定领域任务上超越闭源模型。

Conclusion: 专业化、物理感知的轻量级模型可以在特定领域任务上超越闭源模型，为自动驾驶中的个性化驾驶行为生成提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) bridge visual perception and linguistic reasoning. In Autonomous Driving (AD), this synergy has enabled Vision Language Action (VLA) models, which translate high-level multimodal understanding into driving behaviors, typically represented as future trajectories. However, existing VLA models mainly generate generic collision-free trajectories. Beyond collision avoidance, adapting to diverse driving styles (e.g., sporty, comfortable) is essential for personalized driving. Moreover, many methods treat trajectory generation as naive token prediction, which can produce kinematically infeasible actions. To address these limitations, we present StyleVLA, a physics-informed VLA framework for generating diverse and physically plausible driving behaviors. We introduce a hybrid loss that combines a kinematic consistency constraint with a continuous regression head to improve trajectory feasibility. To train StyleVLA, built on Qwen3-VL-4B, we construct a large-scale instruction dataset with over 1.2k scenarios, 76k Bird's Eye View (BEV) samples, and 42k First Person View (FPV) samples, with ground-truth trajectories for five driving styles and natural-language instructions. Experiments show that our 4B-parameter StyleVLA significantly outperforms proprietary models (e.g., Gemini-3-Pro) and state-of-the-art VLA models. Using a composite driving score measuring success rate, physical feasibility, and style adherence, StyleVLA achieves 0.55 on BEV and 0.51 on FPV, versus 0.32 and 0.35 for Gemini-3-Pro. These results show that a specialized, physics-informed, lightweight model can surpass closed-source models on domain-specific tasks.

</details>


### [41] [Beyond Short-Horizon: VQ-Memory for Robust Long-Horizon Manipulation in Non-Markovian Simulation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09513)
*Wang Honghui,Jing Zhi,Ao Jicong,Song Shiji,Li Xuelong,Huang Gao,Bai Chenjia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RuleSafe是一个基于LLM辅助仿真框架的铰接式操作基准，包含多种解锁机制的安全箱任务，需要多阶段推理和操作策略。论文提出VQ-Memory，一种使用VQ-VAE编码过去本体感知状态的紧凑结构化时序表示，提升VLA模型的长时程规划和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人仿真基准主要关注简单的拾取放置任务，无法捕捉真实世界任务的非马尔可夫特性和铰接物体交互的复杂性。真实机器人数据收集成本高昂，需要更复杂的仿真基准来评估和生成数据。

Method: 1. 提出RuleSafe基准：基于LLM辅助仿真框架构建，包含具有不同解锁机制（钥匙锁、密码锁、逻辑锁）的安全箱，需要多阶段推理和操作策略；2. 提出VQ-Memory表示：使用向量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）将过去本体感知状态编码为离散潜在标记，过滤低级噪声同时保留高级任务阶段上下文，提供轻量且鲁棒的时序线索。

Result: 在先进的VLA模型和扩散策略上进行大量实验，结果表明VQ-Memory能够：1. 持续改进长时程规划；2. 增强对未见配置的泛化能力；3. 实现更高效的操作并降低计算成本。

Conclusion: RuleSafe基准捕捉了真实世界铰接操作的非马尔可夫和长时程特性，VQ-Memory表示通过紧凑结构化时序编码有效提升了现有VLA模型的性能，为复杂机器人操作任务提供了更好的评估框架和解决方案。

Abstract: The high cost of collecting real-robot data has made robotic simulation a scalable platform for both evaluation and data generation. Yet most existing benchmarks concentrate on simple manipulation tasks such as pick-and-place, failing to capture the non-Markovian characteristics of real-world tasks and the complexity of articulated object interactions. To address this limitation, we present RuleSafe, a new articulated manipulation benchmark built upon a scalable LLM-aided simulation framework. RuleSafe features safes with diverse unlocking mechanisms, such as key locks, password locks, and logic locks, which require different multi-stage reasoning and manipulation strategies. These LLM-generated rules produce non-Markovian and long-horizon tasks that require temporal modeling and memory-based reasoning. We further propose VQ-Memory, a compact and structured temporal representation that uses vector-quantized variational autoencoders (VQ-VAEs) to encode past proprioceptive states into discrete latent tokens. This representation filters low-level noise while preserving high-level task-phase context, providing lightweight yet robust temporal cues that are compatible with existing Vision-Language-Action models (VLA). Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art VLA models and diffusion policies show that VQ-Memory consistently improves long-horizon planning, enhances generalization to unseen configurations, and enables more efficient manipulation with reduced computational cost. Project page: vqmemory.github.io

</details>


### [42] [NS-VLA: Towards Neuro-Symbolic Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09542)
*Ziyue Zhu,Shangyang Wu,Shuai Zhao,Zhiqiu Zhao,Shengjie Li,Yi Wang,Fang Li,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NS-VLA框架通过在线强化学习结合神经符号方法，解决VLA模型在可重用基元学习、数据依赖和探索能力方面的挑战，在机器人操作任务中表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型面临三个主要挑战：1) 难以学习相关且可重用的操作基元；2) 过度依赖大规模数据和复杂架构；3) 缺乏超越演示数据的探索能力。需要一种更高效、更具泛化性的框架。

Method: 提出神经符号VLA框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 符号编码器将视觉和语言特征嵌入并提取结构化基元；2) 符号求解器实现数据高效的动作序列生成；3) 在线强化学习优化生成过程并扩展探索空间。

Result: 在机器人操作基准测试中，NS-VLA在单次训练和数据扰动设置下均优于先前方法，同时展现出优越的零样本泛化能力、高数据效率和扩展的探索空间。

Conclusion: NS-VLA框架通过神经符号方法和在线强化学习的结合，有效解决了VLA模型的关键挑战，为机器人操作任务提供了更高效、更具泛化性的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are formulated to ground instructions in visual context and generate action sequences for robotic manipulation. Despite recent progress, VLA models still face challenges in learning related and reusable primitives, reducing reliance on large-scale data and complex architectures, and enabling exploration beyond demonstrations. To address these challenges, we propose a novel Neuro-Symbolic Vision-Language-Action (NS-VLA) framework via online reinforcement learning (RL). It introduces a symbolic encoder to embedding vision and language features and extract structured primitives, utilizes a symbolic solver for data-efficient action sequencing, and leverages online RL to optimize generation via expansive exploration. Experiments on robotic manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that NS-VLA outperforms previous methods in both one-shot training and data-perturbed settings, while simultaneously exhibiting superior zero-shot generalizability, high data efficiency and expanded exploration space. Our code is available.

</details>


### [43] [Trajectory Optimization for Self-Wrap-Aware Cable-Towed Planar Object Manipulation under Implicit Tension Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09557)
*Yu Li,Amin Fakhari,Hamid Sadeghian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种考虑自缠绕的电缆牵引操纵优化方法，通过路由感知、张力隐式轨迹优化（TITO）耦合张力约束和路由条件传输映射，并构建了从严格模式到三种松弛形式的层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电缆/绳索在可变形物体操纵中普遍存在，作为力传递介质，其路由和接触决定了力矩传递方式。在电缆牵引操纵中，传递是单向和混合的：拉紧时只能拉动，松弛时无力；实践中电缆还会接触物体边界并自缠绕边缘，这不仅涉及碰撞避免，更改变了力矩传递通道，通过改变有效作用点和力臂，将路由几何与刚体运动和张力耦合起来。

Method: 将自缠绕牵引建模为路由感知、张力隐式轨迹优化（TITO）问题，耦合(i)张力隐式的拉紧/松弛约束和(ii)路由条件的有效长度和力矩传输映射。构建了从严格模式条件参考到三种可处理松弛形式的层次结构：全模式松弛（FMR）、二元模式松弛（BMR）和隐式模式松弛（IMR）。

Result: 在平面牵引任务中，发现将路由作为显式决策通常会产生保守解，保持在切换边界附近；而IMR通过状态演化诱导自缠绕，并在需要转向时利用重定向的扭矩通道。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了电缆牵引操纵中自缠绕现象的重要性，提出的TITO框架和松弛层次结构能够有效处理路由几何与刚体运动的耦合，其中隐式模式松弛（IMR）方法能够自然地诱导自缠绕并利用重定向扭矩通道，为电缆牵引操纵提供了更有效的优化方法。

Abstract: Cable/rope elements are pervasive in deformable-object manipulation, often serving as a deformable force-transmission medium whose routing and contact determine how wrenches are delivered. In cable-towed manipulation, transmission is unilateral and hybrid: the tether can pull only when taut and becomes force-free when slack; in practice, the tether may also contact the object boundary and self-wrap around edges, which is not merely collision avoidance but a change of the wrench transmission channel by shifting the effective application point and moment arm, thereby coupling routing geometry with rigid-body motion and tensioning. We formulate self-wrap towing as a routing-aware, tensioning-implicit trajectory optimization (TITO) problem that couples (i) a tensioning-implicit taut/slack constraint and (ii) routing-conditioned transmission maps for effective length and wrench, and we build a relaxation hierarchy from a strict mode-conditioned reference to three tractable relaxations: Full-Mode Relaxation (FMR), Binary-Mode Relaxation (BMR), and Implicit-Mode Relaxation (IMR). Across planar towing tasks, we find that making routing an explicit decision often yields conservative solutions that stay near switching boundaries, whereas IMR induces self-wrap through state evolution and exploits the redirected torque channel whenever turning requires it.

</details>


### [44] [SCDP: Learning Humanoid Locomotion from Partial Observations via Mixed-Observation Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09574)
*Milo Carroll,Tianhu Peng,Lingfan Bao,Chengxu Zhou,Zhibin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCDP是一种仅使用机载传感器实现人形机器人运动控制的方法，无需显式状态估计，通过传感器历史条件化扩散模型来预测特权状态-动作轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖特权全身状态，需要复杂且不可靠的状态估计，限制了人形机器人运动控制从离线数据集到可部署策略的蒸馏。

Method: 提出传感器条件化扩散策略(SCDP)：1) 混合观测训练，模型以传感器历史为条件，预测特权未来状态-动作轨迹；2) 受限去噪、上下文分布对齐和上下文感知注意力掩码，鼓励模型内隐状态估计并防止训练-部署不匹配。

Result: 仿真中，SCDP在速度控制任务上达到99-100%成功率，在AMASS测试集上达到93%跟踪成功率，性能与特权基线相当；成功在真实G1人形机器人上以50Hz频率部署，无需外部传感或状态估计。

Conclusion: SCDP通过仅使用机载传感器实现稳健的人形机器人运动控制，消除了对显式状态估计的依赖，为实际部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Distilling humanoid locomotion control from offline datasets into deployable policies remains a challenge, as existing methods rely on privileged full-body states that require complex and often unreliable state estimation. We present Sensor-Conditioned Diffusion Policies (SCDP) that enables humanoid locomotion using only onboard sensors, eliminating the need for explicit state estimation. SCDP decouples sensing from supervision through mixed-observation training: diffusion model conditions on sensor histories while being supervised to predict privileged future state-action trajectories, enforcing the model to infer the motion dynamics under partial observability. We further develop restricted denoising, context distribution alignment, and context-aware attention masking to encourage implicit state estimation within the model and to prevent train-deploy mismatch. We validate SCDP on velocity-commanded locomotion and motion reference tracking tasks. In simulation, SCDP achieves near-perfect success on velocity control (99-100%) and 93% tracking success in AMASS test set, performing comparable to privileged baselines while using only onboard sensors. Finally, we deploy the trained policy on a real G1 humanoid at 50 Hz, demonstrating robust real robot locomotion without external sensing or state estimation.

</details>


### [45] [Towards Terrain-Aware Safe Locomotion for Quadrupedal Robots Using Proprioceptive Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09585)
*Peiyu Yang,Jiatao Ding,Wei Pan,Claudio Semini,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于本体感知的四足机器人安全运动框架，包含2.5维地形估计与安全临界控制屏障函数，仅使用IMU、关节编码器和接触力传感器实现地形感知的安全行走


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决四足机器人在不平坦地形上安全行走的挑战，特别是针对仅配备本体感知传感器（IMU、关节编码器、接触力传感器）的低成本机器人，需要可靠的估计方法和安全保证的控制策略

Method: 1) 提出估计框架：生成2.5维地形图并提取支撑平面参数，集成到接触和状态估计中；2) 将估计框架集成到安全临界控制管道：通过控制屏障函数（CBF）提供严格的安全保证，利用历史地形信息和当前本体感知测量确保全局和局部安全

Result: 地形估计方法提供平滑地形表示；耦合的地形-状态-接触估计框架相比解耦框架：基座位置估计的平均绝对误差降低64.8%，估计方差减少47.2%，接触估计鲁棒性提高；地形感知CBF仅依赖本体感知确保机器人避开危险区域并防止机体-地形碰撞

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种完整的基于本体感知的四足机器人安全运动解决方案，通过耦合的地形-状态-接触估计框架和地形感知控制屏障函数，为低成本机器人提供了在不平坦地形上安全行走的理论保证和实际验证

Abstract: Achieving safe quadrupedal locomotion in real-world environments has attracted much attention in recent years. When walking over uneven terrain, achieving reliable estimation and realising safety-critical control based on the obtained information is still an open question. To address this challenge, especially for low-cost robots equipped solely with proprioceptive sensors (e.g., IMUs, joint encoders, and contact force sensors), this work first presents an estimation framework that generates a 2.5-D terrain map and extracts support plane parameters, which are then integrated into contact and state estimation. Then, we integrate this estimation framework into a safety-critical control pipeline by formulating control barrier functions that provide rigorous safety guarantees. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed terrain estimation method provides smooth terrain representations. Moreover, the coupled estimation framework of terrain, state, and contact reduces the mean absolute error of base position estimation by 64.8%, decreases the estimation variance by 47.2%, and improves the robustness of contact estimation compared to a decoupled framework. The terrain-informed CBFs integrate historical terrain information and current proprioceptive measurements to ensure global safety by keeping the robot out of hazardous areas and local safety by preventing body-terrain collision, relying solely on proprioceptive sensing.

</details>


### [46] [A Generalized Voronoi Graph based Coverage Control Approach for Non-Convex Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09596)
*Zuyi Guo,Ronghao Zheng,Meiqin Liu,Senlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于广义Voronoi图的多机器人覆盖控制方法，通过负载均衡算法和协作覆盖两阶段实现非凸区域高效覆盖


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多机器人系统在包含多个障碍物的非凸区域中实现高效覆盖的挑战

Method: 基于广义Voronoi图的两阶段方法：1)负载均衡算法阶段，将非凸区域划分为多个子区域，考虑子区域质量差异，通过迭代优化机器人分配比例实现负载均衡；2)协作覆盖阶段，每个机器人采用新控制器有效覆盖区域

Result: 证明了方法的收敛性，并通过仿真评估了性能

Conclusion: 提出的基于GVG的覆盖控制方法能够有效解决多机器人在非凸区域中的覆盖问题，实现负载均衡和高效协作覆盖

Abstract: To address the challenge of efficient coverage by multi-robot systems in non-convex regions with multiple obstacles, this paper proposes a coverage control method based on the Generalized Voronoi Graph (GVG), which has two phases: Load-Balancing Algorithm phase and Collaborative Coverage phase. In Load-Balancing Algorithm phase, the non-convex region is partitioned into multiple sub-regions based on GVG. Besides, a weighted load-balancing algorithm is developed, which considers the quality differences among sub-regions. By iteratively optimizing the robot allocation ratio, the number of robots in each sub-region is matched with the sub-region quality to achieve load balance. In Collaborative Coverage phase, each robot is controlled by a new controller to effectively coverage the region. The convergence of the method is proved and its performance is evaluated through simulations.

</details>


### [47] [DRIFT: Dual-Representation Inter-Fusion Transformer for Automated Driving Perception with 4D Radar Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09695)
*Siqi Pei,Andras Palffy,Dariu M. Gavrila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRIFT：一种用于4D雷达点云的双路径架构，通过融合局部和全局上下文信息提升目标检测和道路估计性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D雷达相比激光雷达点云密度显著降低，需要充分利用局部和全局上下文场景信息来弥补这一缺陷

Method: 提出双路径架构：点路径聚合细粒度局部特征，柱路径编码粗粒度全局特征，通过多阶段特征共享层交织两种表示

Result: 在View-of-Delft数据集上达到52.6% mAP（优于CenterPoint的45.4%），在专有内部数据集上也优于基线方法

Conclusion: DRIFT通过有效融合局部和全局上下文，显著提升了4D雷达在目标检测和道路估计任务上的性能

Abstract: 4D radars, which provide 3D point cloud data along with Doppler velocity, are attractive components of modern automated driving systems due to their low cost and robustness under adverse weather conditions. However, they provide a significantly lower point cloud density than LiDAR sensors. This makes it important to exploit not only local but also global contextual scene information. This paper proposes DRIFT, a model that effectively captures and fuses both local and global contexts through a dual-path architecture. The model incorporates a point path to aggregate fine-grained local features and a pillar path to encode coarse-grained global features. These two parallel paths are intertwined via novel feature-sharing layers at multiple stages, enabling full utilization of both representations. DRIFT is evaluated on the widely used View-of-Delft (VoD) dataset and a proprietary internal dataset. It outperforms the baselines on the tasks of object detection and/or free road estimation. For example, DRIFT achieves a mean average precision (mAP) of 52.6\% (compared to, say, 45.4\% of CenterPoint) on the VoD dataset.

</details>


### [48] [Robotic Scene Cloning:Advancing Zero-Shot Robotic Scene Adaptation in Manipulation via Visual Prompt Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09712)
*Binyuan Huang,Yuqing Wen,Yucheng Zhao,Yaosi Hu,Tiancai Wang,Chang Wen Chen,Haoqiang Fan,Zhenzhong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Robotic Scene Cloning (RSC)方法，通过编辑现有机器人操作轨迹实现场景特定适应，解决预训练模型在真实用户场景中零样本能力有限的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人模型在真实用户场景中部署困难，零样本能力有限，通常需要大量现场数据收集。需要一种方法能够实现场景特定的适应，而不需要大量新数据

Method: 提出Robotic Scene Cloning (RSC)方法，通过视觉提示机制和精心调整的条件注入模块编辑现有机器人操作轨迹，实现准确且场景一致的样本生成。不仅能转移纹理，还能根据视觉提示进行适度的形状适应

Result: RSC在各种物体类型上表现出可靠的任务性能。在多种模拟和真实环境中的实验表明，RSC显著增强了目标环境中的策略泛化能力

Conclusion: RSC方法通过编辑现有轨迹实现场景特定适应，有效解决了预训练机器人模型在真实场景部署中的零样本能力限制问题，显著提升了策略泛化性能

Abstract: Modern robots can perform a wide range of simple tasks and adapt to diverse scenarios in the well-trained environment. However, deploying pre-trained robot models in real-world user scenarios remains challenging due to their limited zero-shot capabilities, often necessitating extensive on-site data collection. To address this issue, we propose Robotic Scene Cloning (RSC), a novel method designed for scene-specific adaptation by editing existing robot operation trajectories. RSC achieves accurate and scene-consistent sample generation by leveraging a visual prompting mechanism and a carefully tuned condition injection module. Not only transferring textures but also performing moderate shape adaptations in response to the visual prompts, RSC demonstrates reliable task performance across a variety of object types. Experiments across various simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that RSC significantly enhances policy generalization in target environments.

</details>


### [49] [Let's Reward Step-by-Step: Step-Aware Contrastive Alignment for Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09740)
*Haoyuan Li,Rui Liu,Hehe Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SACA框架通过感知基础的步感知审计器从失败轨迹中提取密集监督，解决VLN-CE中强化微调稀疏奖励和模仿学习复合误差的问题，在VLN-CE基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLN-CE训练范式难以平衡泛化能力、错误恢复和训练稳定性。SFT方法存在复合误差问题，难以从分布外状态恢复；RFT方法（如GRPO）受限于稀疏结果奖励，二元反馈无法为单个步骤分配信用，导致失败主导批次中的梯度信号崩溃。

Method: 提出Step-Aware Contrastive Alignment (SACA)框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Perception-Grounded Step-Aware审计器，逐步评估进展，将失败轨迹解耦为有效前缀和精确分歧点；2) Scenario-Conditioned Group Construction机制，根据审计信号动态路由批次到专门的重新采样和优化策略。

Result: 在VLN-CE基准上的大量实验表明，SACA实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SACA框架通过从非完美轨迹中提取密集监督，有效解决了VLN-CE中强化微调的稀疏奖励问题和模仿学习的复合误差问题，显著提升了导航性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to learn complex reasoning from long-horizon human interactions. While Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have driven recent progress, current training paradigms struggle to balance generalization capability, error recovery and training stability. Specifically, (i) policies derived from SFT suffer from compounding errors, struggling to recover from out-of-distribution states, and (ii) Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) methods e.g. GRPO are bottlenecked by sparse outcome rewards. Their binary feedback fails to assign credit to individual steps, leading to gradient signal collapse in failure dominant batches. To address these challenges, we introduce Step-Aware Contrastive Alignment (SACA), a framework designed to extract dense supervision from imperfect trajectories. At its core, the Perception-Grounded Step-Aware auditor evaluates progress step-by-step, disentangling failed trajectories into valid prefixes and exact divergence points. Leveraging these signals, Scenario-Conditioned Group Construction mechanism dynamically routes batches to specialized resampling and optimization strategies. Extensive experiments on VLN-CE benchmarks demonstrate that SACA achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [50] [Caterpillar-Inspired Spring-Based Compressive Continuum Robot for Bristle-based Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09745)
*Zhixian Hu,Yu She,Juan Wachs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于弹簧的肌腱驱动连续体机器人，用于商业机械臂的受限空间检测，结合弯曲和轴向长度变化，集成人工鬃毛接触传感器实现表面感知


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性机器人在管道、风道等受限空间探索中面临空间限制、几何不规则和访问受限等挑战，需要更灵活、紧凑的解决方案

Method: 设计紧凑的弹簧基肌腱驱动连续体机器人，集成到商业机械臂上；采用机械柔顺的连续体结构配合肌腱驱动模块，实现弯曲和轴向长度变化的耦合运动；使用恒定曲率运动学模型进行位置控制；集成人工鬃毛接触传感器实现表面感知

Result: 在提出的模型和控制管道下，平均位置误差为4.32毫米；成功展示了通过接触交互实现表面感知和受限空间探索的能力

Conclusion: 这种紧凑柔顺的设计为商业机器人提供了经济有效的升级方案，有望在挑战性环境中实现有效的探索

Abstract: Exploration of confined spaces, such as pipelines and ducts, remains challenging for conventional rigid robots due to limited space, irregular geometry, and restricted access. Inspired by caterpillar locomotion and sensing, this paper presents a compact spring-based tendon-driven continuum robot that integrates with commercial robotic arms for confined-space inspection. The system combines a mechanically compliant continuum body with a tendon actuation module, enabling coupled bending and axial length change, and uses a constant-curvature kinematic model for positional control. Experiments show a mean position error of 4.32 mm under the proposed model and control pipeline. To extend the system from motion to inspection, we integrate an artificial bristle contact sensor and demonstrate surface perception and confined-space exploration through contact interactions. This compact and compliant design offers a cost-effective upgrade for commercial robots and promises effective exploration in challenging environments.

</details>


### [51] [TIMID: Time-Dependent Mistake Detection in Videos of Robot Executions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09782)
*Nerea Gallego,Fernando Salanova,Claudio Mannarano,Cristian Mahulea,Eduardo Montijano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TIMID：一种新的视频异常检测架构，专门用于检测机器人执行高层任务时的时间依赖性错误，通过弱监督训练和模拟到真实零样本评估实现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测框架主要关注单一、低级的运动学或动作失败，难以识别更复杂的时空任务违规，因为这些违规不一定表现为低级执行错误。需要专门检测机器人时间依赖性错误的方法

Method: 提出TIMID架构，接收视频、任务提示和潜在错误提示作为输入，输出帧级预测。采用VAD公式实现弱监督训练（每视频仅需单个标签）。引入多机器人模拟数据集，包含受控时间错误和真实执行数据，用于零样本模拟到真实评估

Result: 实验表明，现成的视觉语言模型缺乏此任务所需的显式时间推理能力，而TIMID框架成功检测了不同类型的时间错误

Conclusion: TIMID能够有效检测机器人执行高层任务时的时间依赖性错误，通过弱监督训练和模拟数据解决了数据稀缺问题，在模拟到真实场景中表现出色

Abstract: As robotic systems execute increasingly difficult task sequences, so does the number of ways in which they can fail. Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) frameworks typically focus on singular, low-level kinematic or action failures, struggling to identify more complex temporal or spatial task violations, because they do not necessarily manifest as low-level execution errors. To address this problem, the main contribution of this paper is a new VAD-inspired architecture, TIMID, which is able to detect robot time-dependent mistakes when executing high-level tasks. Our architecture receives as inputs a video and prompts of the task and the potential mistake, and returns a frame-level prediction in the video of whether the mistake is present or not. By adopting a VAD formulation, the model can be trained with weak supervision, requiring only a single label per video. Additionally, to alleviate the problem of data scarcity of incorrect executions, we introduce a multi-robot simulation dataset with controlled temporal errors and real executions for zero-shot sim-to-real evaluation. Our experiments demonstrate that out-of-the-box VLMs lack the explicit temporal reasoning required for this task, whereas our framework successfully detects different types of temporal errors. Project: https://ropertunizar.github.io/TIMID/

</details>


### [52] [Emerging Extrinsic Dexterity in Cluttered Scenes via Dynamics-aware Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09882)
*Yixin Zheng,Jiangran Lyu,Yifan Zhang,Jiayi Chen,Mi Yan,Yuntian Deng,Xuesong Shi,Xiaoguang Zhao,Yizhou Wang,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DAPL框架，通过显式世界建模学习接触诱导物体动力学表示，用于强化学习条件化，实现杂乱环境中非抓取操作的外在灵巧性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对杂乱环境中多物体交互复杂动力学的显式建模，限制了非抓取操作在实际环境中的应用

Method: DAPL框架：通过显式世界建模学习接触诱导物体动力学表示，将该表示作为强化学习的条件输入，无需手工接触启发式或复杂奖励塑形

Result: 在未见过的模拟杂乱场景中，成功率比抓取操作、人工遥操作和先前基于表示的方法高出25%以上；真实世界10个杂乱场景中成功率约50%，杂货部署展示了稳健的模拟到现实迁移

Conclusion: DAPL框架通过显式建模接触动力学，实现了杂乱环境中非抓取操作的外在灵巧性，展示了实际应用的潜力

Abstract: Extrinsic dexterity leverages environmental contact to overcome the limitations of prehensile manipulation. However, achieving such dexterity in cluttered scenes remains challenging and underexplored, as it requires selectively exploiting contact among multiple interacting objects with inherently coupled dynamics. Existing approaches lack explicit modeling of such complex dynamics and therefore fall short in non-prehensile manipulation in cluttered environments, which in turn limits their practical applicability in real-world environments. In this paper, we introduce a Dynamics-Aware Policy Learning (DAPL) framework that can facilitate policy learning with a learned representation of contact-induced object dynamics in cluttered environments. This representation is learned through explicit world modeling and used to condition reinforcement learning, enabling extrinsic dexterity to emerge without hand-crafted contact heuristics or complex reward shaping. We evaluate our approach in both simulation and the real world. Our method outperforms prehensile manipulation, human teleoperation, and prior representation-based policies by over 25% in success rate on unseen simulated cluttered scenes with varying densities. The real-world success rate reaches around 50% across 10 cluttered scenes, while a practical grocery deployment further demonstrates robust sim-to-real transfer and applicability.

</details>


### [53] [Robust Cooperative Localization in Featureless Environments: A Comparative Study of DCL, StCL, CCL, CI, and Standard-CL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09886)
*Nivand Khosravi,Meysam Basiri,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对五种协同定位方法进行了比较研究，揭示了在GPS拒止环境中多机器人系统定位算法的性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS拒止环境中，多机器人系统需要准确的协同定位方法。现有多种CL方法各有特点，但缺乏系统性的比较分析来指导实际应用选择。

Method: 在ROS中实现了五种CL方法：集中式协同定位(CCL)、分布式协同定位(DCL)、顺序协同定位(StCL)、协方差交集(CI)和标准协同定位(Standard-CL)。通过蒙特卡洛模拟在两种条件下评估：弱数据关联和鲁棒检测。

Result: StCL和Standard-CL定位误差最低但滤波器不一致性严重；DCL在挑战条件下稳定性最好；CI在一致性和准确性间达到最佳平衡；CCL理论上最优但对异常值敏感。

Conclusion: 不同CL方法存在基本权衡：StCL和Standard-CL不适合安全关键应用；DCL适合挑战环境；CI是最平衡的方法；CCL适合理论最优但需要异常值处理。研究为基于应用需求选择CL算法提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Cooperative localization (CL) enables accurate position estimation in multi-robot systems operating in GPS-denied environments. This paper presents a comparative study of five CL approaches: Centralized Cooperative Localization (CCL), Decentralized Cooperative Localization (DCL), Sequential Cooperative Localization (StCL), Covariance Intersection (CI), and Standard Cooperative Localization (Standard-CL). All methods are implemented in ROS and evaluated through Monte Carlo simulations under two conditions: weak data association and robust detection. Our analysis reveals fundamental trade-offs among the methods. StCL and Standard-CL achieve the lowest position errors but exhibit severe filter inconsistency, making them unsuitable for safety-critical applications. DCL demonstrates remarkable stability under challenging conditions due to its measurement stride mechanism, which provides implicit regularization against outliers. CI emerges as the most balanced approach, achieving near-optimal consistency while maintaining competitive accuracy. CCL provides theoretically optimal estimation but shows sensitivity to measurement outliers. These findings offer practical guidance for selecting CL algorithms based on application requirements.

</details>


### [54] [Kinodynamic Motion Retargeting for Humanoid Locomotion via Multi-Contact Whole-Body Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09956)
*Xiaoyu Zhang,Steven Haener,Varun Madabushi,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KDMR框架通过多接触全身轨迹优化，结合运动捕捉和地面反作用力数据，解决传统运动重定向中的物理不一致问题，提升下游模仿学习策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于运动学的重定向方法仅依赖空间运动捕捉数据，不可避免地引入物理不一致的伪影（如脚部滑动和地面穿透），严重降低下游模仿学习策略的性能。需要将刚体动力学和接触互补约束纳入重定向过程。

Method: KDMR将重定向过程建模为多接触全身轨迹优化问题，明确强制执行刚体动力学和接触互补约束。通过整合地面反作用力测量和运动捕捉数据，自动检测脚跟-脚趾接触事件，准确复制复杂的人类接触模式。

Result: KDMR在三个关键维度上显著优于最先进的基线GMR：1）重定向运动的动态可行性和平滑性；2）与原始源数据相比的地面反作用力跟踪准确性；3）通过BeyondMimic框架训练的下游控制策略的训练效率和最终性能。实验结果表明KDMR产生动态可行的参考轨迹，加速策略收敛并增强整体运动稳定性。

Conclusion: KDMR框架通过整合动力学约束和接触力测量，解决了传统运动重定向的物理不一致问题，为下游模仿学习提供了高质量的参考轨迹，显著提升了策略性能和训练效率。该端到端管道将在发表后开源。

Abstract: We present the KinoDynamic Motion Retargeting (KDMR) framework, a novel approach for humanoid locomotion that models the retargeting process as a multi-contact, whole-body trajectory optimization problem. Conventional kinematics-based retargeting methods rely solely on spatial motion capture (MoCap) data, inevitably introducing physically inconsistent artifacts, such as foot sliding and ground penetration, that severely degrade the performance of downstream imitation learning policies. To bridge this gap, KDMR extends beyond pure kinematics by explicitly enforcing rigid-body dynamics and contact complementarity constraints. Further, by integrating ground reaction force (GRF) measurements alongside MoCap data, our method automatically detects heel-toe contact events to accurately replicate complex human-like contact patterns. We evaluate KDMR against the state-of-the-art baseline, GMR, across three key dimensions: 1) the dynamic feasibility and smoothness of the retargeted motions, 2) the accuracy of GRF tracking compared to raw source data, and 3) the training efficiency and final performance of downstream control policies trained via the BeyondMimic framework. Experimental results demonstrate that KDMR significantly outperforms purely kinematic methods, yielding dynamically viable reference trajectories that accelerate policy convergence and enhance overall locomotion stability. Our end-to-end pipeline will be open-sourced upon publication.

</details>


### [55] [BEACON: Language-Conditioned Navigation Affordance Prediction under Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09961)
*Xinyu Gao,Gang Chen,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BEACON：一种基于鸟瞰图的语言条件局部导航方法，通过预测包含遮挡区域的BEV可通行热图，解决现有图像空间方法在遮挡区域目标定位的困难


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的空间定位方法通常在图像空间进行推理，产生与可见像素绑定的2D预测，难以推断被家具或移动人类遮挡区域的目标位置

Method: 提出BEACON方法，预测包含遮挡区域的局部鸟瞰图可通行热图。给定指令和机器人周围四个方向的RGB-D观测，通过向VLM注入空间线索并将VLM输出与深度导出的BEV特征融合来预测BEV热图

Result: 在Habitat模拟器中构建的遮挡感知数据集上进行实验，BEACON在验证子集上（包含遮挡目标位置）相比最先进的图像空间基线，在测地线阈值平均准确率上提高了22.74个百分点

Conclusion: BEACON通过BEV空间表示有效解决了语言条件局部导航中的遮挡问题，显著提升了在遮挡区域的目标定位性能

Abstract: Language-conditioned local navigation requires a robot to infer a nearby traversable target location from its current observation and an open-vocabulary, relational instruction. Existing vision-language spatial grounding methods usually rely on vision-language models (VLMs) to reason in image space, producing 2D predictions tied to visible pixels. As a result, they struggle to infer target locations in occluded regions, typically caused by furniture or moving humans. To address this issue, we propose BEACON, which predicts an ego-centric Bird's-Eye View (BEV) affordance heatmap over a bounded local region including occluded areas. Given an instruction and surround-view RGB-D observations from four directions around the robot, BEACON predicts the BEV heatmap by injecting spatial cues into a VLM and fusing the VLM's output with depth-derived BEV features. Using an occlusion-aware dataset built in the Habitat simulator, we conduct detailed experimental analysis to validate both our BEV space formulation and the design choices of each module. Our method improves the accuracy averaged across geodesic thresholds by 22.74 percentage points over the state-of-the-art image-space baseline on the validation subset with occluded target locations. Our project page is: https://xin-yu-gao.github.io/beacon.

</details>


### [56] [TiPToP: A Modular Open-Vocabulary Planning System for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09971)
*William Shen,Nishanth Kumar,Sahit Chintalapudi,Jie Wang,Christopher Watson,Edward Hu,Jing Cao,Dinesh Jayaraman,Leslie Pack Kaelbling,Tomás Lozano-Pérez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TiPToP是一个结合预训练视觉基础模型与现有任务和运动规划器的模块化系统，能够直接从RGB图像和自然语言指令解决多步操作任务，无需机器人数据，性能优于经过350小时演示微调的VLA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操作任务通常需要大量机器人特定数据或复杂的端到端学习系统。TiPToP旨在开发一个简单易用、无需机器人数据、可快速部署到新机器人平台的模块化系统，实现学习与规划的紧密结合。

Method: TiPToP采用模块化架构，将预训练的视觉基础模型与现有的任务和运动规划器(TAMP)结合。系统从输入RGB图像和自然语言指令直接生成多步操作计划，可在标准DROID设置中一小时内安装运行，并能以最小努力适配新机器人平台。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的28个桌面操作任务评估中，TiPToP（零机器人数据）匹配或优于π₀.₅-DROID（经过350小时机器人特定演示微调的视觉-语言-动作模型）。通过对173次试验的失败模式分析，识别了组件级改进方向。

Conclusion: TiPToP展示了模块化系统在机器人操作任务中的有效性，无需大量机器人数据即可实现高性能。其开源发布将促进模块化操作系统研究以及学习与规划的更紧密集成。

Abstract: We present TiPToP, an extensible modular system that combines pretrained vision foundation models with an existing Task and Motion Planner (TAMP) to solve multi-step manipulation tasks directly from input RGB images and natural-language instructions. Our system aims to be simple and easy-to-use: it can be installed and run on a standard DROID setup in under one hour and adapted to new embodiments with minimal effort. We evaluate TiPToP -- which requires zero robot data -- over 28 tabletop manipulation tasks in simulation and the real world and find it matches or outperforms $π_{0.5}\text{-DROID}$, a vision-language-action (VLA) model fine-tuned on 350 hours of embodiment-specific demonstrations. TiPToP's modular architecture enables us to analyze the system's failure modes at the component level. We analyze results from an evaluation of 173 trials and identify directions for improvement. We release TiPToP open-source to further research on modular manipulation systems and tighter integration between learning and planning. Project website and code: https://tiptop-robot.github.io

</details>

### [57] [MM-tau-p$^2$: Persona-Adaptive Prompting for Robust Multi-Modal Agent Evaluation in Dual-Control Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09643)
*Anupam Purwar,Aditya Choudhary*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出MM-tau-p²基准测试，用于评估多模态智能体在双重控制设置下的鲁棒性，包含用户角色适应和用户参与规划过程，引入12个新指标来衡量多模态鲁棒性和轮次开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估框架主要针对文本聊天驱动，且不暴露用户角色，而客户体验管理领域需要智能体根据用户个性调整行为。随着实时TTS和多模态语言模型的发展，LLM智能体正逐渐多模态化，需要新的评估框架。

Method: 提出MM-tau-p²基准测试，在双重控制设置下评估多模态智能体，包含有无用户角色适应的情况，并让用户参与查询解决规划过程。使用LLM-as-judge方法，通过精心设计的提示和评分标准，在电信和零售领域估计12个新指标。

Result: 研究表明，即使使用GPT-5、GPT-4.1等前沿LLM，在多模态智能体中仍需考虑多模态鲁棒性和轮次开销等额外因素。基准测试在电信和零售领域提供了指标估计。

Conclusion: MM-tau-p²基准测试基于先前FOCAL工作，通过引入12个新指标，为多模态智能体评估提供了全面的自动化方法，填补了现有评估框架在多模态和用户角色适应方面的空白。

Abstract: Current evaluation frameworks and benchmarks for LLM powered agents focus on text chat driven agents, these frameworks do not expose the persona of user to the agent, thus operating in a user agnostic environment. Importantly, in customer experience management domain, the agent's behaviour evolves as the agent learns about user personality. With proliferation of real time TTS and multi-modal language models, LLM based agents are gradually going to become multi-modal. Towards this, we propose the MM-tau-p$^2$ benchmark with metrics for evaluating the robustness of multi-modal agents in dual control setting with and without persona adaption of user, while also taking user inputs in the planning process to resolve a user query. In particular, our work shows that even with state of-the-art frontier LLMs like GPT-5, GPT 4.1, there are additional considerations measured using metrics viz. multi-modal robustness, turn overhead while introducing multi-modality into LLM based agents. Overall, MM-tau-p$^2$ builds on our prior work FOCAL and provides a holistic way of evaluating multi-modal agents in an automated way by introducing 12 novel metrics. We also provide estimates of these metrics on the telecom and retail domains by using the LLM-as-judge approach using carefully crafted prompts with well defined rubrics for evaluating each conversation.

</details>


### [58] [WVA: A Global Optimization Control Plane for llmd](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09730)
*Abhishek Malvankar,Lionel Villard,Mohammed Abdi,Evgeny Shindin,Braulio Dumba,Vishakha Ramani,Asser Tantawi,Tamar Eilam*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: WVA（Workload Variant Autoscaler）是一种专为大规模LLM推理设计的控制平面，通过结合推理服务器内部饱和状态进行智能扩缩容，相比传统Kubernetes HPA实现了37%的有效吞吐提升和10倍的请求失败减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM处理大规模并发流量，优化推理基础设施成为主要挑战。传统资源中心化的自动扩缩器（如Kubernetes HPA）不考虑应用特定的SLO、硬件异构性或内部引擎状态（如KV缓存利用率），导致不必要的扩缩、严重的资源利用不足和状态推理中断。

Method: WVA与llmd协同设计，将扩缩决策与推理服务器内部饱和状态紧密耦合。采用基于前瞻性余量的扩缩和碎片感知的缩容策略，通过成本感知的分层机制优先使用低成本、高能效的硬件变体。

Result: 实验表明，WVA相比HPA实现了37%的有效吞吐提升和10倍的请求失败减少。其成本感知分层机制通过优先使用低成本硬件变体而非同质化扩展高端加速器，固有地降低了整体功耗。

Conclusion: WVA通过紧密耦合扩缩决策与推理服务器内部状态，有效解决了传统自动扩缩器在LLM推理场景中的局限性，显著提升了吞吐性能、降低了请求失败率，并实现了更好的成本效益和能效优化。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) scale to handle massive concurrent traffic, optimizing the infrastructure required for inference has become a primary challenge. To manage the high cost of GPU resources while ensuring strict service-level objectives (SLOs), operators increasingly deploy models across heterogeneous hardware clusters that multiplex latency-sensitive online requests and throughput-oriented offline requests. However, traditional resource-centric autoscalers like the Kubernetes horizontal pod autoscaler (HPA) do not consider application-specific SLOs, hardware heterogeneity, or internal engine state (like KV cache utilization) globally. This leads to unnecessary scaling, severe resource underutilization, and disrupted stateful inference. To address these limitations, we introduce the Workload Variant Autoscaler (WVA), a specialized control plane co-designed with \texttt{llmd} that tightly couples scaling decisions with the inference server's internal saturation state. By utilizing proactive headroom-based scaling and fragmentation-aware scale-down, our experiments demonstrate that WVA achieves a \textbf{37\% improvement in effective throughput} and a \textbf{10x reduction in request failures} compared to HPA. Furthermore, WVA's cost-aware tiering intrinsically reduces overall power consumption by prioritizing lower-cost, energy-efficient hardware variants over homogeneous scaling on high-end accelerators.

</details>


### [59] [Trade-Offs in FMCW Radar-Based Respiration and Heart Rate Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09791)
*Silvia Mura,Davide Scazzoli,Lorenzo Fineschi,Maurizio Magarini*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 低成本FMCW MIMO雷达用于非接触式生命体征监测的实验评估，量化了传感距离和发射啁啾数量对呼吸率和心率估计精度的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估低成本FMCW MIMO雷达在非接触式生命体征监测中的性能，特别关注呼吸率和心率估计的准确性，并量化关键参数（距离和啁啾数量）对测量精度的影响

Method: 使用低成本FMCW MIMO雷达进行实验评估，系统量化传感距离和发射啁啾数量对测量精度的影响，分析不同距离下的误差分布

Result: 结果显示U形误差分布，在70cm处性能最优，呼吸率平均绝对误差0.8bpm，心率3.2bpm；短距离（<60cm）和长距离（>100cm）精度下降；增加啁啾数量可提升性能，呼吸率需要≥96个啁啾，心率至少需要96个啁啾；心率变异性和呼吸率变异性误差较大（>15-30%）

Conclusion: 该雷达能够稳健估计平均呼吸率和心率，但在高分辨率的心跳间和呼吸间瞬时波动监测方面精度有限，揭示了平均估计精度与瞬时波动监测能力之间的基本权衡

Abstract: This study presents a comprehensive experimental assessment of a low-cost frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) radar for non-contact vital sign monitoring, focusing on respiratory rate (RR) and heart rate (HR) estimation. The influence of sensing distance and number of transmitted chirps on measurement accuracy is systematically quantified. Results exhibit a U-shaped error profile with optimal performance near $70~cm$, achieving mean absolute errors of $0.8~bpm$ for RR and $3.2~bpm$ for HR. Accuracy deteriorates at short ($<60~cm$) and long ($>100~cm$) distances due to multipath, near-field, and signal-to-noise effects. Increasing chirp count enhances performance: RR errors converge asymptotically for $\geq96$ chirps, while HR requires at least 96 chirps for stable detection. Variability metrics, including heart and respiratory rate variability, remain less accurate ($>15$--$30\%$ error), indicating limited capability in capturing instantaneous fluctuations. These findings define a fundamental trade-off: the radar ensures robust estimation of average RR and HR but exhibits restricted precision in high-resolution beat-to-beat and breath-to-breath monitoring.

</details>


### [60] [Layered Dielectric Characterization of Human Skin in the Sub-Terahertz and Terahertz Frequency Ranges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09822)
*Silvia Mura,Elisabetta Marini,Maurizio Magarini,Matti Hamalainen,Marco Hernandez*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 开发了人体皮肤及其细胞成分在亚太赫兹和太赫兹频段的综合介电模型，结合多德拜弛豫理论和有效介质公式，为生物医学诊断和成像应用提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚太赫兹和太赫兹辐射因其对生物组织水含量和分子动力学的敏感性，在非侵入性诊断和成像方面具有独特优势。然而，需要建立准确的介电模型来理解和预测组织在这些频段的相互作用，以支持新一代诊断和成像技术的开发。

Method: 结合多德拜弛豫理论和有效介质公式，开发了人体皮肤及其细胞成分的综合介电模型。模型整合了细胞内水动力学和大分子弛豫过程，关键细胞参数（水含量、蛋白质和脂质分数、离子电导率等）来自实验验证的数据源。

Result: 建立了能够对不同皮肤层和细胞类型的频率依赖性介电常数进行现实预测的框架，提供了亚太赫兹和太赫兹组织相互作用的物理解释性描述。

Conclusion: 该模型为设计和优化在亚太赫兹和太赫兹频段工作的新一代诊断和成像技术奠定了基础，有助于推动这些频段在生物医学应用中的发展。

Abstract: Sub-terahertz (sub-THz) and terahertz (THz) radiation offer unique opportunities for non-invasive diagnostics and imaging due to their sensitivity to water content and molecular dynamics in biological tissues. In this work, a comprehensive dielectric model of human skin and its cellular constituents is developed across these frequency ranges. The model combines multi-Debye relaxation theory with effective medium formulations to account for intracellular water dynamics and macromolecular relaxation processes. Key cellular parameters, including water content, protein and lipid fractions, and ionic conductivity, are integrated from experimentally validated sources. The proposed framework enables realistic predictions of frequency-dependent permittivity for different skin layers and cell types, providing a physically interpretable description of sub-THz and THz tissue interactions. This approach establishes a foundation for the design and optimization of next-generation diagnostic and imaging techniques operating in these frequency bands.

</details>

### [61] [Idempotent Slices with Applications to Code-Size Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09726)
*Rafael Alvarenga de Azevedo,Daniel Augusto Costa de Sa,Rodrigo Caetano Rocha,Fernando Magno Quintão Pereira*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 该论文形式化了幂等后向切片的概念，提出了从GSA形式程序中提取幂等后向切片的正确高效算法，并展示了其在稀疏代码大小优化中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guimaraes等人之前使用的非正式幂等切片概念不足以正确应用于一般控制流图。本文旨在解决这一不足，形式化幂等后向切片概念，并提供正确高效的提取算法。

Method: 形式化幂等后向切片概念，提出从Gated Static Single Assignment（GSA）形式程序中提取幂等后向切片的算法。该算法能够正确处理一般控制流图，并支持稀疏代码大小优化。

Result: 在LLVM测试套件上的实验表明，该算法在特定基准测试中，对经过clang 17的-Os优化序列高度优化的程序，实现了高达-7.24%的代码大小减少。

Conclusion: 本文成功形式化了幂等后向切片概念，提出了正确高效的提取算法，并展示了其在稀疏代码大小优化中的实际应用价值，能够显著减少优化后程序的代码大小。

Abstract: Given a value computed within a program, an idempotent backward slice with respect to this value is a maximal subprogram that computes it. An informal notion of an idempotent slice has previously been used by Guimaraes et al. to transform eager into strict evaluation in the LLVM intermediate representation. However, that algorithm is insufficient to be correctly applied to general control-flow graphs. This paper addresses these omissions by formalizing the notion of idempotent backward slices and presenting a sound and efficient algorithm for extracting them from programs in Gated Static Single Assignment (GSA) form. As an example of their practical use, the paper describes how identifying and extracting idempotent backward slices enables a sparse code-size reduction optimization; that is, one capable of merging non-contiguous sequences of instructions within the control-flow graph of a single function or across functions. Experiments with the LLVM test suite show that, in specific benchmarks, this new algorithm achieves code-size reductions up to -7.24% on programs highly optimized by the -Os sequence of passes from clang 17.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [62] [OptBench: An Interactive Workbench for AI/ML-SQL Co-Optimization[Extended Demonstration Proposal]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08880)
*Jaykumar Tandel,Douglas Oscarson,Jia Zou*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: OptBench：一个用于构建和基准测试混合SQL+AI/ML查询优化器的交互式工作台，提供统一后端和可视化界面


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合SQL+AI/ML查询优化面临挑战：ML算子如黑盒、数据依赖效应难以预测、领域专家依赖难以集成的启发式方法、大量协同优化机会扩大搜索空间，且现有研究原型难以公平评估

Method: 开发OptBench工作台，基于DuckDB统一后端，提供交互式Web界面，支持：1）通过抽象逻辑计划重写动作构建查询优化器；2）在多样化查询套件上基准测试不同优化器实现，记录决策轨迹和延迟；3）并排可视化不同优化器生成的逻辑计划

Result: 创建了一个透明、公平比较的优化器开发和评估平台，使实践者和研究人员能够在单一工作台中原型化优化器想法、检查计划转换、定量比较多模态推理查询的优化器设计

Conclusion: OptBench解决了混合SQL+AI/ML查询优化器开发和评估的公平性、透明度问题，为研究社区提供了标准化测试平台，促进了该领域优化技术的创新和比较

Abstract: Database workloads are increasingly nesting artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) pipelines and AI/ML model inferences with data processing, yielding hybrid SQL+AI/ML queries that mix relational operators with expensive, opaque AI/ML operators, often expressed as UDFs. These workloads are challenging to optimize because ML operators behave like black boxes, data-dependent effects such as sparsity, selectivity, and cardinalities can dominate runtime, domain experts often rely on practical heuristics that are difficult to develop with monolithic optimizers, and AI/ML operators introduce numerous co-optimization opportunities such as factorization, pushdown, ML-to-SQL conversion, and linear-algebra-to-relational-algebra rewrites, significantly enlarging the search space of equivalent execution plans. At the same time, research prototypes for SQL+ML optimization are difficult to evaluate fairly because they are typically developed on different platforms and evaluated using different queries.
  We present OptBench, an interactive workbench for building and benchmarking query optimizers for hybrid SQL+AI/ML queries in a transparent, apples-to-apples manner. OptBench runs all optimizers on a unified backend using DuckDB and exposes an interactive web interface that allows users to (i) construct query optimizers by leveraging and extending abstracted logical plan rewrite actions, (ii) benchmark and compare different optimizer implementations over a suite of diverse queries while recording decision traces and latency, and (iii) visualize logical plans produced by different optimizers side-by-side. The system enables practitioners and researchers to prototype optimizer ideas, inspect plan transformations, and quantitatively compare optimizer designs on multimodal inference queries within a single workbench.

</details>


### [63] [Evaluating the Practical Effectiveness of LLM-Driven Index Tuning with Microsoft Database Tuning Advisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09181)
*Xiaoying Wang,Wentao Wu,Vivek Narasayya,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LLM驱动的索引调优在工业基准和真实企业工作负载中表现出潜力，能识别出优于DTA的配置，但存在性能方差大、集成困难和高验证成本等挑战，可作为DTA的补充技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统索引调优工具（如SQL Server的DTA）依赖查询优化器的"what-if" API进行成本估算，当估算不准确时会导致次优推荐。LLM从大规模训练数据中学习知识，为索引调优提供了新方法，但其实际效果尤其是相对于商业调优器的优势尚不明确。

Method: 使用工业基准和真实企业客户工作负载，评估LLM驱动的索引调优的实际效果，并与Microsoft SQL Server的Database Tuning Advisor (DTA)进行比较。分析LLM的推理过程，并尝试将其直接集成到DTA中。

Result: DTA通常更可靠，但LLM通过少量调用就能在相当多情况下识别出执行时间显著优于DTA的配置。LLM的推理能捕捉人类直觉的洞察，这些洞察可能被提炼用于改进DTA。然而，LLM驱动的索引调优存在显著的性能方差，直接集成到DTA中效果有限甚至负面，且性能验证成本高昂。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的索引调优具有作为DTA补充技术的潜力，但在生产环境中采用仍面临挑战。LLM的推理洞察可能有助于改进传统调优器。该研究为学术界和工业界未来在LLM驱动的索引调优方面的工作提供了动机、经验和实践见解。

Abstract: Index tuning is critical for the performance of modern database systems. Industrial index tuners, such as the Database Tuning Advisor (DTA) developed for Microsoft SQL Server, rely on the "what-if" API provided by the query optimizer to estimate the cost of a query given an index configuration, which can lead to suboptimal recommendations when the estimations are inaccurate. Large language model (LLM) offers a new approach to index tuning, with knowledge learned from web-scale training datasets. However, the effectiveness of LLM-driven index tuning, especially beyond what is already achieved by commercial index tuners, remains unclear.
  In this paper, we study the practical effectiveness of LLM-driven index tuning using both industrial benchmarks and real-world enterprise customer workloads, and compare it with DTA. Our results show that although DTA is generally more reliable, with a few invocations, LLM can identify configurations that significantly outperform those found by DTA in execution time in a considerable number of cases, highlighting its potential as a complementary technique. We also observe that LLM's reasoning captures human-intuitive insights that may be distilled to potentially improve DTA. However, adopting LLM-driven index tuning in production remains challenging due to its substantial performance variance, limited and often negative impact when directly integrated into DTA, and the high cost of performance validation. This work provides motivation, lessons, and practical insights that will inspire future work on LLM-driven index tuning both in academia and industry.

</details>


### [64] [The Virtuous Cycle: AI-Powered Vector Search and Vector Search-Augmented AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09347)
*Jiuqi Wei,Quanqing Xu,Chuanhui Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该教程综述了AI与向量搜索相互促进的良性循环：AI通过智能索引、自适应剪枝等技术提升向量搜索性能，而向量搜索通过RAG等技术增强AI能力，形成协同优化的研究前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与向量搜索的快速融合形成了一个有前景的研究前沿。一方面，AI进展显著提升了向量搜索的语义准确性和效率；另一方面，强大的向量搜索技术赋能了新的AI范式，特别是检索增强生成（RAG），有效缓解了LLM的知识过时和幻觉问题。这种相互促进形成了良性循环，需要系统性地梳理这一交叉领域的研究进展。

Method: 采用教程综述的方法，首先讨论向量搜索与AI整合的基础背景和动机，然后分别探讨AI如何赋能向量搜索（AI4VS）以及向量搜索如何赋能AI（VS4AI），特别关注RAG框架，接着分析端到端协同优化策略，最后指出关键挑战和未来研究方向。

Result: 该教程在ICDE 2026发表，系统性地梳理了AI与向量搜索交叉领域的研究现状，包括：AI在向量搜索管道各步骤中的赋能作用（学习索引、自适应剪枝、自动参数调优等），向量搜索通过RAG等技术对AI的增强作用，以及两者协同优化的策略框架。

Conclusion: AI与向量搜索的融合形成了相互促进的良性循环，这一交叉领域具有重要研究价值。未来需要进一步探索端到端协同优化、解决关键挑战，以充分释放AI与向量搜索结合的潜力，推动智能信息系统的创新发展。

Abstract: Modern AI and vector search are rapidly converging, forming a promising research frontier in intelligent information systems. On one hand, advances in AI have substantially improved the semantic accuracy and efficiency of vector search, including learned indexing structures, adaptive pruning strategies, and automated parameter tuning. On the other hand, powerful vector search techniques have enabled new AI paradigms, notably Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), which effectively mitigates challenges in Large Language Models (LLMs) like knowledge staleness and hallucinations. This mutual reinforcement establishes a virtuous cycle where AI injects intelligence and adaptive optimization into vector search, while vector search, in turn, expands AI's capabilities in knowledge integration and context-aware generation. This tutorial provides a comprehensive overview of recent research and advancements at this intersection. We begin by discussing the foundational background and motivations for integrating vector search and AI. Subsequently, we explore how AI empowers vector search (AI4VS) across each step of the vector search pipeline. We then investigate how vector search empowers AI (VS4AI), with a particular focus on RAG frameworks that integrate dynamic, external knowledge sources into the generative process of LLMs. Furthermore, we analyze end-to-end co-optimization strategies that fully unlock the potential of the ``virtuous cycle" between vector search and AI. Finally, we highlight key challenges and future research opportunities in this emerging area. This paper was published in ICDE 2026.

</details>


### [65] [GeoBenchr: An Application-Centric Benchmarking Suite for Spatiotemporal Database Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09398)
*Tim C. Rese,Nils Japke,Diana Baumann,Natalie Carl,David Bermbach*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: GeoBenchr是一个开源、以应用为中心的时空平台基准测试套件，用于全面评估不同时空数据库系统在真实场景下的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空数据库系统（如PostGIS、SpaceTime、MobilityDB）在功能和性能上差异较大，且缺乏全面的应用中心化基准测试套件来评估这些系统在真实场景下的适用性。

Method: 提出GeoBenchr基准测试套件，支持多样化数据集、查询类型和工作负载模式，涵盖骑行、航空、海事跟踪等真实应用领域，通过原型系统评估可扩展性、配置影响和跨平台性能比较。

Result: GeoBenchr能够全面评估时空数据库系统的性能，结果显示应用中心化基准测试对于为实际场景选择合适的时空数据库系统至关重要。

Conclusion: 应用中心化基准测试是评估和选择时空数据库系统的关键工具，GeoBenchr为这一需求提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of spatiotemporal data volumes needs to be handled by database systems capable of efficiently managing and querying such data. Existing systems such as PostGIS, SpaceTime, and MobilityDB offer partial solutions but differ widely in scope and performance. Also, first spatiotemporal benchmarks provide valuable insights but are limited in scope and, to our knowledge, no application-centric benchmarking suite exists. In this paper, we propose GeoBenchr, an open-source, application-centric benchmarking suite for spatiotemporal platforms. GeoBenchr enables comprehensive evaluation across diverse datasets, query types, and workload patterns, reflecting realistic use cases from domains such as cycling, aviation, and maritime tracking. We use our GeoBenchr prototype to evaluate several system aspects including scalability, configuration impact, and cross-platform performance comparison. Our results highlight the importance of application-centric benchmarking in selecting suitable spatiotemporal database systems for real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [66] [No Cliques Allowed: The Next Step Towards BDD/FC Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09558)
*Lucas Larroque,Piotr Ostropolski-Nalewaja,Michaël Thomazo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文证明了有界推导深度规则集生成的通用模型不能包含任意大的锦标赛（有向团），除非蕴含循环查询∃x E(x,x)，这缩小了(bdd⇒fc)猜想潜在反例的空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决存在规则领域的一个基本开放问题：有界推导深度规则集是否具有有限可控性的猜想(bdd⇒fc)。该猜想是存在规则理论中的核心未解决问题。

Method: 通过分析有界推导深度规则集生成的通用模型的结构特性，证明这些模型不能包含任意大的锦标赛（任意大的有向团），除非蕴含循环查询∃x E(x,x)。

Result: 证明了有界推导深度规则集生成的通用模型不能包含任意大的锦标赛而不蕴含循环查询，这缩小了(bdd⇒fc)猜想潜在反例的搜索空间。

Conclusion: 该结果为(bdd⇒fc)猜想的正面解决迈出了重要一步，通过排除一类可能的反例结构，使该猜想的证明路径更加清晰。

Abstract: This paper addresses one of the fundamental open questions in the realm of existential rules: the conjecture on the finite controllability of bounded derivation depth rule sets (bdd $\Rightarrow$ fc). We take a step toward a positive resolution of this conjecture by demonstrating that universal models generated by bdd rule sets cannot contain arbitrarily large tournaments (arbitrarily directed cliques) without entailing a loop query, $\exists{x} E(x, x)$. This simple yet elegant result narrows the space of potential counterexamples to the (bdd $\Rightarrow$ fc) conjecture.

</details>


### [67] [Local Stability of Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09724)
*Felix S. Campbell,Yuval Moskovitch*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出局部稳定性新概念，衡量排名中项目值的微小变化对其排名的影响，考虑密集区域效应，开发近似算法并验证有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有排名稳定性研究主要关注算法变化的影响，但项目值的微小变化也可能显著影响排名质量，从而影响决策可靠性。需要量化这种敏感性，特别是在存在多个相似质量项目（密集区域）的情况下。

Method: 1. 提出局部稳定性新定义，考虑项目值微小变化对排名的影响及密集区域效应；2. 证明计算该度量一般困难，提出松弛定义允许近似；3. 开发LStability采样算法，使用集中不等式提供PAC类型保证；4. 开发Detect-Dense-Region算法检测项目所在密集区域；5. 引入多种优化提高算法可扩展性和效率。

Result: 1. 理论证明局部稳定性计算在一般情况下是困难的；2. 提出的松弛定义允许有效近似；3. 开发的两个算法在实验中验证了有效性；4. 通过广泛的实验验证了框架的实用性，包括展示定义实用性的案例研究。

Conclusion: 局部稳定性为评估排名对项目值微小变化的敏感性提供了新框架，特别是在存在密集区域时。提出的算法能够有效近似该度量并检测密集区域，为排名系统的鲁棒性评估提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Rankings play a crucial role in decision-making. However, if minor changes to items significantly alter their rankings, the quality of the decisions being made can be compromised. The stability of ranking is a measure used to assess how modifications to the ranking algorithm or data affect results. While previous work has focused on stability of the ranking under changes to the algorithm, we introduce a novel measure we refer to as local stability. Local stability indicates the effect of minor changes to the values of an item in the ranking on its rank. Our proposed definition furthermore takes into account the presence of multiple items with similar qualities in the ranking, called dense regions, permitting minor modifications to swap the positions of items within the region. We show that computing this measure in general is hard, and in turn propose a relaxation of the definition to admit approximation.
  We present (i) LStability, a sampling-based algorithm for approximating local stability, on which we make probably-approximately-correct-type guarantees through the use of concentration inequalities, and (ii) Detect-Dense-Region, an algorithm based on this approach to detect the dense region an item lies in, if it exists. We introduce a number of optimizations to our algorithms to improve their scalability and efficiency. We validate our proposed framework through an extensive suite of experiments, including case studies highlighting the utility of our definitions.

</details>


### [68] [Expressive Power of Property Graph Constraint Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09806)
*Stefania Dumbrava,Nadime Francis,Victor Marsault,Steven Sailly*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究了属性图约束语言的表达能力，重点关注PG-Keys语言，通过统一框架比较PG-Keys与GFD、GGD的表达式能力，建立了完整严格的能力层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为即将修订的GQL标准提供理论依据，系统研究属性图约束语言的表达能力，特别是PG-Keys语言在现有形式化方法中的定位，解决不同形式化方法使用不同图模式语言和数据谓词导致的公平比较难题。

Method: 提出统一框架，使用合取正则路径查询（CRPQ）作为带有等式和不等式谓词的图模式，识别良好行为的片段，建立表达能力包含关系并证明分离结果。

Result: 建立了完整且严格的表达能力层次结构，精确识别了PG-Keys在哪些情况下提供严格更强的表达能力，明确了其在最先进属性图约束形式化方法中的地位。

Conclusion: 该研究为GQL标准修订提供了理论基础，通过系统比较阐明了PG-Keys相对于GFD和GGD的表达能力优势，建立了属性图约束语言的完整表达能力层次。

Abstract: We present the first principled and systematic study of the expressive power of property graph constraint languages, focused on the recent PG-Keys language, set to inform the upcoming revision of the GQL standard. To this end, we position PG-Keys within the broader landscape of existing formalisms. In particular, we compare PG-Keys with two core property graph constraint languages: Graph Functional Dependencies (GFD) and Graph Generating Dependencies (GGD). One hurdle is that these formalisms allow different kinds of graph pattern languages and data predicates. To make a fair comparison, based on their structural differences only, we first present a unifying framework. Within this framework, we consider conjunctive regular path queries (CRPQ) as graph patterns with equality and inequality predicates. We then identify well-behaved fragments, establish expressiveness inclusion, and prove separation results, yielding a complete and strict hierarchy of expressive power. The results identify precisely when PG-Keys provide strictly greater expressive power, clarifying their place among state-of-the-art property graph constraint formalisms.

</details>


### [69] [How to Write to SSDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09927)
*Bohyun Lee,Tobias Ziegler,Viktor Leis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文证明采用异地写入对充分利用SSD性能和延长SSD寿命至关重要，提出一套异地优化方案，显著降低DBMS和SSD层的写入放大


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据库系统采用原地写入方式，无法充分利用SSD性能优势，且会加速SSD磨损。需要探索异地写入架构来优化数据库系统与SSD的协同工作

Method: 重新设计基于B树的LeanStore数据库系统，从原地写入改为异地写入架构，实现一系列异地写入优化技术，减少DBMS和SSD层的写入放大

Result: 在YCSB-A基准测试中，吞吐量提升1.65-2.24倍，每个事务的闪存写入减少6.2-9.8倍；在TPC-C（15,000仓库）测试中，吞吐量提升2.45倍，闪存写入减少7.2倍；同时支持ZNS和FDP等新型SSD接口

Conclusion: 异地写入架构是数据库系统充分发挥SSD性能潜力和延长SSD寿命的关键，提出的优化方案能显著降低写入放大，提升系统性能，并兼容新兴SSD技术

Abstract: This paper demonstrates that adopting out-of-place writes is essential for database systems to fully leverage SSD performance and extend SSD lifespan. We propose a set of out-of-place optimizations that collectively reduce write amplification across both the DBMS and SSD layers. We redesign the in-place, B-tree-based LeanStore to write out-of-place and support these optimizations, and evaluate it on diverse OLTP benchmarks, dataset sizes, and SSDs. The final design improves throughput by 1.65-2.24x and reduces flash writes per transaction by 6.2-9.8x on YCSB-A. On TPC-C with 15,000 warehouses, throughput improves by 2.45x while flash writes decrease by 7.2x. Finally, we show that the architecture can seamlessly support novel SSD interfaces such as ZNS and FDP.

</details>
