### [70] [HeteroFedSyn: Differentially Private Tabular Data Synthesis for Heterogeneous Federated Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08832)
*Xiaochen Li,Fengyu Gao,Xizixiang Wei,Tianhao Wang,Cong Shen,Jing Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首个针对水平联邦场景的差分隐私表格数据合成框架，通过创新的分布式边际选择机制，在联邦设置下实现与集中式合成相当的效用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DP机制通常针对特定分析任务设计，限制了受保护数据的可重用性。DP表格数据合成通过生成可共享的合成数据集克服了这一限制，但现有方法主要假设集中式或本地设置，忽视了更实际的水平联邦场景。在本地合成数据或扰动单个记录会产生有偏的混合或引入过多噪声，特别是在参与者数据分布异构的情况下。

Method: 基于PrivSyn的2-way边际合成范式，提出HeteroFedSyn框架，包含三个关键创新：1) 基于L2的依赖度量与随机投影，用于噪声高效的相关性测量；2) 无偏估计器校正乘法噪声；3) 自适应选择策略动态更新依赖分数以避免冗余。

Result: 在范围查询、Wasserstein保真度和机器学习任务上的广泛实验表明，尽管联邦执行增加了噪声，但HeteroFedSyn实现了与集中式合成相当的效用。

Conclusion: HeteroFedSyn是首个专门为水平联邦设置设计的DP表格数据合成框架，通过创新的分布式边际选择机制，在联邦场景下实现了与集中式方法相当的效用，解决了现有方法在异构数据分布下的局限性。

Abstract: Traditional Differential Privacy (DP) mechanisms are typically tailored to specific analysis tasks, which limits the reusability of protected data. DP tabular data synthesis overcomes this by generating synthetic datasets that can be shared for arbitrary downstream tasks. However, existing synthesis methods predominantly assume centralized or local settings and overlook the more practical horizontal federated scenario. Naively synthesizing data locally or perturbing individual records either produces biased mixtures or introduces excessive noise, especially under heterogeneous data distributions across participants.
  We propose HeteroFedSyn, the first DP tabular data synthesis framework designed specifically for the horizontal federated setting. Built upon the PrivSyn paradigm of 2-way marginal-based synthesis, HeteroFedSyn introduces three key innovations for distributed marginal selection: (i) an L2-based dependency metric with random projection for noise-efficient correlation measurement, (ii) an unbiased estimator to correct multiplicative noise, and (iii) an adaptive selection strategy that dynamically updates dependency scores to avoid redundancy. Extensive experiments on range queries, Wasserstein fidelity, and machine learning tasks show that, despite the increased noise inherent to federated execution, HeteroFedSyn achieves utility comparable to centralized synthesis. Our code is open-sourced via the link.

</details>


### [71] [AgenticCyOps: Securing Multi-Agentic AI Integration in Enterprise Cyber Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09134)
*Shaswata Mitra,Raj Patel,Sudip Mittal,Md Rayhanur Rahman,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgenticCyOps框架通过系统分解多智能体系统的攻击面，重点关注工具编排和内存管理两个关键集成表面，提出五项防御原则，在SOC工作流中实现深度防御，显著减少可利用的信任边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究主要关注提示级攻击和单一攻击向量，缺乏针对企业级多智能体系统安全的整体架构模型。LLM驱动的多智能体系统虽然能实现自适应、推理驱动的工作流，但赋予智能体对工具、内存和通信的自主控制权会引入确定性管道中不存在的攻击面。

Method: 提出AgenticCyOps框架，系统分解攻击面到组件、协调和协议层，识别工具编排和内存管理为关键集成表面。基于此形式化信任边界，定义五项防御原则：授权接口、能力范围限定、验证执行、内存完整性与同步、访问控制的数据隔离。在SOC工作流中应用该框架，采用模型上下文协议作为结构基础，实现阶段限定智能体、共识验证循环和每组织内存边界。

Result: 覆盖分析、攻击路径追踪和信任边界评估表明：设计能处理已记录的攻击向量并实现深度防御；在四分之三的代表性攻击链中，前两步即可拦截攻击；相比扁平多智能体系统，可利用信任边界至少减少72%。

Conclusion: AgenticCyOps为企业级多智能体系统集成安全提供了基础框架，通过系统化识别关键攻击面并实施结构化防御原则，显著提升了企业环境中AI驱动的多智能体工作流的安全性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) powered by LLMs promise adaptive, reasoning-driven enterprise workflows, yet granting agents autonomous control over tools, memory, and communication introduces attack surfaces absent from deterministic pipelines. While current research largely addresses prompt-level exploits and narrow individual vectors, it lacks a holistic architectural model for enterprise-grade security. We introduce AgenticCyOps (Securing Multi-Agentic AI Integration in Enterprise Cyber Operations), a framework built on a systematic decomposition of attack surfaces across component, coordination, and protocol layers, revealing that documented vectors consistently trace back to two integration surfaces: tool orchestration and memory management. Building on this observation, we formalize these integration surfaces as primary trust boundaries and define five defensive principles: authorized interfaces, capability scoping, verified execution, memory integrity & synchronization, and access-controlled data isolation; each aligned with established compliance standards (NIST, ISO 27001, GDPR, EU AI Act). We apply the framework to a Security Operations Center (SOC) workflow, adopting the Model Context Protocol (MCP) as the structural basis, with phase-scoped agents, consensus validation loops, and per-organization memory boundaries. Coverage analysis, attack path tracing, and trust boundary assessment confirm that the design addresses the documented attack vectors with defense-in-depth, intercepts three of four representative attack chains within the first two steps, and reduces exploitable trust boundaries by a minimum of 72% compared to a flat MAS, positioning AgenticCyOps as a foundation for securing enterprise-grade integration.

</details>


### [72] [Synergistic Directed Execution and LLM-Driven Analysis for Zero-Day AI-Generated Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09044)
*George Edwards,Mahdi Eslamimehr*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合符号执行与LLM引导路径优先级的混合分析框架，用于检测AI生成的零日恶意软件，在保证检测算法可靠性的同时显著提升了检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被武器化用于自动生成恶意软件，对传统检测范式构成生存威胁。AI生成的恶意软件具有多态性、变形性和上下文感知的规避能力，使得基于签名的浅层启发式防御失效。

Method: 提出混合分析框架，结合具体符号执行与LLM增强的路径优先级排序及深度学习漏洞分类。引入三种新算法：1) LLM引导的具体符号探索策略；2) 基于Transformer的路径约束分类器；3) 使用强化学习迭代优化LLM优先级策略的反馈循环。

Result: 在EMBER、Malimg、SOREL-20M和包含2500个LLM合成样本的AI-Gen-Malware基准测试中，对传统恶意软件达到98.7%准确率，对AI生成威胁达到97.5%准确率，比基线方法提升8.4-52.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架通过形式化验证的检测算法和LLM增强的路径探索，为检测AI生成的零日恶意软件提供了可证明的保证，显著优于现有检测系统。

Abstract: The weaponization of LLMs for automated malware generation poses an existential threat to conventional detection paradigms. AI-generated malware exhibits polymorphic, metamorphic, and context-aware evasion capabilities that render signature-based and shallow heuristic defenses obsolete. This paper introduces a novel hybrid analysis framework that synergistically combines \emph{concolic execution} with \emph{LLM-augmented path prioritization} and \emph{deep-learning-based vulnerability classification} to detect zero-day AI-generated malware with provable guarantees. We formalize the detection problem within a first-order temporal logic over program execution traces, define a lattice-theoretic abstraction for path constraint spaces, and prove both the \emph{soundness} and \emph{relative completeness} of our detection algorithm, assuming classifier correctness. The framework introduces three novel algorithms: (i) an LLM-guided concolic exploration strategy that reduces the average number of explored paths by 73.2\% compared to depth-first search while maintaining equivalent malicious-path coverage; (ii) a transformer-based path-constraint classifier trained on symbolic execution traces; and (iii) a feedback loop that iteratively refines the LLM's prioritization policy using reinforcement learning from detection outcomes. We provide a comprehensive implementation built upon \texttt{angr} 9.2, \texttt{Z3} 4.12, Hugging Face Transformers 4.38, and PyTorch 2.2, with configuration details enabling reproducibility. Experimental evaluation on the EMBER, Malimg, SOREL-20M, and a novel AI-Gen-Malware benchmark comprising 2{,}500 LLM-synthesized samples demonstrates that achieves 98.7\% accuracy on conventional malware and 97.5\% accuracy on AI-generated threats, outperforming ClamAV, YARA, MalConv, and EMBER-GBDT baselines by margins of 8.4--52.2 percentage points on AI-generated samples.

</details>


### [73] [PixelConfig: Longitudinal Measurement and Reverse-Engineering of Meta Pixel Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09380)
*Abdullah Ghani,Yash Vekaria,Zubair Shafiq*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PixelConfig框架分析Meta Pixel在网站上的配置差异，发现健康网站中活动追踪和身份追踪功能采用率高达98.4%，且被用于追踪敏感医疗信息，而追踪限制功能保护有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去研究主要关注追踪像素在网页上的普遍存在，而对其在不同网站上的配置差异关注有限。Meta Pixel在不同网站上的配置可能存在显著差异，特别是涉及敏感健康信息时，需要系统分析其配置特征和隐私影响。

Method: 提出PixelConfig差分分析框架，用于逆向工程Meta Pixel在网页上的配置。使用互联网档案馆Wayback Machine数据，分析2017-2024年间18K健康相关网站和10K控制组网站的Meta Pixel配置，重点关注活动追踪、身份追踪和追踪限制三类配置。

Result: 活动追踪功能（如自动收集按钮点击和页面元数据）和身份追踪功能（如不受第三方cookie阻断影响的第一方cookie）采用率高达98.4%，主要由Pixel默认设置驱动。健康网站上Pixel被用于追踪敏感信息，如预约医疗服务和特定医疗条件（如勃起功能障碍）相关的按钮点击。追踪限制功能（如Core Setup）在健康网站配置率为34.3%，控制网站为8.7%，但这些功能保护有限且可被绕过。

Conclusion: Meta Pixel的默认设置导致高采用率的敏感信息追踪，特别是在健康网站上。现有的追踪限制功能配置率低且保护效果有限，需要更强的隐私保护机制和监管措施来防止敏感健康信息的过度追踪。

Abstract: Tracking pixels are used to optimize online ad campaigns through personalization, re-targeting, and conversion tracking. Past research has primarily focused on detecting the prevalence of tracking pixels on the web, with limited attention to how they are configured across websites. A tracking pixel may be configured differently on different websites. In this paper, we present a differential analysis framework: PixelConfig, to reverse-engineer the configurations of Meta Pixel deployments across the web. Using this framework, we investigate three types of Meta Pixel configurations: activity tracking (i.e., what a user is doing on a website), identity tracking (i.e., who a user is or who the device is associated with), and tracking restrictions (i.e., mechanisms to limit the sharing of potentially sensitive information).
  Using data from the Internet Archive's Wayback Machine, we analyze and compare Meta Pixel configurations on 18K health-related websites with a control group of the top 10K websites from 2017 to 2024. We find that activity tracking features, such as automatic events that collect button clicks and page metadata, and identity tracking features, such as first-party cookies that are unaffected by third-party cookie blocking, reached adoption rates of up to 98.4%, largely driven by the Pixel's default settings. We also find that the Pixel is being used to track potentially sensitive information, such as user interactions related to booking medical appointments and button clicks associated with specific medical conditions (e.g., erectile dysfunction) on health-related websites. Tracking restriction features, such as Core Setup, are configured on up to 34.3% of health websites and 8.7% of control websites. However, even when enabled, these tracking restriction features provide limited protection and can be circumvented in practice.

</details>


### [74] [Optimal partition selection with Rényi differential privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09167)
*Charlie Harrison,Pasin Pasin Manurangsi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对分区选择问题的最优近似Rényi差分隐私算法，扩展了单分区最优算法到多分区场景，并展示了其在现有算法中的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在私有数据分析中，分区选择问题面临挑战：如何在满足差分隐私约束的同时最大化释放的分区集合。现有工作仅解决了用户提交单个分区的最优算法，但实际应用中用户通常提交多个分区，且缺乏通用的最优性结果。

Method: 1. 将单分区最优算法推广到δ-近似(α, ε)-Rényi差分隐私下的最优算法；2. 针对L²有界加权分区选择提出扩展算法，可作为高斯机制的改进替代；3. 将该原语集成到现有最优分区选择算法中；4. 形式化使用加性噪声的分区选择基本最优概念。

Result: 1. 提出的算法可轻松集成到PolicyGaussian和MAD2R等先进算法中，提升并行和顺序自适应算法的性能；2. 证明了支持同时释放分区和频率的算法存在固有代价；3. 展示了加性噪声与非加性噪声机制在该问题上的数值分离。

Conclusion: 本文为多分区场景提供了最优的近似Rényi差分隐私算法，显著提升了现有分区选择算法的性能，并揭示了同时支持分区和频率释放的算法存在固有代价，为实际应用提供了理论指导和实用工具。

Abstract: A common problem in private data analysis is the partition selection problem, where each user holds a set of partitions (e.g. keys in a GROUP BY operation) from a possibly unbounded set. The challenge here is in maximizing the set of released partitions while respecting a differential privacy constraint. Previous work [Desfontaines et al., PoPETS 2022] presented an optimal $(\varepsilon, δ)$-DP algorithm when each user submits only a single partition. We generalize this approach to find the optimal algorithm under $δ$-approximate $(α, \varepsilon)$-Rényi differential privacy (RDP), which allows much tighter analysis under composition. Motivated by the non-existence of a general optimality result in the case where users submit multiple partitions each, we present an extension of our optimal algorithm tuned for $L^2$ bounded weighted partition selection which can be used as a drop-in improvement over the Gaussian mechanism any time the partition frequency is not also needed. We show that our primitive can be easily plugged into state of the art partition selection algorithms (PolicyGaussian from [Gopi et al., ICML 2020] and MAD2R from [Chen et al., ICML 2025]), improving performance both for parallel and sequential adaptive algorithms. Finally, we show that there is an inherent cost to algorithms which do support releasing the frequency as well as the partitions. Specifically, we formulate a basic notion of optimal approximate RDP algorithm for partition selection using additive noise, and show that there is a numerical separation between additive and non-additive noise mechanisms for this problem.

</details>


### [75] [ZipPIR: High-throughput Single-server PIR without Client-side Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09190)
*Rasoul Akhavan Mahdavi,Abdulrahman Diaa,Florian Kerschbaum*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ZipPIR是一种高吞吐量的私有信息检索协议，通过将LWE密文压缩为更小的Paillier密文，实现了无客户端存储的高性能PIR，在1GB数据库上达到超过2GB/s的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PIR协议存在吞吐量限制或客户端存储需求大的问题：基于RLWE的PIR吞吐量有限；高吞吐量协议如SimplePIR需要大量客户端存储，Piano离线阶段通信成本过高。这些限制在动态数据库更新时更加严重，与资源受限客户端的实际约束相冲突。

Method: ZipPIR将LWE密文压缩为显著更小的Paillier密文，利用离线阶段实现这种大小缩减，而不在在线阶段产生相关计算成本。在计算假设下，ZipPIR具有几乎静默的离线阶段，仅需初始公钥，服务器可在空闲时间独立生成和更新提示而无需客户端交互。

Result: ZipPIR实现超过2GB/s的吞吐量，与SimplePIR等最先进协议相当，但无需大型客户端存储提示。在1GB数据库的PIR中，相比无客户端存储的现有协议，吞吐量提高高达10倍，同时每个客户端仅需不到200KB的服务器端存储，显著增强了实际部署的可扩展性。

Conclusion: ZipPIR是首个使用Paillier加密且吞吐量与最先进PIR协议竞争的PIR协议，解决了现有高吞吐量PIR协议的客户端存储和通信成本问题，特别适用于资源受限客户端和动态数据库场景。

Abstract: Private Information Retrieval (PIR) allows a client to privately access a database without revealing which element is accessed. Initial PIR protocols based on Ring Learning with Errors (RLWE) demonstrated the practicality of PIR, but achieve limited throughput. Alternatively, high-throughput protocols leverage an offline phase that requires substantial client-side storage (e.g., hints in SimplePIR) or involve prohibitive communication costs during the offline phase (e.g., Piano). These limitations conflict with the practical constraints of resource-limited clients and are further exacerbated by dynamic databases, where updates necessitate costly regeneration and retransmission of hints.
  To address these challenges, we propose ZipPIR, a high-throughput PIR protocol that compresses LWE ciphertexts into significantly smaller Paillier ciphertexts. ZipPIR leverages the offline phase to obtain this size reduction without incurring the associated computational cost in the online phase. Moreover, under computational assumptions, ZipPIR features an almost silent offline phase, requiring no communication beyond an initial public key, enabling the server to independently generate and update hints during idle times without client interaction. ZipPIR achieves over 2 GB/s of throughput - comparable to state-of-the-art protocols such as SimplePIR - without the need for a large client-stored hint. For PIR over a 1 GB database, ZipPIR has up to 10x higher throughput than existing protocols with no client-side storage, while requiring less than 200 KB of server-side storage per client, significantly enhancing scalability for practical deployments. While prior PIR protocols using Paillier are very inefficient, ZipPIR is the first PIR protocol using Paillier that achieves throughput that is competitive with state-of-the-art PIR protocols.

</details>


### [76] [Reasoning-Oriented Programming: Chaining Semantic Gadgets to Jailbreak Large Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09246)
*Quanchen Zou,Moyang Chen,Zonghao Ying,Wenzhuo Xu,Yisong Xiao,Deyue Zhang,Dongdong Yang,Zhao Liu,Xiangzheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大型视觉语言模型安全对齐的新攻击方法——推理导向编程，通过组合良性输入诱导模型合成有害逻辑，有效绕过感知级安全防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型视觉语言模型的安全防御主要针对输入表示中的显性恶意模式，忽视了组合推理中的系统性漏洞。研究发现模型可能被诱导从良性前提合成有害逻辑，需要揭示这种深层安全风险。

Method: 提出推理导向编程攻击范式，类比系统安全中的返回导向编程。开发了Tool自动框架，通过优化语义正交性和空间隔离性，生成语义上与有害意图解耦的视觉小工具，并安排它们防止过早特征融合，使恶意逻辑仅在后期推理过程中显现。

Result: 在SafeBench和MM-SafetyBench基准上评估了7个最先进的LVLM（包括GPT-4o和Claude 3.7 Sonnet）。Tool方法在开源模型上平均比最强基线高出4.67%，在商业模型上高出9.50%，能持续绕过安全对齐。

Conclusion: 揭示了大型视觉语言模型在组合推理层面的系统性安全漏洞，现有感知级安全对齐不足以防御推理导向编程攻击，需要开发更全面的安全机制来应对此类深层威胁。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) undergo safety alignment to suppress harmful content. However, current defenses predominantly target explicit malicious patterns in the input representation, often overlooking the vulnerabilities inherent in compositional reasoning. In this paper, we identify a systemic flaw where LVLMs can be induced to synthesize harmful logic from benign premises. We formalize this attack paradigm as \textit{Reasoning-Oriented Programming}, drawing a structural analogy to Return-Oriented Programming in systems security. Just as ROP circumvents memory protections by chaining benign instruction sequences, our approach exploits the model's instruction-following capability to orchestrate a semantic collision of orthogonal benign inputs. We instantiate this paradigm via \tool{}, an automated framework that optimizes for \textit{semantic orthogonality} and \textit{spatial isolation}. By generating visual gadgets that are semantically decoupled from the harmful intent and arranging them to prevent premature feature fusion, \tool{} forces the malicious logic to emerge only during the late-stage reasoning process. This effectively bypasses perception-level alignment. We evaluate \tool{} on SafeBench and MM-SafetyBench across 7 state-of-the-art 0.LVLMs, including GPT-4o and Claude 3.7 Sonnet. Our results demonstrate that \tool{} consistently circumvents safety alignment, outperforming the strongest existing baseline by an average of 4.67\% on open-source models and 9.50\% on commercial models.

</details>


### [77] [External entropy supply for IoT devices employing a RISC-V Trusted Execution Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09311)
*Arttu Paju,Alejandro Cabrera Aldaya,Nicola Tuveri,Juha Savimäki,Marko Kivikangas,Brian McGillion*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 为资源受限的IoT设备提供基于RISC-V TEE的外部熵服务，解决其熵源不足问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备通常体积小、资源受限，难以收集足够的熵来生成安全的加密密钥，这构成了IoT安全的关键瓶颈

Method: 采用基于RISC-V的可信执行环境(TEE)构建外部熵服务系统：1) 使用少量真实熵或预装密钥建立初始安全通信；2) 设备可从TEE服务器请求密码学强度的熵；3) RISC-V提供真随机数生成器和TEE用于设备认证；4) 可扩展集成带高质量熵传感器的IoT设备作为额外熵源

Result: 开源实现证明在开放RISC-V平台上构建可信熵基础设施是可行且有效的，能够为IoT设备群提供可靠的熵供应

Conclusion: 基于RISC-V TEE的外部熵服务方案成功解决了资源受限IoT设备的熵供应问题，为IoT安全提供了可扩展的解决方案

Abstract: Entropy--a measure of randomness--is compulsory for the generation of secure cryptographic keys; however, Internet of Things (IoT) devices that are small or constrained often struggle to collect suf ficient entropy. In this article, we solve the entropy provisioning problem for a fleet of IoT devices that can generate a limited amount of entropy. We employ a Trusted Execution Environment (TEE) based on RISC-V to create an external entropy service for a fleet of IoT devices. A small measure of true entropy or pre-installed keys can establish initial secure communication. Once connected, devices can request cryptographically strong entropy from a TEE-backed server. RISC-V offers True Random Number Generators (TRNGs) and a TEE for devices to attest that they are receiving reliable entropy. In addition, this solution can be expanded by adding IoT devices with sensors that produce high-quality entropy as additional entropy sources for the RISC-V entropy provider. Our open-source implementation shows that building trusted entropy infrastructure for IoT is both feasible and effective on open RISC-V platforms.

</details>


### [78] [Robust Provably Secure Image Steganography via Latent Iterative Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09348)
*Yanan Li,Zixuan Wang,Qiyang Xiao,Yanzhen Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于潜在空间迭代优化的鲁棒可证明安全图像隐写框架，通过接收端迭代优化潜在变量来最小化重构误差，提高消息提取准确性，同时保持可证明安全性并显著增强抗压缩和图像处理鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可证明安全的隐写方法在鲁棒性方面存在不足，特别是在图像压缩和处理场景下。需要在保持可证明安全性的同时，显著提升隐写系统对实际应用中常见图像处理的鲁棒性。

Method: 基于潜在空间迭代优化的框架，接收端将传输图像作为固定参考，迭代优化潜在变量以最小化重构误差。该方法可作为独立模块增强其他可证明安全隐写方案的鲁棒性。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法不仅提高了抗图像压缩的鲁棒性并保持可证明安全性，还能作为独立模块增强其他可证明安全隐写方案的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 潜在空间优化为构建可靠、鲁棒且安全的隐写系统提供了实用且有前景的途径，展示了迭代优化在提升隐写系统实际应用性能方面的潜力。

Abstract: We propose a robust and provably secure image steganography framework based on latent-space iterative optimization. Within this framework, the receiver treats the transmitted image as a fixed reference and iteratively refines a latent variable to minimize the reconstruction error, thereby improving message extraction accuracy. Unlike prior methods, our approach preserves the provable security of the embedding while markedly enhancing robustness under various compression and image processing scenarios. On benchmark datasets, the experimental results demonstrate that the proposed iterative optimization not only improves robustness against image compression while preserving provable security, but can also be applied as an independent module to further reinforce robustness in other provably secure steganographic schemes. This highlights the practicality and promise of latent-space optimization for building reliable, robust, and secure steganographic systems.

</details>


### [79] [ProvAgent: Threat Detection Based on Identity-Behavior Binding and Multi-Agent Collaborative Attack Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09358)
*Wenhao Yan,Ning An,Linxu Li,Bingsheng Bi,Bo Jiang,Zhigang Lu,Baoxu Liu,Junrong Liu,Cong Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ProvAgent是一个将威胁溯源范式从人机协作转变为多智能体系统与传统模型协作的框架，通过图对比学习实现细粒度身份-行为一致性分析，并采用假设验证多智能体框架进行深度自主调查，显著提升了APT检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于溯源的APT检测面临两个矛盾问题：专家对传统检测模型识别复杂攻击能力的怀疑（专家怀疑论），以及分析师无法在没有这些模型的情况下手动处理大规模原始日志来检测威胁（专家依赖）。这导致调查质量依赖于人类专业知识，并经常产生告警疲劳。

Method: ProvAgent采用三层架构：1）利用传统模型的速度和成本效益进行大规模日志的初始异常筛查；2）通过图对比学习强制执行细粒度身份-行为一致性，基于特定属性分析实体以生成高保真告警；3）以这些告警为调查切入点，通过假设验证多智能体框架实现深度自主调查。

Result: 在真实数据集上的评估表明，ProvAgent在异常检测方面优于六个最先进的基线方法。通过自动化调查，ProvAgent能够以每天最低0.06美元的成本重建近乎完整的攻击过程。

Conclusion: ProvAgent成功地将威胁溯源范式从人机协作演进为多智能体系统与传统模型的协作，解决了当前APT检测中的专家怀疑和专家依赖矛盾，显著提升了检测效率和准确性，同时降低了成本。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs) pose critical challenges to modern cybersecurity due to their multi-stage and stealthy nature. While provenance-based detection approaches show promise in capturing causal attack semantics, current threat provenance practices face two paradoxical issues: (1) expert skepticism, where human analysts doubt the capability of traditional detection models to identify complex attacks; and (2) expert dependence, as analysts cannot manually process large-scale raw logs to detect threats without these models. Consequently, collaboration between humans and traditional models remains the prevailing paradigm. However, this renders investigation quality contingent upon human expertise and frequently results in alert fatigue. To address these challenges, we present ProvAgent, a framework that evolves the threat provenance paradigm from human-model collaboration to a novel collaboration between multi-agent systems and traditional models. ProvAgent leverages the speed and cost-efficiency of traditional models for initial anomaly screening over large-scale logs. By enforcing fine-grained identity-behavior consistency via graph contrastive learning, it profiles entities based on specific attributes to generate high-fidelity alerts. With these alerts serving as investigation entry points, ProvAgent achieves in-depth autonomous investigation through a hypothesis-verification multi-agent framework. Evaluations with real-world datasets demonstrate that ProvAgent outperforms six state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines in anomaly detection. Through automated investigation, ProvAgent reconstructs near-complete attack processes at a minimum cost of \$0.06 per day.

</details>


### [80] [ShapeMark: Robust and Diversity-Preserving Watermarking for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09454)
*Yuqi Qian,Yun Cao,Haocheng Fu,Meiyang Lv,Meineng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于结构化噪声模式的扩散模型水印方法，通过将水印编码到噪声模式而非单个像素值中，在保持生成多样性的同时实现了卓越的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成图像的广泛传播和重用带来了知识产权保护和内容溯源的新挑战。现有的噪声即水印方法无法平衡鲁棒性和多样性，主要因为值编码方式在实际应用场景中极其脆弱。

Method: 提出将水印比特编码到结构化噪声模式中，即使单个像素值被扰动也能保留水印。引入专门的随机化设计，在不改变噪声值的情况下重新洗牌噪声元素位置，防止水印导致固定的噪声模式或空间位置。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在各种有损场景下实现了最先进的鲁棒性，同时保持了高生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过将水印编码到结构化噪声模式而非单个像素值中，并引入随机化设计，该方法成功解决了现有噪声即水印方法在鲁棒性和多样性之间的权衡问题，为扩散模型的版权保护提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have made substantial advances in recent years, enabling high-quality image synthesis; however, the widespread dissemination and reuse of their outputs have introduced new challenges in intellectual property protection and content provenance. Image watermarking offers a solution to these challenges, and recent work has increasingly explored Noise-as-Watermark (NaW) approaches that integrate watermarking directly into the diffusion process. However, existing NaW methods fail to balance robustness and diversity. We attribute this weakness to value encoding, which encodes watermark bits into individual sampled values. It is extremely fragile in practical application scenarios. To address this, we encode watermark bits into the structured noise pattern, so that the watermark is preserved even when individual values are perturbed. To further ensure generation diversity, we introduce a dedicated randomization design that reshuffles the positions of noise elements without changing their values, preventing the watermark from inducing fixed noise patterns or spatial locations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art robustness while maintaining high generation quality across a wide range of lossy scenarios.

</details>


### [81] [Compartmentalization-Aware Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09544)
*Jia Hu,Youcheng Sun,Pierre Olivier*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究利用大语言模型自动修复软件隔离中跨隔离接口漏洞的方法，提出专门框架解决现有LLM缺乏隔离意识的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件隔离技术通过将应用分解为相互隔离的隔间来限制攻击影响，但现有隔离软件大多存在跨隔间接口漏洞，攻击者可借此逃逸隔离。传统修复方法工程量大且困难，而现有自动程序修复方法和大语言模型缺乏对隔离特性的理解，无法有效修复此类漏洞。

Method: 设计并实现专门针对隔间接口安全的APR框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 专门化模糊测试器发现跨隔间接口漏洞；2) 补丁生成组件通过系列分析技术弥补现有LLM缺乏隔离意识的问题；3) 补丁验证组件评估生成修复的有效性。这些组件集成在反馈循环中。

Result: 通过样本接口漏洞验证了框架有效性，与通用LLM的简单使用进行对比，展示了专门化框架的优势。虽然论文处于进行中状态，但早期结果显示了该方法的潜力。

Conclusion: 现有APR方法和通用LLM不适合修复跨隔间接口漏洞，需要专门设计的框架来弥补LLM缺乏隔离意识的问题。提出的集成反馈循环框架为解决这一挑战提供了有前景的方向，并讨论了未来的研究路径。

Abstract: Software compartmentalization breaks down an application into compartments isolated from each other: an attacker taking over a compartment will be confined to it, limiting the damage they can cause to the rest of the application. Despite the security promises of this approach, recent studies have shown that most existing compartmentalized software is plagued by vulnerabilities at cross-compartment interfaces, allowing an attacker taking over a compartment to escape its confinement and negate the security guarantees expected from compartmentalization. In that context, securing cross-compartment interfaces is notoriously difficult and engineering-intensive.
  In light of recent advances in Automated Program Repair (APR), notably through the use of Large Language Models (LLMs), this paper presents a work in progress investigating the suitability of LLM-based APR at securing cross-compartment interfaces as automatically as possible. We observe that existing APR approaches and general purpose/code-centric LLMs used as is are unfit for this task, and present the design, implementation, and early results of a new APR framework dedicated to compartment interface safety. The framework integrates into a feedback loop 1) a specialized fuzzer uncovering cross-compartment interface vulnerabilities; 2) a patch generation component bridging the lack of compartmentalization awareness of existing LLMs with a series of analysis techniques; and 3) a patch validation component assessing the effectiveness of generated vulnerability fixes. We validate our framework over a sample interface vulnerability, comparing it to a naive use of general-purpose LLMs, and discuss future research avenues.

</details>


### [82] [Enabling Multi-Client Authorization in Dynamic SSE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09550)
*Seydina Ousmane Diallo,Maryline Laurent,Nesrine Kaaniche*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MASSE是一个支持属性访问控制的动态多客户端可搜索对称加密方案，扩展了OXT框架，实现了细粒度访问控制、动态更新和高效查询


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可搜索对称加密方案存在元数据泄露过多、缺乏细粒度访问控制等限制，阻碍了多客户端环境下安全搜索的实际应用扩展

Method: 基于OXT框架扩展，提出MASSE方案，结合属性访问控制，支持动态文档更新和客户端授权撤销，无需重新加密数据库或索引

Result: 形式化证明MASSE的安全性和前向/后向隐私保护，实验显示在100个关键词、每个关联150个文档的数据库中，生成10-100个查询耗时<2秒，检索50个匹配文档需14秒

Conclusion: MASSE在安全性和效率上优于现有方案，支持大规模加密数据库，适用于动态云部署环境

Abstract: Outsourcing encrypted data to the cloud creates a fundamental tension between data privacy and functional searchability. Current Searchable Symmetric Encryption (SSE) solutions frequently have significant limitations, such as excessive metadata leakage, or a lack of fine-grained access control. These issues restrict the scalability of secure searches in real-world applications where multiple clients require different levels of authorization. Our paper proposes MASSE, a dynamic multi-client SSE scheme incorporating attribute-based access control, which expands the OXT framework. With MASSE, clients are restricted sto searching for keywords authorized by their specific attribute sets, and the server remains unaware of the keywords and attributes. MASSE supports practical dynamic updates to documents, and client authorizations, including revocation, without requiring reencryption of the database or indices, or a large number of interactions. We formally prove the security of MASSE, that is, forward and backward privacy under a well-defined leakage profile, and token unforgeability. An experimental evaluation in a database containing 100 keywords, each associated with 150 documents, demonstrates the practical efficiency of MASSE. It takes less than two seconds to generate 10 to 100 keyword queries and 14 seconds to retrieve 50 matching documents. Theoretical results show that MASSE outperforms competing solutions, including OXT, and can be scaled to large encrypted databases. MASSE is also suitable for dynamic cloud deployments.
  Keywords: Searchable Encryption, SSE, Multi-Client, Attribute Based SSE, Access Control, Revocation, OXT

</details>


### [83] [CLIOPATRA: Extracting Private Information from LLM Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09781)
*Meenatchi Sundaram Muthu Selva Annamalai,Emiliano De Cristofaro,Peter Kairouz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CLIOPATRA是首个针对"隐私保护"LLM洞察系统的隐私攻击，通过精心设计的恶意聊天记录突破多层隐私保护机制，成功从目标用户聊天中提取敏感医疗信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI助手广泛使用，隐私感知平台如Anthropic的Clio被引入用于从真实AI使用中生成洞察。这些平台声称通过多层启发式技术（PII脱敏、聚类、过滤、LLM隐私审计）保护隐私，但缺乏对这些声明的实际安全评估。

Method: 提出CLIOPATRA攻击方法：攻击者基于目标用户基本人口统计信息和单个症状，精心设计和插入恶意聊天记录到系统中，突破PII脱敏、聚类、过滤和LLM隐私审计等多层保护机制，诱导系统泄露目标用户的敏感医疗信息。

Result: 在合成生成的医疗目标聊天上评估CLIOPATRA：仅知道目标用户基本人口统计信息和单个症状的攻击者，通过检查Clio输出成功提取用户医疗历史的概率为39%。当Clio配置其他最先进模型且攻击者对目标用户了解更多时，成功率接近100%。现有临时缓解措施（如LLM隐私审计）不可靠，无法检测主要泄露。

Conclusion: 即使采用分层设计，当前启发式隐私保护机制仍不足以充分保护LLM分析系统中的用户数据。CLIOPATRA攻击揭示了隐私保护LLM洞察系统的实际脆弱性，需要更强大的隐私保护方法。

Abstract: As AI assistants become widely used, privacy-aware platforms like Anthropic's Clio have been introduced to generate insights from real-world AI use. Clio's privacy protections rely on layering multiple heuristic techniques together, including PII redaction, clustering, filtering, and LLM-based privacy auditing. In this paper, we put these claims to the test by presenting CLIOPATRA, the first privacy attack against "privacy-preserving" LLM insight systems. The attack involves a realistic adversary that carefully designs and inserts malicious chats into the system to break multiple layers of privacy protections and induce the leakage of sensitive information from a target user's chat.
  We evaluated CLIOPATRA on synthetically generated medical target chats, demonstrating that an adversary who knows only the basic demographics of a target user and a single symptom can successfully extract the user's medical history in 39% of cases by just inspecting Clio's output. Furthermore, CLIOPATRA can reach close to 100% when Clio is configured with other state-of-the-art models and the adversary's knowledge of the target user is increased. We also show that existing ad hoc mitigations, such as LLM-based privacy auditing, are unreliable and fail to detect major leaks. Our findings indicate that even when layered, current heuristic protections are insufficient to adequately protect user data in LLM-based analysis systems.

</details>

### [84] [Why Channel-Centric Models are not Enough to Predict End-to-End Performance in Private 5G: A Measurement Campaign and Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08865)
*Nils Jörgensen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 在工业5G环境中，传统基于信道指标（如信号强度）的吞吐量预测方法存在系统性高估，主要原因是MIMO层数预测不准确；数据驱动的高斯过程回归模型能显著降低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前通信感知机器人规划中基于信道指标预测吞吐量的假设，验证这些指标是否能可靠地转化为端到端吞吐量，特别是在工业5G环境中。

Method: 在屏蔽无线电的地下工业设施中进行测量活动，使用移动机器人收集数据。评估商业射线追踪模拟器的吞吐量预测以及数据驱动的高斯过程回归模型，与实测数据对比。使用现成的用户设备，并进行详细的3D建模。

Result: 射线追踪模拟器能合理捕捉室内传播的空间结构并预测信道指标，但系统性高估吞吐量，即使在视距区域也是如此。主要误差来源是高估了可持续的MIMO空间层数：模拟器假设接近均匀的四层传输，而实测显示层数在1-3层间大幅调整。高斯过程模型（使用有理二次核）将预测误差降低约三分之二，且偏差接近零。

Conclusion: 良好的信道条件不能保证高吞吐量；仅依赖信道中心预测的通信感知规划器可能产生过于乐观的轨迹，违反可靠性要求。准确的5G系统吞吐量预测需要广泛的链路层模型校准或能捕捉真实系统行为的数据驱动方法。

Abstract: Communication-aware robot planning requires accurate predictions of wireless network performance. Current approaches rely on channel-level metrics such as received signal strength and signal-to-noise ratio, assuming these translate reliably into end-to-end throughput. We challenge this assumption through a measurement campaign in a private 5G industrial environment. We evaluate throughput predictions from a commercial ray-tracing simulator as well as data-driven Gaussian process regression models against measurements collected using a mobile robot. The study uses off-the-shelf user equipment in an underground, radio-shielded facility with detailed 3D modeling, representing a best-case scenario for prediction accuracy. The ray-tracing simulator captures the spatial structure of indoor propagation and predicts channel-level metrics with reasonable fidelity. However, it systematically over-predicts throughput, even in line-of-sight regions. The dominant error source is shown to be over-estimation of sustainable MIMO spatial layers: the simulator assumes near-uniform four-layer transmission while measurements reveal substantial adaptation between one and three layers. This mismatch inflates predicted throughput even when channel metrics appear accurate. In contrast, a Gaussian process model with a rational quadratic kernel achieves approximately two-thirds reduction in prediction error with near-zero bias by learning end-to-end throughput directly from measurements. These findings demonstrate that favorable channel conditions do not guarantee high throughput; communication-aware planners relying solely on channel-centric predictions risk overly optimistic trajectories that violate reliability requirements. Accurate throughput prediction for 5G systems requires either extensive calibration of link-layer models or data-driven approaches that capture real system behavior.

</details>


### [85] [The 802.11 MAC protocol leads to inefficient equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09902)
*Godfrey Tan,John Guttag*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文分析表明，802.11 DCF及其增强版本在非合作环境中会导致节点选择次优的传输策略，造成无线信道使用效率低下的纳什均衡。通过将信道资源分配与节点传输策略解耦，可以设计出更优的MAC协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非合作环境（如公共热点或邻近企业WLAN）中，802.11 WLAN节点会自私地调整数据速率或帧大小以最大化自身吞吐量，而不考虑对整体系统性能的影响。现有802.11 DCF及其增强版本可能导致信道使用效率低下的均衡状态。

Method: 采用博弈论模型进行理论分析，并通过仿真验证。研究比较了现有802.11 DCF协议及其增强版本（802.11e）的性能，并提出了通过将信道资源分配与节点传输策略分离来改进MAC协议的设计思路。

Result: 分析表明，在非合作环境中，DCF和802.11e会导致节点达到不良的纳什均衡，造成无线信道使用效率低下。通过建立信道资源分配与节点传输策略的独立性，理想MAC协议可以使理性节点达到所有竞争节点都能获得比DCF更高吞吐量的均衡状态。

Conclusion: 现有802.11 MAC协议在非合作环境中存在设计缺陷，会导致系统性能下降。通过重新设计MAC协议，将信道资源分配机制与节点的传输策略决策解耦，可以引导节点达到更优的均衡状态，提高整体系统效率。

Abstract: Wireless local area networks (WLANs) based on the family of 802.11 technologies are becoming ubiquitous. These technologies support multiple data transmission rates. Transmitting at a lower data rate (by using a more resilient modulation scheme) increases the frame transmission time but reduces the bit error rate. In non-cooperative environments such as public hot-spots or WLANs operated by different enterprises that are physically close to each other, individual nodes attempt to maximize their achieved throughput by adjusting the data rate or frame size used, irrespective of the impact of this on overall system performance.
  In this paper, we show both analytically using a game theoretic model and through simulation that the existing 802.11 distributed MAC protocol, DCF (for Distributed Coordination Function), as well as its enhanced version, which is being standardized as part of 802.11e, can lead non-cooperative nodes to undesirable Nash equilibriums in which the wireless channel is inefficiently used. We show that by establishing independence between the allocation of the shared channel resource and the transmission strategies used by individual nodes, an ideal MAC protocol can lead rational nodes to arrive at equilibriums in which all competing nodes achieve higher throughputs than with DCF.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Strategically Robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning with Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09208)
*Jake Gonzales,Max Horwitz,Eric Mazumdar,Lillian J. Ratliff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RQRE-OVI算法，用于计算风险敏感量化响应均衡，在一般和马尔可夫博弈中实现可证明高效且鲁棒的均衡计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳什均衡在一般和博弈中计算困难且脆弱，存在均衡多重性和对近似误差敏感的问题。需要一种具有唯一性、平滑性并能平衡性能与鲁棒性的均衡概念。

Method: 提出RQRE-OVI算法，基于乐观值迭代和线性函数逼近，用于计算风险敏感量化响应均衡。算法包含有限样本遗憾分析，收敛性证明，并明确样本复杂度与理性和风险敏感参数的标度关系。

Result: 理论分析显示遗憾界限揭示了定量权衡：增加理性收紧遗憾，而风险敏感性诱导正则化增强稳定性。RQRE策略映射在估计收益上具有Lipschitz连续性，且RQRE具有分布鲁棒优化解释。实证表明RQRE-OVI在自对弈中具有竞争力，在交叉对弈中比基于纳什的方法产生更鲁棒的行为。

Conclusion: RQRE-OVI为均衡学习提供了原则性、可扩展且可调优的路径，在保持预期性能的同时显著提高了鲁棒性和泛化能力，纳什均衡可作为完美理性和风险中性极限情况恢复。

Abstract: Provably efficient and robust equilibrium computation in general-sum Markov games remains a core challenge in multi-agent reinforcement learning. Nash equilibrium is computationally intractable in general and brittle due to equilibrium multiplicity and sensitivity to approximation error. We study Risk-Sensitive Quantal Response Equilibrium (RQRE), which yields a unique, smooth solution under bounded rationality and risk sensitivity. We propose \texttt{RQRE-OVI}, an optimistic value iteration algorithm for computing RQRE with linear function approximation in large or continuous state spaces. Through finite-sample regret analysis, we establish convergence and explicitly characterize how sample complexity scales with rationality and risk-sensitivity parameters. The regret bounds reveal a quantitative tradeoff: increasing rationality tightens regret, while risk sensitivity induces regularization that enhances stability and robustness. This exposes a Pareto frontier between expected performance and robustness, with Nash recovered in the limit of perfect rationality and risk neutrality. We further show that the RQRE policy map is Lipschitz continuous in estimated payoffs, unlike Nash, and RQRE admits a distributionally robust optimization interpretation. Empirically, we demonstrate that \texttt{RQRE-OVI} achieves competitive performance under self-play while producing substantially more robust behavior under cross-play compared to Nash-based approaches. These results suggest \texttt{RQRE-OVI} offers a principled, scalable, and tunable path for equilibrium learning with improved robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [87] [Equitable Multi-Task Learning for AI-RANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08717)
*Panayiotis Raptis,Fatih Aslan,George Iosifidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在线-在线公平多任务学习框架，确保AI-RAN中异构用户在共享边缘资源上的长期公平推理性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-RAN需要为异构用户提供随时间变化的学习任务服务，确保不同用户间推理性能的公平性，需要自适应且公平的学习机制

Method: OWO-FMTL框架包含两个学习循环：外层循环更新跨轮次的共享模型，内层循环通过轻量级原始-对偶更新在每轮内重新平衡用户优先级，使用广义α公平性量化公平性

Result: 框架保证随时间推移性能差异逐渐减小，计算开销低适合边缘部署，在凸优化和深度学习任务实验中优于现有多任务学习方法

Conclusion: OWO-FMTL为AI-RAN提供了一种有效的公平多任务学习解决方案，能够在动态场景下平衡效率和公平性

Abstract: AI-enabled Radio Access Networks (AI-RANs) are expected to serve heterogeneous users with time-varying learning tasks over shared edge resources. Ensuring equitable inference performance across these users requires adaptive and fair learning mechanisms. This paper introduces an online-within-online fair multi-task learning (OWO-FMTL) framework that ensures long-term equity across users. The method combines two learning loops: an outer loop updating the shared model across rounds and an inner loop rebalancing user priorities within each round with a lightweight primal-dual update. Equity is quantified via generalized alpha-fairness, allowing a trade-off between efficiency and fairness. The framework guarantees diminishing performance disparity over time and operates with low computational overhead suitable for edge deployment. Experiments on convex and deep learning tasks confirm that OWO-FMTL outperforms existing multi-task learning baselines under dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [88] [PPO-Based Hybrid Optimization for RIS-Assisted Semantic Vehicular Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09082)
*Wei Feng,Jingbo Zhang,Qiong Wu,Pingyi Fan,Qiang Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RIS辅助的语义感知车联网边缘计算框架，通过优化卸载比例、语义符号数量和RIS相移来降低延迟，采用PPO和LP混合方案实现40-50%延迟降低


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持车联网中对延迟敏感的应用，解决动态环境和间歇性链路问题，需要优化无线连接并减少传输延迟

Method: 提出RIS辅助的语义感知VEC框架，集成RIS优化无线连接和语义通信传输语义特征；采用两阶段混合方案：PPO处理离散决策，LP优化卸载比例

Result: 仿真验证了框架的优越性，PPO混合方案比GA和QPSO降低40-50%的平均端到端延迟；系统在30辆车拥堵场景下仍能保持低延迟，展现良好可扩展性

Conclusion: RIS辅助的语义感知VEC框架能有效降低车联网应用延迟，提出的混合优化方案在动态环境中表现优异，具有实际应用潜力

Abstract: To support latency-sensitive Internet of Vehicles (IoV) applications amidst dynamic environments and intermittent links, this paper proposes a Reconfigurable Intelligent Surface (RIS)-aided semantic-aware Vehicle Edge Computing (VEC) framework. This approach integrates RIS to optimize wireless connectivity and semantic communication to minimize latency by transmitting semantic features. We formulate a comprehensive joint optimization problem by optimizing offloading ratios, the number of semantic symbols, and RIS phase shifts. Considering the problem's high dimensionality and non-convexity, we propose a two-tier hybrid scheme that employs Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) for discrete decision-making and Linear Programming (LP) for offloading optimization. {The simulation results have validated the proposed framework's superiority over existing methods. Specifically, the proposed PPO-based hybrid optimization scheme reduces the average end-to-end latency by approximately 40% to 50% compared to Genetic Algorithm (GA) and Quantum-behaved Particle Swarm Optimization (QPSO). Moreover, the system demonstrates strong scalability by maintaining low latency even in congested scenarios with up to 30 vehicles.

</details>


### [89] [Towards a Neural Debugger for Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09951)
*Maximilian Beck,Jonas Gehring,Jannik Kossen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出神经调试器，这是一种能够模拟传统调试器操作（如单步执行、设置断点）的语言模型，支持正向和逆向执行预测，为未来智能编码系统提供执行反馈基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经解释器方法缺乏交互控制能力，而开发者在实际调试中通常使用调试器在断点处暂停并单步执行相关代码段。需要一种能够模拟传统调试器交互操作的神经模型。

Method: 通过微调大型语言模型或从头预训练较小模型来创建神经调试器，支持单步进入、单步跳过、单步跳出函数以及在特定源代码行设置断点等调试操作，能够建模正向执行（预测未来状态和输出）和逆向执行（推断先前状态或输入）。

Result: 在CruxEval基准测试中，神经调试器在输出和输入预测任务上均表现出色，展示了强大的条件执行建模能力，为未来智能编码系统提供了可靠的世界模型基础。

Conclusion: 神经调试器是迈向未来智能编码系统的重要一步，可作为模拟调试环境的世界模型，为代码生成、程序理解和自动化调试提供更强大的能力基础。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) on Python execution traces grounds them in code execution and enables the line-by-line execution prediction of whole Python programs, effectively turning them into neural interpreters (FAIR CodeGen Team et al., 2025). However, developers rarely execute programs step by step; instead, they use debuggers to stop execution at certain breakpoints and step through relevant portions only while inspecting or modifying program variables. Existing neural interpreter approaches lack such interactive control. To address this limitation, we introduce neural debuggers: language models that emulate traditional debuggers, supporting operations such as stepping into, over, or out of functions, as well as setting breakpoints at specific source lines. We show that neural debuggers -- obtained via fine-tuning large LLMs or pre-training smaller models from scratch -- can reliably model both forward execution (predicting future states and outputs) and inverse execution (inferring prior states or inputs) conditioned on debugger actions. Evaluated on CruxEval, our models achieve strong performance on both output and input prediction tasks, demonstrating robust conditional execution modeling. Our work takes first steps towards future agentic coding systems in which neural debuggers serve as a world model for simulated debugging environments, providing execution feedback or enabling agents to interact with real debugging tools. This capability lays the foundation for more powerful code generation, program understanding, and automated debugging.

</details>


### [90] [Quantifying Memorization and Privacy Risks in Genomic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08913)
*Alexander Nemecek,Wenbiao Li,Xiaoqian Jiang,Jaideep Vaidya,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个全面的多向量隐私评估框架，用于量化基因组语言模型中的记忆风险，通过整合困惑度检测、金丝雀序列提取和成员推断三种方法，评估不同架构和训练机制下的记忆风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基因组语言模型越来越多地在敏感基因组队列上进行训练或微调，它们存在记忆训练数据中特定序列的风险，这引发了隐私、数据泄露和监管合规方面的严重担忧。目前缺乏针对基因组领域独特属性（如固定核苷酸字母表、强生物结构和个体可识别性）的系统性记忆风险评估。

Method: 提出了一个多向量隐私评估框架，整合了三种互补的风险评估方法：基于困惑度的检测、金丝雀序列提取和成员推断。通过将金丝雀序列以不同重复率植入合成和真实基因组数据集，精确量化重复率和训练动态对记忆的影响。在多个GLM架构上评估该框架，研究序列重复、模型容量和记忆风险之间的关系。

Result: GLMs表现出可测量的记忆现象，且记忆程度因架构和训练机制而异。研究发现没有单一的攻击向量能够捕捉记忆风险的全貌，强调了多向量隐私审计作为基因组AI系统标准实践的必要性。

Conclusion: 基因组语言模型存在可量化的记忆风险，需要采用多向量隐私评估框架进行系统性审计。该研究为基因组AI系统的隐私保护提供了重要的评估工具和方法论基础。

Abstract: Genomic language models (GLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for learning representations of DNA sequences, enabling advances in variant prediction, regulatory element identification, and cross-task transfer learning. However, as these models are increasingly trained or fine-tuned on sensitive genomic cohorts, they risk memorizing specific sequences from their training data, raising serious concerns around privacy, data leakage, and regulatory compliance. Despite growing awareness of memorization risks in general-purpose language models, little systematic evaluation exists for these risks in the genomic domain, where data exhibit unique properties such as a fixed nucleotide alphabet, strong biological structure, and individual identifiability. We present a comprehensive, multi-vector privacy evaluation framework designed to quantify memorization risks in GLMs. Our approach integrates three complementary risk assessment methodologies: perplexity-based detection, canary sequence extraction, and membership inference. These are combined into a unified evaluation pipeline that produces a worst-case memorization risk score. To enable controlled evaluation, we plant canary sequences at varying repetition rates into both synthetic and real genomic datasets, allowing precise quantification of how repetition and training dynamics influence memorization. We evaluate our framework across multiple GLM architectures, examining the relationship between sequence repetition, model capacity, and memorization risk. Our results establish that GLMs exhibit measurable memorization and that the degree of memorization varies across architectures and training regimes. These findings reveal that no single attack vector captures the full scope of memorization risk, underscoring the need for multi-vector privacy auditing as a standard practice for genomic AI systems.

</details>


### [91] [Hindsight Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08754)
*Hui-Ze Tan,Xiao-Wen Yang,Hao Chen,Jie-Jing Shao,Yi Wen,Yuteng Shen,Weihong Luo,Xiku Du,Lan-Zhe Guo,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HCAPO：首个将事后信用分配集成到LLM智能体中的框架，通过事后推理优化步级Q值估计，解决长视野任务中的信用分配问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长视野、多步骤任务中面临稀疏奖励带来的信用分配挑战，现有无价值方法（如GRPO）存在步级Q值估计不准确和中间状态价值基线错位两个瓶颈

Method: HCAPO利用LLM本身作为事后评论家，通过事后推理优化步级Q值；采用多尺度优势机制补充关键决策状态的不准确价值基线

Result: 在WebShop、ALFWorld等三个挑战性基准测试中，HCAPO始终优于最先进的RL方法。使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型时，在WebShop上成功率比GRPO提高7.7%，在ALFWorld上提高13.8%

Conclusion: HCAPO显著提升了探索效率，促进了简洁决策，并确保了在复杂长视野任务中的可扩展性，为LLM智能体的信用分配问题提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents often face significant credit assignment challenges in long-horizon, multi-step tasks due to sparse rewards. Existing value-free methods, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), encounter two fundamental bottlenecks: inaccurate step-level Q-value estimation and misaligned value baselines for intermediate states. To address these limitations, we introduce HCAPO, the first framework to integrate hindsight credit assignment into LLM agents. HCAPO leverages the LLM itself as a post-hoc critic to refine step-level Q-values through hindsight reasoning. Furthermore, HCAPO's multi-scale advantage mechanism effectively supplements the inaccurate value baselines at critical decision states. Evaluations across three challenging benchmarks, including WebShop and ALFWorld, demonstrate that HCAPO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art RL methods. Notably, HCAPO achieves a 7.7% improvement in success rate on WebShop and a 13.8% on ALFWorld over GRPO using the Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct model. These results indicate that HCAPO significantly enhances exploration efficiency, promotes concise decision-making, and ensures scalability in complex, long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [92] [Generalized Reduction to the Isotropy for Flexible Equivariant Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08758)
*Alejandro García-Castellanos,Gijs Bellaard,Remco Duits,Daniel Pelt,Erik J Bekkers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种将异构乘积空间上的群不变函数约简为各向同性子群作用的方法，建立了轨道等价关系，并将其应用于等变神经场以扩展其适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多几何学习问题需要在异构乘积空间（不同群作用的空间的乘积）上处理不变量，而标准技术无法直接应用。现有方法对等变神经场施加了主要的结构约束，需要一种更通用的框架来处理任意群作用和齐次条件空间。

Method: 当群G可迁地作用在空间M上时，证明任何在乘积空间X×M上的G-不变函数可以约简为M的各向同性子群H在X上的不变量。建立了显式的轨道等价关系(X×M)/G ≅ X/H，提供了一个保持表达能力的原理性约简方法。

Result: 建立了异构乘积空间上群不变函数的约简理论，将等变神经场扩展到任意群作用和齐次条件空间，移除了现有方法的主要结构约束。

Conclusion: 提出的轨道等价框架为处理异构乘积空间上的几何学习问题提供了理论基础，显著扩展了等变神经场的适用范围，使其能够处理更一般的群作用和条件空间。

Abstract: Many geometric learning problems require invariants on heterogeneous product spaces, i.e., products of distinct spaces carrying different group actions, where standard techniques do not directly apply. We show that, when a group $G$ acts transitively on a space $M$, any $G$-invariant function on a product space $X \times M$ can be reduced to an invariant of the isotropy subgroup $H$ of $M$ acting on $X$ alone. Our approach establishes an explicit orbit equivalence $(X \times M)/G \cong X/H$, yielding a principled reduction that preserves expressivity. We apply this characterization to Equivariant Neural Fields, extending them to arbitrary group actions and homogeneous conditioning spaces, and thereby removing the major structural constraints imposed by existing methods.

</details>


### [93] [SPREAD: Subspace Representation Distillation for Lifelong Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08763)
*Kaushik Roy,Giovanni D'urso,Nicholas Lawrance,Brendan Tidd,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPREAD：一种几何保持的终身模仿学习框架，通过奇异值分解对齐低秩子空间中的策略表示，结合置信度引导的蒸馏策略，有效缓解灾难性遗忘并提升知识迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终身模仿学习的关键挑战在于让智能体从专家演示中学习新技能的同时保留先验知识。现有基于L2范数特征匹配的蒸馏方法对噪声和高维变异性敏感，难以保持任务表示的内在几何结构。

Method: 提出SPREAD框架：1) 使用奇异值分解将策略表示对齐到低秩子空间，保持多模态特征的底层几何结构；2) 提出置信度引导的蒸馏策略，对前M个最置信的动作样本应用KL散度损失，强调可靠模式并提升优化稳定性。

Result: 在LIBERO终身模仿学习基准测试中，SPREAD显著提升了知识迁移能力，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，并实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SPREAD通过几何保持的特征对齐和置信度引导的蒸馏，为终身模仿学习提供了一种有效的解决方案，能够更好地保持任务表示的底层流形和几何结构，实现稳定迁移和泛化。

Abstract: A key challenge in lifelong imitation learning (LIL) is enabling agents to acquire new skills from expert demonstrations while retaining prior knowledge. This requires preserving the low-dimensional manifolds and geometric structures that underlie task representations across sequential learning. Existing distillation methods, which rely on L2-norm feature matching in raw feature space, are sensitive to noise and high-dimensional variability, often failing to preserve intrinsic task manifolds. To address this, we introduce SPREAD, a geometry-preserving framework that employs singular value decomposition (SVD) to align policy representations across tasks within low-rank subspaces. This alignment maintains the underlying geometry of multimodal features, facilitating stable transfer, robustness, and generalization. Additionally, we propose a confidence-guided distillation strategy that applies a Kullback-Leibler divergence loss restricted to the top-M most confident action samples, emphasizing reliable modes and improving optimization stability. Experiments on the LIBERO, lifelong imitation learning benchmark, show that SPREAD substantially improves knowledge transfer, mitigates catastrophic forgetting, and achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [94] [Multi-level meta-reinforcement learning with skill-based curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08773)
*Sichen Yang,Mauro Maggioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多级MDP压缩框架，通过分层策略将复杂任务分解为子任务，实现高效策略搜索和技能迁移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有自然多层结构的序列决策问题中，如何系统性地推断和利用层次结构这一长期挑战，通过分层压缩降低随机性、缩小策略搜索空间

Method: 开发多级MDP压缩程序：将低层策略族视为高层MDP中的单一动作，保持原始MDP语义和结构；高层MDP作为独立MDP求解；结合嵌入（问题特定）和技能（高阶函数）的因子分解；在课程学习框架中组织学习过程

Result: 多级表示解耦子任务，大幅减少不必要随机性和策略搜索空间，降低求解迭代次数和计算量；实现跨问题和跨层次的技能迁移；在MazeBase+等示例中展示抽象、可迁移性和课程学习效果

Conclusion: 该框架在温和假设下保证一致性，通过分层压缩和技能分解有效解决复杂MDP问题，促进跨任务和跨层次的迁移学习，为结构化序列决策提供系统化解决方案

Abstract: We consider problems in sequential decision making with natural multi-level structure, where sub-tasks are assembled together to accomplish complex goals. Systematically inferring and leveraging hierarchical structure has remained a longstanding challenge; we describe an efficient multi-level procedure for repeatedly compressing Markov decision processes (MDPs), wherein a parametric family of policies at one level is treated as single actions in the compressed MDPs at higher levels, while preserving the semantic meanings and structure of the original MDP, and mimicking the natural logic to address a complex MDP. Higher-level MDPs are themselves independent MDPs with less stochasticity, and may be solved using existing algorithms. As a byproduct, spatial or temporal scales may be coarsened at higher levels, making it more efficient to find long-term optimal policies. The multi-level representation delivered by this procedure decouples sub-tasks from each other and usually greatly reduces unnecessary stochasticity and the policy search space, leading to fewer iterations and computations when solving the MDPs. A second fundamental aspect of this work is that these multi-level decompositions plus the factorization of policies into embeddings (problem-specific) and skills (including higher-order functions) yield new transfer opportunities of skills across different problems and different levels. This whole process is framed within curriculum learning, wherein a teacher organizes the student agent's learning process in a way that gradually increases the difficulty of tasks and and promotes transfer across MDPs and levels within and across curricula. The consistency of this framework and its benefits can be guaranteed under mild assumptions. We demonstrate abstraction, transferability, and curriculum learning in examples, including MazeBase+, a more complex variant of the MazeBase example.

</details>


### [95] [SoftJAX & SoftTorch: Empowering Automatic Differentiation Libraries with Informative Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08824)
*Anselm Paulus,A. René Geist,Vít Musil,Sebastian Hoffmann,Onur Beker,Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftJAX和SoftTorch是开源、功能完整的软可微分编程库，为JAX和PyTorch中的硬原语提供软松弛替代方案，解决梯度为零或未定义的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动微分框架（如JAX和PyTorch）中的许多硬原语（如阈值处理、布尔逻辑、离散索引、排序操作）会产生零梯度或未定义梯度，不利于优化。虽然已有多种软松弛方法被提出，但实现分散在不同项目中，难以组合和比较。

Method: 开发SoftJAX和SoftTorch库，提供四类软函数作为硬原语的直接替代：(1)逐元素运算符（如clip、abs）；(2)通过模糊逻辑操作布尔值和索引的实用方法；(3)基于最优传输或置换多面体投影的轴操作（如排序、排名）；(4)完整的直通梯度估计支持。

Result: 创建了功能完整的开源库，使软松弛工具箱易于在可微分编程中使用，通过基准测试和实际案例研究验证了其有效性。代码已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: SoftJAX和SoftTorch将软松弛工具箱整合到统一库中，解决了硬原语梯度问题，为可微分编程提供了更丰富的工具集，促进了软松弛方法的可访问性和应用。

Abstract: Automatic differentiation (AD) frameworks such as JAX and PyTorch have enabled gradient-based optimization for a wide range of scientific fields. Yet, many "hard" primitives in these libraries such as thresholding, Boolean logic, discrete indexing, and sorting operations yield zero or undefined gradients that are not useful for optimization. While numerous "soft" relaxations have been proposed that provide informative gradients, the respective implementations are fragmented across projects, making them difficult to combine and compare. This work introduces SoftJAX and SoftTorch, open-source, feature-complete libraries for soft differentiable programming. These libraries provide a variety of soft functions as drop-in replacements for their hard JAX and PyTorch counterparts. This includes (i) elementwise operators such as clip or abs, (ii) utility methods for manipulating Booleans and indices via fuzzy logic, (iii) axiswise operators such as sort or rank -- based on optimal transport or permutahedron projections, and (iv) offer full support for straight-through gradient estimation. Overall, SoftJAX and SoftTorch make the toolbox of soft relaxations easily accessible to differentiable programming, as demonstrated through benchmarking and a practical case study. Code is available at github.com/a-paulus/softjax and github.com/a-paulus/softtorch.

</details>


### [96] [Expressivity-Efficiency Tradeoffs for Hybrid Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08859)
*John Cooper,Ilias Diakonikolas,Mingchen Ma,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了混合序列模型（Transformer与状态空间模型的结合）相对于单一模型的理论优势，证明了在某些合成任务上混合模型能以更小的参数和内存需求实现单一模型无法达到的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管混合序列模型（结合Transformer和状态空间模型）受到广泛关注，但缺乏对其优势和机制的基本理解。研究者希望明确混合模型在哪些场景下、通过何种机制优于其构成模型

Method: 通过理论分析研究一类核心合成任务，证明非混合模型（纯Transformer或纯状态空间模型）存在基本限制。针对选择性复制和关联回忆两个典型任务，构建小型混合模型并证明其可解性。同时进行实验验证，包括参数效率、长度泛化和分布外鲁棒性评估

Result: 理论证明：对于特定任务家族，非混合模型需要大量参数或工作内存；而混合模型能以小尺寸和小内存解决这些任务。实验验证：学习的混合模型在参数效率上优于非混合模型（最多6倍参数优势），且在长度泛化和分布外鲁棒性方面表现更强

Conclusion: 混合序列模型在理论上和实验上都展现出显著优势，能够结合Transformer的表达能力和状态空间模型的计算效率，实现"两全其美"。混合模型在参数效率、长度泛化和鲁棒性方面均优于单一模型，为序列建模提供了有前景的方向

Abstract: Hybrid sequence models--combining Transformer and state-space model layers--seek to gain the expressive versatility of attention as well as the computational efficiency of state-space model layers. Despite burgeoning interest in hybrid models, we lack a basic understanding of the settings where--and underlying mechanisms through which--they offer benefits over their constituent models. In this paper, we study this question, focusing on a broad family of core synthetic tasks. For this family of tasks, we prove the existence of fundamental limitations for non-hybrid models. Specifically, any Transformer or state-space model that solves the underlying task requires either a large number of parameters or a large working memory. On the other hand, for two prototypical tasks within this family--namely selective copying and associative recall--we construct hybrid models of small size and working memory that provably solve these tasks, thus achieving the best of both worlds. Our experimental evaluation empirically validates our theoretical findings. Importantly, going beyond the settings in our theoretical analysis, we empirically show that learned--rather than constructed--hybrids outperform non-hybrid models with up to 6x as many parameters. We additionally demonstrate that hybrid models exhibit stronger length generalization and out-of-distribution robustness than non-hybrids.

</details>


### [97] [A New Modeling to Feature Selection Based on the Fuzzy Rough Set Theory in Normal and Optimistic States on Hybrid Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08900)
*Mohammad Hossein Safarpour,Seyed Mohammad Alavi,Mohammad Izadikhah,Hossein Dibachi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FSbuHD特征选择模型，通过计算对象间组合距离推导模糊等价关系，将特征选择转化为优化问题，在高维大数据中提升效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大数据特征选择中模糊粗糙集理论的两个主要挑战：1) 高维空间中通过交集运算获取模糊等价关系耗时耗内存；2) 可能产生噪声数据使特征选择复杂化

Method: 提出FSbuHD特征选择模型：1) 计算对象间组合距离；2) 基于距离推导模糊等价关系；3) 将特征选择问题重构为优化问题，使用元启发式算法求解；4) 提供正常和乐观两种模式，基于两种不同的模糊等价关系

Result: 在UCI标准数据集上测试表明，FSbuHD相比先前方法和算法是最有效和高效的特征选择方法之一

Conclusion: FSbuHD模型成功解决了模糊粗糙集在高维特征选择中的计算效率和噪声问题，通过距离计算和优化重构提供了更有效的特征选择方案

Abstract: Considering the high volume, wide variety, and rapid speed of data generation, investigating feature selection methods for big data presents various applications and advantages. By removing irrelevant and redundant features, feature selection reduces data dimensions, thereby facilitating optimal decision-making within decision systems. One of the key tools for feature selection in hybrid information systems is fuzzy rough set theory. However, this theory faces two significant challenges: First, obtaining fuzzy equivalence relations through intersection operations in high-dimensional spaces can be both time-consuming and memory-intensive. Additionally, this method may produce noisy data, complicating the feature selection process. The purpose and innovation of this paper are to address these issues. We proposed a new feature selection model that calculates the combined distance between objects and subsequently used this information to derive the fuzzy equivalence relation. Rather than directly solving the feature selection problem, this approach reformulates it into an optimization problem that can be tackled using appropriate meta-heuristic algorithms. We have named this new approach FSbuHD. The FSbuHD model operates in two modes - normal and optimistic - based on the selection of one of the two introduced fuzzy equivalence relations. The model is then tested on standard datasets from the UCI repository and compared with other algorithms. The results of this research demonstrate that FSbuHD is one of the most efficient and effective methods for feature selection when compared to previous methods and algorithms.

</details>


### [98] [Cross-Domain Uncertainty Quantification for Selective Prediction: A Comprehensive Bound Ablation with Transfer-Informed Betting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08907)
*Abhinaba Basu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了九种选择性预测风险控制的有限样本界方法，提出了基于域迁移的Transfer-Informed Betting方法，在数据稀缺场景下显著提升覆盖率，并应用于智能体缓存系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性预测需要在有限样本下提供风险保证，现有方法在数据稀缺场景下性能受限。需要开发既能利用集中不等式和多测试校正，又能通过域迁移信息提升性能的新方法。

Method: 结合九种有限样本界方法：Hoeffding、Empirical Bernstein、Clopper-Pearson、Wasserstein DRO、CVaR等集中不等式，与union bound、Learn Then Test固定序列等多测试校正，以及基于赌博的置信序列(WSR)。提出Transfer-Informed Betting方法，利用源域风险配置文件热启动WSR财富过程。

Result: 在四个基准测试上评估：MASSIVE、NyayaBench、CLINC-150、Banking77。在MASSIVE上，LTT消除union-bound惩罚，在α=0.10时达到94.0%保证覆盖率，比Hoeffding的73.8%提升27%。在NyayaBench上，Transfer-Informed Betting在α=0.10时达到18.5%覆盖率，比LTT+Hoeffding提升5.4倍。与分拆一致性预测相比，选择性预测提供单预测风险保证而非预测集。

Conclusion: Transfer-Informed Betting通过域迁移信息热启动赌博过程，在数据稀缺场景下显著提升性能，具有形式化优势保证。该方法结合赌博置信序列、LTT单调测试和跨域迁移，是文献中未见的创新。应用于智能体缓存系统，建立了渐进信任模型。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive ablation of nine finite-sample bound families for selective prediction with risk control, combining concentration inequalities (Hoeffding, Empirical Bernstein, Clopper-Pearson, Wasserstein DRO, CVaR) with multiple-testing corrections (union bound, Learn Then Test fixed-sequence) and betting-based confidence sequences (WSR). Our main theoretical contribution is Transfer-Informed Betting (TIB), which warm-starts the WSR wealth process using a source domain's risk profile, achieving tighter bounds in data-scarce settings with a formal dominance guarantee. We prove that the TIB wealth process remains a valid supermartingale under all source-target divergences, that TIB dominates standard WSR when domains match, and that no data-independent warm-start can achieve better convergence. The combination of betting-based confidence sequences, LTT monotone testing, and cross-domain transfer is, to our knowledge, a three-way novelty not present in the literature. We evaluate all nine bound families on four benchmarks-MASSIVE (n=1,102), NyayaBench (n=280), CLINC-150 (n=22.5K), and Banking77 (n=13K)-across 18 (alpha, delta) configurations. On MASSIVE at alpha=0.10, LTT eliminates the ln(K) union-bound penalty, achieving 94.0% guaranteed coverage versus 73.8% for Hoeffding-a 27% relative improvement. On NyayaBench, where the small calibration set makes Hoeffding-family bounds infeasible below alpha=0.20, Transfer-Informed Betting achieves 18.5% coverage at alpha=0.10, a 5.4x improvement over LTT + Hoeffding. We additionally compare with split-conformal prediction, showing that conformal methods produce prediction sets (avg. 1.67 classes) whereas selective prediction provides single-prediction risk guarantees. We apply these methods to agentic caching systems, formalizing a progressive trust model where the guarantee determines when cached responses can be served autonomously.

</details>


### [99] [The $qs$ Inequality: Quantifying the Double Penalty of Mixture-of-Experts at Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08960)
*Vignesh Adhinarayanan,Nuwan Jayasena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE模型在训练时FLOPs效率高，但在推理时面临双重惩罚：专家路由导致微批次碎片化减少权重重用，以及大专家池占用HBM空间压缩KV缓存容量。这种重用碎片化现象使FFN进入带宽受限状态，尤其在长上下文场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究MoE模型在推理时效率下降的根本原因，特别是解码阶段的结构性劣势。虽然MoE在训练时能以较低FLOPs实现高质量，但这种效率优势在推理时往往消失，需要深入分析其架构层面的问题。

Method: 提出qs不等式作为预测准则，统一考虑稀疏度(s)和质量等价因子(q)。通过评估包括DeepSeek-V3、Qwen3-235B、Grok-1和Switch-C在内的前沿模型，验证重用碎片化是普遍存在的架构现象。分析不同上下文长度下的吞吐量表现。

Result: 评估显示重用碎片化是MoE架构的普遍现象。对于DeepSeek-V3在128k上下文长度下，质量匹配的稠密模型具有4.5倍的吞吐量优势。像Switch-C这样的大规模架构在集群规模上可能变得不可行，而质量匹配的稠密模型仍可行。

Conclusion: 训练时的FLOPs效率不能完全代表推理性能，特别是在长上下文服务场景下。MoE最好被视为训练时优化技术，通过蒸馏到稠密模型可能是实现推理高效部署的可行路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models deliver high quality at low training FLOPs, but this efficiency often vanishes at inference. We identify a double penalty that structurally disadvantages MoE architectures during decoding: first, expert routing fragments microbatches and reduces weight reuse; second, massive resident expert pools reduce high-bandwidth memory (HBM) headroom for the KV cache. This phenomenon, formalized as reuse fragmentation, pushes feed-forward networks (FFNs) into a bandwidth-bound regime, especially at long context lengths.
  We introduce the $qs$ inequality, a predictive criterion that identifies when MoE is structurally disadvantaged relative to a quality-matched dense model. This criterion unifies sparsity ($s$), the fraction of parameters activated per token, and the quality-equivalence factor ($q$), the size multiplier required for a dense model to match MoE performance. Our evaluation across frontier models including DeepSeek-V3, Qwen3-235B, Grok-1, and Switch-C demonstrates that this fragmentation is a general architectural phenomenon. For DeepSeek-V3 at 128k context, this results in a 4.5x throughput advantage for a quality-matched dense baseline. Crucially, massive architectures like Switch-C can become infeasible on cluster sizes where a quality-matched dense model remains viable.
  Our results suggest that training-time FLOP efficiency is an incomplete proxy for inference-time performance in long-context serving. They also indicate that MoE may be best viewed as a training-time optimization, with distillation into dense models as a possible path toward inference-efficient deployment.

</details>


### [100] [Semantic Level of Detail: Multi-Scale Knowledge Representation via Heat Kernel Diffusion on Hyperbolic Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08965)
*Edward Izgorodin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出语义细节层次(SLoD)框架，通过庞加莱球上的热核扩散实现连续缩放操作，自动检测知识图谱中的抽象层次边界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI记忆系统将知识组织为图结构，但缺乏连续分辨率控制的机制，无法确定抽象层次之间的定性边界以及智能体应如何在这些层次间导航

Method: 基于庞加莱球上的热核扩散定义连续缩放算子，在粗粒度时聚合嵌入为高层摘要，在细粒度时保留局部语义细节；证明层次一致性并利用图拉普拉斯算子的谱间隙自动检测尺度边界

Result: 在合成层次结构(HSBM)上，边界扫描器以ARI达1.00恢复植入层次；在完整WordNet名词层次(82K同义词集)上，检测到的边界与真实分类深度高度相关(τ=0.79)

Conclusion: SLoD框架为知识图谱提供了连续分辨率控制的理论基础，能够自动发现有意义的抽象层次，无需监督即可在真实世界知识图中导航

Abstract: AI memory systems increasingly organize knowledge into graph structures -- knowledge graphs, entity relations, community hierarchies -- yet lack a principled mechanism for continuous resolution control: where do the qualitative boundaries between abstraction levels lie, and how should an agent navigate them? We introduce Semantic Level of Detail (SLoD), a framework that answers both questions by defining a continuous zoom operator via heat kernel diffusion on the Poincaré ball $\mathbb{B}^d$. At coarse scales ($σ\to \infty$), diffusion aggregates embeddings into high-level summaries; at fine scales ($σ\to 0$), local semantic detail is preserved. We prove hierarchical coherence with bounded approximation error $O(σ)$ and $(1+\varepsilon)$ distortion for tree-structured hierarchies under Sarkar embedding. Crucially, we show that spectral gaps in the graph Laplacian induce emergent scale boundaries -- scales where the representation undergoes qualitative transitions -- which can be detected automatically without manual resolution parameters. On synthetic hierarchies (HSBM), our boundary scanner recovers planted levels with ARI up to 1.00, with detection degrading gracefully near the information-theoretic Kesten-Stigum threshold. On the full WordNet noun hierarchy (82K synsets), detected boundaries align with true taxonomic depth ($τ= 0.79$), demonstrating that the method discovers meaningful abstraction levels in real-world knowledge graphs without supervision.

</details>


### [101] [MAPLE: Elevating Medical Reasoning from Statistical Consensus to Process-Led Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08987)
*Kailong Fan,Anqi Pu,Yichen Wu,Wanhua Li,Yicong Li,Hanspeter Pfister,Huafeng Liu,Xiang Li,Quanzheng Li,Ning Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的训练范式，将医疗过程奖励模型与测试时强化学习结合，用专家对齐的细粒度监督替代多数投票，提升医疗AI系统的可靠性和可扩展性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准测试时强化学习依赖多数投票作为启发式监督信号，但在复杂医疗场景中，最频繁的推理路径不一定是临床正确的，需要更可靠的监督机制

Method: 提出统一训练范式，将医疗过程奖励模型与测试时强化学习结合，用Med-RPM提供细粒度、专家对齐的监督，替代传统的多数投票机制

Result: 在四个不同基准测试上的广泛评估表明，该方法在性能上持续且显著优于当前的测试时强化学习和独立的PRM选择方法

Conclusion: 从随机启发式方法转向结构化、逐步奖励对于开发可靠且可扩展的医疗AI系统至关重要，该方法成功将搜索式智能提炼到模型的参数化记忆中

Abstract: Recent advances in medical large language models have explored Test-Time Reinforcement Learning (TTRL) to enhance reasoning. However, standard TTRL often relies on majority voting (MV) as a heuristic supervision signal, which can be unreliable in complex medical scenarios where the most frequent reasoning path is not necessarily the clinically correct one. In this work, we propose a novel and unified training paradigm that integrates medical process reward models with TTRL to bridge the gap between test-time scaling (TTS) and parametric model optimization. Specifically, we advance the TTRL framework by replacing the conventional MV with a fine-grained, expert-aligned supervision paradigm using Med-RPM. This integration ensures that reinforcement learning is guided by medical correctness rather than mere consensus, effectively distilling search-based intelligence into the model's parametric memory. Extensive evaluations on four different benchmarks have demonstrated that our developed method consistently and significantly outperforms current TTRL and standalone PRM selection. Our findings establish that transitioning from stochastic heuristics to structured, step-wise rewards is essential for developing reliable and scalable medical AI systems

</details>


### [102] [The Coupling Within: Flow Matching via Distilled Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09014)
*David Berthelot,Tianrong Chen,Jiatao Gu,Marco Cuturi,Laurent Dinh,Bhavik Chandna,Michal Klein,Josh Susskind,Shuangfei Zhai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NFM：利用预训练归一化流的确定性耦合来训练学生流模型，超越独立耦合和最优传输耦合


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流匹配通常使用独立耦合，虽然最优传输耦合有所改进，但作者认为可以从预训练归一化流中提取更优的耦合关系，因为NF通过最大似然和可逆性要求天然建立了噪声与数据空间的双射关系

Method: 提出归一化流匹配(NFM)方法：利用自回归块预训练的NF模型提取准确定性耦合，然后蒸馏这些耦合来训练学生流模型，结合了NF的确定性映射和流模型的推理灵活性

Result: NFM训练的学生流模型显著优于使用独立耦合或最优传输耦合的流模型，甚至超越了作为教师的AR-NF模型，实现了两全其美

Conclusion: 通过从预训练NF模型中蒸馏耦合关系，NFM方法为流匹配训练提供了更优的耦合策略，在保持流模型推理灵活性的同时获得了更好的生成性能

Abstract: Flow models have rapidly become the go-to method for training and deploying large-scale generators, owing their success to inference-time flexibility via adjustable integration steps. A crucial ingredient in flow training is the choice of coupling measure for sampling noise/data pairs that define the flow matching (FM) regression loss. While FM training defaults usually to independent coupling, recent works show that adaptive couplings informed by noise/data distributions (e.g., via optimal transport, OT) improve both model training and inference. We radicalize this insight by shifting the paradigm: rather than computing adaptive couplings directly, we use distilled couplings from a different, pretrained model capable of placing noise and data spaces in bijection -- a property intrinsic to normalizing flows (NF) through their maximum likelihood and invertibility requirements. Leveraging recent advances in NF image generation via auto-regressive (AR) blocks, we propose Normalized Flow Matching (NFM), a new method that distills the quasi-deterministic coupling of pretrained NF models to train student flow models. These students achieve the best of both worlds: significantly outperforming flow models trained with independent or even OT couplings, while also improving on the teacher AR-NF model.

</details>


### [103] [Two Teachers Better Than One: Hardware-Physics Co-Guided Distributed Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09032)
*Yuchen Yuan,Junhuan Yang,Hao Wan,Yipei Liu,Hanhan Wu,Youzuo Lin,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EPIC是一个硬件和物理协同引导的分布式科学机器学习框架，通过轻量级本地编码和物理感知解码，显著降低通信成本同时保持物理保真度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习在实地处理、控制和监测中的应用面临广域传感、实时需求以及严格的能耗和可靠性约束，集中式实现不切实际。现有方法要么通信成本过高，要么分布式实现会破坏物理原理导致性能下降。

Method: EPIC采用硬件和物理协同引导的分布式框架，以全波形反演为代表任务。在终端设备上进行轻量级本地编码，在中央节点进行物理感知解码。通过传输紧凑的潜在特征而非高容量原始数据，并使用交叉注意力捕捉接收器间的波场耦合。

Result: 在包含5个终端设备和1个中央节点的分布式测试平台上，使用OpenFWI的10个数据集进行评估，EPIC将延迟降低8.9倍，通信能耗降低33.8倍，同时在10个数据集中的8个上提高了重建保真度。

Conclusion: EPIC框架有效解决了分布式科学机器学习中的通信成本与物理保真度权衡问题，通过硬件和物理协同设计实现了高效、准确的分布式处理。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning (SciML) is increasingly applied to in-field processing, controlling, and monitoring; however, wide-area sensing, real-time demands, and strict energy and reliability constraints make centralized SciML implementation impractical. Most SciML models assume raw data aggregation at a central node, incurring prohibitively high communication latency and energy costs; yet, distributing models developed for general-purpose ML often breaks essential physical principles, resulting in degraded performance. To address these challenges, we introduce EPIC, a hardware- and physics-co-guided distributed SciML framework, using full-waveform inversion (FWI) as a representative task. EPIC performs lightweight local encoding on end devices and physics-aware decoding at a central node. By transmitting compact latent features rather than high-volume raw data and by using cross-attention to capture inter-receiver wavefield coupling, EPIC significantly reduces communication cost while preserving physical fidelity. Evaluated on a distributed testbed with five end devices and one central node, and across 10 datasets from OpenFWI, EPIC reduces latency by 8.9$\times$ and communication energy by 33.8$\times$, while even improving reconstruction fidelity on 8 out of 10 datasets.

</details>


### [104] [SPAARS: Safer RL Policy Alignment through Abstract Exploration and Refined Exploitation of Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09378)
*Swaminathan S K,Aritra Hazra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPAARS是一个课程学习框架，用于离线到在线强化学习，通过先约束探索在低维潜在空间实现安全行为改进，然后无缝转移到原始动作空间以绕过解码器瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线到在线强化学习面临的核心挑战是如何在在线交互中安全探索而不偏离离线数据的行为支持。现有基于CVAE的方法存在"利用差距"问题，即解码器重建损失导致的性能上限。

Method: SPAARS采用课程学习框架：第一阶段将探索约束在低维潜在流形上，实现样本高效的安全行为改进；第二阶段无缝转移到原始动作空间，绕过解码器瓶颈。提供两种实现：基于CVAE的变体仅需无序(s,a)对；SPAARS-SUPE结合OPAL时序技能预训练，需要轨迹块但提供更强的探索结构。

Result: SPAARS-SUPE在kitchen-mixed-v0上达到0.825标准化回报，优于SUPE的0.75，样本效率提高5倍；独立SPAARS在hopper-medium-v2和walker2d-medium-v2上分别达到92.7和102.9标准化回报，显著超过IQL基线的66.3和78.3。

Conclusion: SPAARS通过课程学习框架有效解决了离线到在线强化学习中的安全探索问题，既保持了样本效率，又避免了基于CVAE方法的利用差距，在多个基准测试中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising paradigm for robotics by pre-training policies on safe, offline demonstrations and fine-tuning them via online interaction. However, a fundamental challenge remains: how to safely explore online without deviating from the behavioral support of the offline data? While recent methods leverage conditional variational autoencoders (CVAEs) to bound exploration within a latent space, they inherently suffer from an exploitation gap -- a performance ceiling imposed by the decoder's reconstruction loss. We introduce SPAARS, a curriculum learning framework that initially constrains exploration to the low-dimensional latent manifold for sample-efficient, safe behavioral improvement, then seamlessly transfers control to the raw action space, bypassing the decoder bottleneck. SPAARS has two instantiations: the CVAE-based variant requires only unordered (s,a) pairs and no trajectory segmentation; SPAARS-SUPE pairs SPAARS with OPAL temporal skill pretraining for stronger exploration structure at the cost of requiring trajectory chunks. We prove an upper bound on the exploitation gap using the Performance Difference Lemma, establish that latent-space policy gradients achieve provable variance reduction over raw-space exploration, and show that concurrent behavioral cloning during the latent phase directly controls curriculum transition stability. Empirically, SPAARS-SUPE achieves 0.825 normalized return on kitchen-mixed-v0 versus 0.75 for SUPE, with 5x better sample efficiency; standalone SPAARS achieves 92.7 and 102.9 normalized return on hopper-medium-v2 and walker2d-medium-v2 respectively, surpassing IQL baselines of 66.3 and 78.3 respectively, confirming the utility of the unordered-pair CVAE instantiation.

</details>


### [105] [Dynamic Multi-period Experts for Online Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09062)
*Seungha Hong,Sukang Chae,Suyeon Kim,Sanghwan Jang,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DynaME框架通过区分循环漂移和新兴漂移，使用动态专家委员会处理循环模式，用通用专家处理新出现模式，显著提升在线时间序列预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线时间序列预测方法将概念漂移视为单一现象，无法有效区分不同类型的漂移模式，限制了模型对时间序列动态变化的适应能力。
