By linearizing the McKean-Vlasov dynamics and probing them in Fourier space, we reveal frequency-dependent convergence timescales comparable to \emph{Landau damping} in plasma kinetic theory: the Gaussian kernel suffers an exponential high-frequency bottleneck, explaining the empirical preference for the Laplacian kernel. We also propose an exponential bandwidth annealing schedule $σ(t)=σ_0 e^{-rt}$ that reduces convergence time from $\exp(O(K_{\max}^2))$ to $O(\log K_{\max})$. Finally, by formalizing drifting as a Wasserstein gradient flow of the smoothed KL divergence, we prove that the stop-gradient operator is derived directly from the frozen-field discretization mandated by the JKO scheme, and removing it severs training from any gradient-flow guarantee. This variational perspective further provides a general template for constructing novel drift operators, demonstrated with a Sinkhorn divergence drift.

</details>


### [146] [SignalMC-MED: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Biosignal Foundation Models on Single-Lead ECG and PPG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09940)
*Fredrik K. Gustafsson,Xiao Gu,Mattia Carletti,Patitapaban Palo,David W. Eyre,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SignalMC-MED是一个用于评估生物信号基础模型在同步单导联心电图和光电容积脉搏波数据上的基准测试，包含22,256次就诊记录和20个临床相关任务，评估发现领域特定模型优于通用时序模型，多模态融合优于单模态输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生物信号基础模型在多样化临床预测任务中表现出潜力，但缺乏对长时程多模态数据的系统性评估。需要建立一个标准化的基准来评估生物信号基础模型在真实临床环境中的性能。

Method: 基于MC-MED数据集构建SignalMC-MED基准，包含22,256次就诊的10分钟重叠ECG和PPG信号，涵盖20个临床相关任务。系统性评估代表性时序模型和生物信号基础模型在ECG-only、PPG-only和ECG+PPG三种设置下的性能。

Result: 领域特定的生物信号基础模型始终优于通用时序模型；多模态ECG+PPG融合比单模态输入表现更好；使用完整10分钟信号优于较短片段；较大模型变体并不总是优于较小模型；手工制作的ECG领域特征提供了强大的基线，并与学习到的特征表示具有互补价值。

Conclusion: SignalMC-MED为生物信号基础模型的评估提供了标准化基准，并为实际部署提供了实用指导：领域特定模型、多模态融合和完整信号长度的重要性，以及手工特征与学习表示的互补性。

Abstract: Recent biosignal foundation models (FMs) have demonstrated promising performance across diverse clinical prediction tasks, yet systematic evaluation on long-duration multimodal data remains limited. We introduce SignalMC-MED, a benchmark for evaluating biosignal FMs on synchronized single-lead electrocardiogram (ECG) and photoplethysmogram (PPG) data. Derived from the MC-MED dataset, SignalMC-MED comprises 22,256 visits with 10-minute overlapping ECG and PPG signals, and includes 20 clinically relevant tasks spanning prediction of demographics, emergency department disposition, laboratory value regression, and detection of prior ICD-10 diagnoses. Using this benchmark, we perform a systematic evaluation of representative time-series and biosignal FMs across ECG-only, PPG-only, and ECG + PPG settings. We find that domain-specific biosignal FMs consistently outperform general time-series models, and that multimodal ECG + PPG fusion yields robust improvements over unimodal inputs. Moreover, using the full 10-minute signal consistently outperforms shorter segments, and larger model variants do not reliably outperform smaller ones. Hand-crafted ECG domain features provide a strong baseline and offer complementary value when combined with learned FM representations. Together, these results establish SignalMC-MED as a standardized benchmark and provide practical guidance for evaluating and deploying biosignal FMs.

</details>


### [147] [Task Aware Modulation Using Representation Learning for Upsaling of Terrestrial Carbon Fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09974)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAM-RL框架通过结合时空表征学习和基于碳平衡方程的知识引导架构，显著提升了全球碳通量估算的准确性和可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估算陆地碳通量对全球碳预算至关重要，但现有数据驱动方法由于地面测量数据稀疏且分布不均，难以泛化到未观测区域，导致系统性区域偏差和高预测不确定性。

Method: 提出了任务感知调制与表征学习(TAM-RL)框架，将时空表征学习与基于碳平衡方程的知识引导编码器-解码器架构和损失函数相结合。

Result: 在150多个代表不同生物群落和气候区的通量塔站点上，TAM-RL相比现有最先进数据集，RMSE降低8-9.6%，解释方差从19.4%提升至43.8%，具体取决于目标通量类型。

Conclusion: 将物理基础约束与自适应表征学习相结合，能够显著增强全球碳通量估算的鲁棒性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Accurately upscaling terrestrial carbon fluxes is central to estimating the global carbon budget, yet remains challenging due to the sparse and regionally biased distribution of ground measurements. Existing data-driven upscaling products often fail to generalize beyond observed domains, leading to systematic regional biases and high predictive uncertainty. We introduce Task-Aware Modulation with Representation Learning (TAM-RL), a framework that couples spatio-temporal representation learning with knowledge-guided encoder-decoder architecture and loss function derived from the carbon balance equation. Across 150+ flux tower sites representing diverse biomes and climate regimes, TAM-RL improves predictive performance relative to existing state-of-the-art datasets, reducing RMSE by 8-9.6% and increasing explained variance ($R^2$) from 19.4% to 43.8%, depending on the target flux. These results demonstrate that integrating physically grounded constraints with adaptive representation learning can substantially enhance the robustness and transferability of global carbon flux estimates.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Symbolic Discovery of Stochastic Differential Equations with Genetic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09597)
*Sigur de Vries,Sander W. Keemink,Marcel A. J. van Gerven*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出一种基于遗传编程的随机微分方程符号发现方法，联合优化漂移和扩散函数，扩展了符号回归在随机动力系统中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号回归主要关注常微分方程，而噪声通常被视为恢复确定性动力学的障碍。然而，在随机微分方程中显式学习噪声分量的符号函数可以增强建模能力、增加知识获取并实现生成采样。

Method: 基于遗传编程的随机微分方程符号发现方法，通过最大似然估计联合优化漂移和扩散函数。

Result: 方法能够准确恢复控制方程，高效扩展到高维系统，对稀疏采样问题具有鲁棒性，并能推广到随机偏微分方程。

Conclusion: 该工作将符号回归扩展到随机动力系统的可解释发现，为在噪声和动态世界中自动化科学发现做出贡献。

Abstract: Automated scientific discovery aims to improve scientific understanding through machine learning. A central approach in this field is symbolic regression, which uses genetic programming or sparse regression to learn interpretable mathematical expressions to explain observed data. Conventionally, the focus of symbolic regression is on identifying ordinary differential equations. The general view is that noise only complicates the recovery of deterministic dynamics. However, explicitly learning a symbolic function of the noise component in stochastic differential equations enhances modelling capacity, increases knowledge gain and enables generative sampling. We introduce a method for symbolic discovery of stochastic differential equations based on genetic programming, jointly optimizing drift and diffusion functions via the maximum likelihood estimate. Our results demonstrate accurate recovery of governing equations, efficient scaling to higher-dimensional systems, robustness to sparsely sampled problems and generalization to stochastic partial differential equations. This work extends symbolic regression toward interpretable discovery of stochastic dynamical systems, contributing to the automation of science in a noisy and dynamic world.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Test-Driven AI Agent Definition (TDAD): Compiling Tool-Using Agents from Behavioral Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08806)
*Tzafrir Rehan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TDAD是一种将AI智能体提示视为编译产物的方法，通过测试驱动开发确保智能体行为符合规范，包含可见/隐藏测试分割、语义突变测试和规范演化场景三大机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体开发实践缺乏可测量的行为合规性保证，小提示改动会导致静默回归，工具误用难以检测，策略违规仅在部署后显现，需要系统化方法来确保智能体行为符合规范。

Method: TDAD将智能体提示视为编译产物：工程师提供行为规范，编码智能体将其转换为可执行测试，第二个编码智能体迭代优化提示直到测试通过。引入三大机制：可见/隐藏测试分割、语义突变测试（生成合理错误提示变体并测量测试套件检测能力）、规范演化场景（量化需求变更时的回归安全性）。

Result: 在SpecSuite-Core基准测试（包含策略合规、基础分析、运行手册遵循和确定性执行四个深度规范智能体）上，TDAD在24次独立试验中实现92% v1编译成功率，97%平均隐藏测试通过率；演化规范编译成功率58%，大多数失败运行通过除1-2个外的所有可见测试；显示86-100%突变分数，78% v2隐藏通过率，97%回归安全分数。

Conclusion: TDAD为AI智能体开发提供了系统化、可测量的行为合规保证方法，通过测试驱动开发有效减少规范规避问题，提高智能体部署的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: We present Test-Driven AI Agent Definition (TDAD), a methodology that treats agent prompts as compiled artifacts: engineers provide behavioral specifications, a coding agent converts them into executable tests, and a second coding agent iteratively refines the prompt until tests pass. Deploying tool-using LLM agents in production requires measurable behavioral compliance that current development practices cannot provide. Small prompt changes cause silent regressions, tool misuse goes undetected, and policy violations emerge only after deployment. To mitigate specification gaming, TDAD introduces three mechanisms: (1) visible/hidden test splits that withhold evaluation tests during compilation, (2) semantic mutation testing via a post-compilation agent that generates plausible faulty prompt variants, with the harness measuring whether the test suite detects them, and (3) spec evolution scenarios that quantify regression safety when requirements change. We evaluate TDAD on SpecSuite-Core, a benchmark of four deeply-specified agents spanning policy compliance, grounded analytics, runbook adherence, and deterministic enforcement. Across 24 independent trials, TDAD achieves 92% v1 compilation success with 97% mean hidden pass rate; evolved specifications compile at 58%, with most failed runs passing all visible tests except 1-2, and show 86-100% mutation scores, 78% v2 hidden pass rate, and 97% regression safety scores. The implementation is available as an open benchmark at https://github.com/f-labs-io/tdad-paper-code.

</details>


### [150] [Declarative Scenario-based Testing with RoadLogic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09455)
*Ezio Bartocci,Alessio Gambi,Felix Gigler,Cristinel Mateis,Dejan Ničković*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RoadLogic：连接声明式OS2场景规范与可执行仿真的开源工具，使用ASP生成满足约束的抽象计划，通过运动规划细化为可行轨迹，实现自动驾驶系统场景化测试


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶场景测试方法依赖命令式场景定义，需要手动枚举大量变体以实现覆盖。声明式语言（如OpenSCENARIO DSL）提高了抽象层次，但缺乏将规范实例化为具体仿真的系统方法，目前没有开源解决方案提供此能力

Method: RoadLogic采用三层方法：1）使用答案集编程（ASP）生成满足场景约束的抽象计划；2）通过运动规划将抽象计划细化为可行轨迹；3）基于规范的监控验证正确性，支持参数采样捕获行为变体

Result: 在CommonRoad框架中实例化代表性OS2场景的评估显示，RoadLogic能在数分钟内一致地生成现实、满足规范的仿真，通过参数采样捕获多样行为变体

Conclusion: RoadLogic填补了声明式场景规范与可执行仿真之间的空白，为自动驾驶系统提供了系统化的场景测试方法，开启了基于场景的系统化测试之门

Abstract: Scenario-based testing is a key method for cost-effective and safe validation of autonomous vehicles (AVs). Existing approaches rely on imperative scenario definitions, requiring developers to manually enumerate numerous variants to achieve coverage. Declarative languages, such as OpenSCENARIO DSL (OS2), raise the abstraction level but lack systematic methods for instantiating concrete, specification-compliant scenarios as simulations. To our knowledge, currently, no open-source solution provides this capability.
  We present RoadLogic that bridges declarative OS2 specifications and executable simulations. It uses Answer Set Programming to generate abstract plans satisfying scenario constraints, motion planning to refine the plans into feasible trajectories, and specification-based monitoring to verify correctness.
  We evaluate RoadLogic on instantiating representative OS2 scenarios as simulations in the CommonRoad framework. Results show that RoadLogic consistently produces realistic, specification-satisfying simulations within minutes and captures diverse behavioral variants through parameter sampling, thus opening the door to systematic scenario-based testing for autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [151] [GenAI Is No Silver Bullet for Qualitative Research in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08951)
*Neil A. Ernst,Christoph Treude*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式AI在软件工程定性研究中的应用，分析其潜力与局限，强调需要根据具体研究策略和数据特性进行适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型和生成式AI的发展，出现了AI可以自动化定性分析的过度概括性主张。本文旨在澄清这些误解，为研究者提供关于GenAI辅助定性研究的承诺与陷阱的全面理解。

Method: 通过梳理软件工程中定性研究的维度，回顾GenAI辅助的实证证据，分析GenAI介导的定性研究实践的利弊，并在GenAI背景下重新审视定性研究质量因素。

Result: 研究发现GenAI支持需要根据具体数据和特定研究策略特征进行精心适配，不能一概而论。当前关于AI自动化定性分析的主张过于概括化，忽视了定性研究的多样性和复杂性。

Conclusion: GenAI在软件工程定性研究中具有应用潜力，但需要谨慎使用。未来需要进一步推进对其在软件工程中应用的理解，帮助研究者明智地利用这些工具，同时保持研究的严谨性和质量。

Abstract: Qualitative research gives rich insights into the quintessentially human aspects of software engineering as a socio-technical system. Qualitative research spans diverse strategies and methods, from interpretivist, in situ observational field studies, to deductive coding of data from mining studies. Advances in large language models and generative AI (GenAI) have prompted claims that artificial intelligence could automate qualitative analysis. Such claims are overgeneralizing from narrow successes. GenAI support must be carefully adapted to the data of interest, but also to the characteristics of a particular research strategy. In this Frontiers of SE paper, we discuss the emerging use of GenAI in relation to the broad spectrum of qualitative research in software engineering. We outline the dimensions of qualitative work in software engineering, review emerging empirical evidence for GenAI assistance, examine the pros and cons of GenAI-mediated qualitative research practices, and revisit qualitative research quality factors, in light of GenAI. Our goal is to inform researchers about the promises and pitfalls of GenAI-assisted qualitative research. We conclude with future plans to advance understanding of its use in software engineering.

</details>


### [152] [Automating Detection and Root-Cause Analysis of Flaky Tests in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09029)
*Janakan Sivaloganathan,Ainaz Jamshidi,Andriy Miranskyy,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种自动化流水线，用于检测量子软件仓库中的flaky测试问题，利用LLMs进行flakiness分类和根因识别，将原始数据集扩大了54%，最佳模型F1分数达到0.94以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件系统因其固有的概率性输出容易产生flaky测试（测试结果不一致），这会掩盖真实缺陷并降低开发效率，但目前缺乏系统化的检测和诊断工具。

Method: 基于先前对14个量子软件仓库的手动分析，自动化发现flaky测试用例（使用LLMs和余弦相似度），并评估多种LLMs（OpenAI GPT、Meta LLaMA、Google Gemini、Anthropic Claude）在flakiness分类和根因识别方面的性能。

Result: 识别出25个先前未知的flaky测试，将原始数据集大小增加了54%；最佳模型Google Gemini在flakiness检测上获得F1分数0.9420，在根因识别上获得0.9643。

Conclusion: LLMs能够为量子软件中的flaky报告分类和根因理解提供实际支持；扩展的数据集和自动化流水线为量子软件工程社区提供了可重用工具；未来工作将改进检测鲁棒性和探索flaky测试的自动修复。

Abstract: Like classical software, quantum software systems rely on automated testing. However, their inherently probabilistic outputs make them susceptible to quantum flakiness -- tests that pass or fail inconsistently without code changes. Such quantum flaky tests can mask real defects and reduce developer productivity, yet systematic tooling for their detection and diagnosis remains limited.
  This paper presents an automated pipeline to detect flaky-test-related issues and pull requests in quantum software repositories and to support the identification of their root causes. We aim to expand an existing quantum flaky test dataset and evaluate the capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) for flakiness classification and root-cause identification.
  Building on a prior manual analysis of 14 quantum software repositories, we automate the discovery of additional flaky test cases using LLMs and cosine similarity. We further evaluate a variety of LLMs from OpenAI GPT, Meta LLaMA, Google Gemini, and Anthropic Claude suites for classifying flakiness and identifying root causes from issue descriptions and code context. Classification performance is assessed using standard performance metrics, including F1-score.
  Using our pipeline, we identify 25 previously unknown flaky tests, increasing the original dataset size by 54%. The best-performing model, Google Gemini, achieves an F1-score of 0.9420 for flakiness detection and 0.9643 for root-cause identification, demonstrating that LLMs can provide practical support for triaging flaky reports and understanding their underlying causes in quantum software.
  The expanded dataset and automated pipeline provide reusable artifacts for the quantum software engineering community. Future work will focus on improving detection robustness and exploring automated repair of quantum flaky tests.

</details>


### [153] [The Future of Software Engineering Conferences: A New Zealand Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09035)
*Kelly Blincoe,Sherlock A. Licorish,Judith Fuchs,Amjed Tahir*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文分析了新西兰等地理偏远地区研究人员参与软件工程国际会议的障碍，并提出促进全球公平参与的策略建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程会议对知识交流和合作至关重要，但新西兰等地理偏远地区的研究人员面临显著参与障碍，包括高昂的旅行成本、学术日历不匹配和有限代表性，这影响了全球研究社区的公平性和活力。

Method: 通过识别和分析地理偏远地区研究人员参与软件工程会议的具体障碍（如高旅行成本、学术日历错位、有限代表性），并提出相应的策略方案，包括混合参与模式、成本敏感型会议选址和治理改革等。

Result: 识别出三大主要障碍：1）高旅行成本；2）学术日历不匹配；3）有限代表性。提出了具体策略：混合参与模式、成本敏感型会议选址、治理改革等，以促进全球公平参与。

Conclusion: 软件工程会议需要通过实施混合参与、成本敏感选址和治理改革等策略来降低地理偏远地区研究人员的参与障碍，从而促进全球公平参与并加强软件工程研究社区的多样性和活力。

Abstract: Software engineering (SE) conferences are vital for knowledge exchange and collaboration, yet can also involve significant barriers for researchers in geographically distant regions such as New Zealand. We identify barriers such as high travel costs, misaligned academic calendars, and limited representation, and propose strategies including hybrid participation, cost-conscious venues, and governance reforms. We make recommendations to promote equitable global participation and strengthen the SE research community.

</details>


### [154] [Class Model Generation from Requirements using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09100)
*Jackson Nguyen,Rui En Koe,Fanyu Wang,Chetan Arora,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估了GPT-5、Claude Sonnet 4.0、Gemini 2.5 Flash Thinking和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct等先进大语言模型从自然语言需求自动生成UML类图的能力，提出了结合LLM-as-a-Judge和人工评估的双重验证框架，在八个异构数据集上测试了模型生成的完整度、正确性、标准符合性、可理解性和术语一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的出现为自动化传统上需要大量人工工作的软件工程活动提供了新机会。类图生成是软件设计中关键但资源密集的阶段，本研究旨在探索先进LLMs从自然语言需求自动生成UML类图的能力，评估其有效性和可靠性。

Method: 提出了全面的双重验证框架，结合LLM-as-a-Judge方法和人工评估。使用八个异构数据集，应用思维链提示提取领域实体、属性和关联，生成相应的PlantUML表示。通过五个质量维度评估生成模型：完整度、正确性、标准符合性、可理解性和术语一致性。使用两个独立的LLM法官(Grok和Mistral)进行结构化成对比较，并与专家评估进行验证。

Result: 研究结果表明，LLMs能够生成结构连贯且语义有意义的UML图，与人类评估者达成显著一致。LLM基评估与人工评估之间的一致性表明，LLMs不仅可作为建模助手，还可作为自动化需求工程工作流中的可靠评估者。

Conclusion: LLMs在UML类图自动化方面展现出潜力，可作为建模助手和可靠评估者。LLM基评估与人工评估的一致性验证了其在自动化需求工程工作流中的实用性，为LLM驱动的UML类图自动化提供了能力和局限性的实践见解。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs) has opened new opportunities to automate software engineering activities that traditionally require substantial manual effort. Among these, class diagram generation represents a critical yet resource-intensive phase in software design. This paper investigates the capabilities of state-of-the-art LLMs, including GPT-5, Claude Sonnet 4.0, Gemini 2.5 Flash Thinking, and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, to generate UML class diagrams from natural language requirements automatically. To evaluate the effectiveness and reliability of LLM-based model generation, we propose a comprehensive dual-validation framework that integrates an LLM-as-a-Judge methodology with human-in-the-loop assessment. Using eight heterogeneous datasets, we apply chain-of-thought prompting to extract domain entities, attributes, and associations, generating corresponding PlantUML representations. The resulting models are evaluated across five quality dimensions: completeness, correctness, conformance to standards, comprehensibility, and terminological alignment. Two independent LLM judges (Grok and Mistral) perform structured pairwise comparisons, and their judgments are further validated against expert evaluations. Our results demonstrate that LLMs can generate structurally coherent and semantically meaningful UML diagrams, achieving substantial alignment with human evaluators. The consistency observed between LLM-based and human-based assessments highlights the potential of LLMs not only as modeling assistants but also as reliable evaluators in automated requirements engineering workflows, offering practical insights into the capabilities and limitations of LLM-driven UML class diagram automation.

</details>


### [155] [Can ChatGPT Generate Realistic Synthetic System Requirement Specifications? Results of a Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09335)
*Alex R. Mattukat,Florian M. Braun,Horst Lichter*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 使用ChatGPT生成合成系统需求规格说明(SyRS)，通过系统化提示工程和LLM质量评估，生成300个跨10个行业的SSyRS，62%专家认为真实，但存在矛盾陈述和缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实系统需求规格说明(SyRS)因专有性或保密性难以获取，限制了相关研究。合成SyRS(SSyRS)可缓解数据稀缺问题。大型语言模型(LLM)如ChatGPT具有自然语言生成能力，但存在幻觉和过度自信问题，本研究探索能否在不接触真实SyRS的情况下生成真实的SSyRS。

Method: 采用探索性研究设计，结合系统化方法：利用提示模式、基于LLM的质量评估和迭代提示优化。使用ChatGPT生成300个SSyRS，覆盖10个不同行业。通过跨模型检查和专家研究(n=87份问卷)进行评估。

Result: 62%的专家认为生成的SSyRS是真实的。然而，深入检查发现存在矛盾陈述和缺陷。总体上，能在一定程度上用ChatGPT生成真实的SSyRS，但基于LLM的质量评估无法完全替代专家评估。

Conclusion: 尽管存在幻觉和过度自信问题，ChatGPT能在一定程度上生成真实的合成系统需求规格说明。但LLM质量评估有局限性，不能完全替代专家评估。研究提供了方法论和关键见解，对使用LLM生成技术文档具有参考价值。

Abstract: System requirement specifications (SyRSs) are central, natural-language (NL) artifacts. Access to real SyRS for research purposes is highly valuable but limited by proprietary restrictions or confidentiality concerns. Generating synthetic SyRSs (SSyRSs) can address this scarcity. Black-box large language models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT offer compelling generation capabilities by providing easy access to NL generation functions without requiring access to real data. However, LLMs suffer from hallucinations and overconfidence, which pose major challenges in their use. We designed an exploratory study to investigate whether, despite these challenges, we can generate realistic SSyRSs with ChatGPT without having access to real SyRSs. Using a systematic approach that leverages prompt patterns, LLM-based quality assessments, and iterative prompt refinements, we generated 300 SSyRSs across 10 industries with ChatGPT. The results were evaluated using cross-model checks and an expert study, with n=87 submitted surveys. 62\% of experts considered the SSyRSs to be realistic. However, in-depth examination revealed contradictory statements and deficiencies. Overall, we were able to generate realistic SSyRSs to a certain extent with ChatGPT, but LLM-based quality assessments cannot fully replace thorough expert evaluations. This paper presents the methodology and results of our study and discusses the key insights we obtained.

</details>


### [156] [Experience Report on the Adaptable Integration of Requirements Engineering Courses into Curricula for Professionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09467)
*Oleksandr Kosenkov,Konstantin Blaschke,Tony Gorschek,Michael Unterkalmsteiner,Oleksandr Adamov,Davide Fucci*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了如何将需求工程课程系统有效地整合到面向专业人士的软件工程教育课程中，提出了基于课程内容映射的系统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于软件行业需求增长、技术快速演进以及实践所需技能变化，专业人士对软件工程教育的需求日益增加。然而，将需求工程课程整合到面向专业人士的课程体系中面临挑战，因为这些课程具有需求不同、更动态、模块化等特点。

Method: 基于开发三个面向专业人士的软件工程教育课程的经验，提出了整合需求工程课程的系统方法，重点关注课程内容映射，并制定了基本整合原则。

Result: 研究提出了将需求工程课程整合到专业人士软件工程教育课程中的基本原则，并开发了以课程内容映射为核心的系统方法。

Conclusion: 通过实践经验总结，为专业人士软件工程教育中需求工程课程的整合提供了系统方法和指导原则，有助于应对专业人士教育的特殊挑战。

Abstract: There is a growing demand for software engineering education (SEE) for professionals because of the increasing demand, active evolution of the technological landscape, and changes in the skills required by the practice. Integrating requirements engineering (RE) courses into SEE curricula for professionals systematically and effectively is challenging. In particular, curricula for professionals have different demands, are more dynamic, and modular in nature. In this study, we report on our experience in the development of three SEE curricula for professionals and the integration of RE courses into such curricula. We suggest basic principles for such integration and describe the systematic approach focused on course content mapping that we have developed.

</details>


### [157] [Towards Viewpoint-centric Artifact-based Regulatory Requirements Engineering for Compliance by Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09492)
*Oleksandr Kosenkov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出AM4RRE模型以系统化整合法规要求工程到软件开发中，解决法规复杂性带来的合规设计挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法规数量和复杂性不断增加，行业面临将法规要求整合到软件开发生命周期的压力，但现有方法缺乏实证理解，需要解决法律知识密集性、跨职能沟通和一致性等挑战

Method: 提出Artefact Model for Regulatory Requirements Engineering (AM4RRE)模型，旨在实现集成化的合规设计，目前处于合成和未来评估阶段

Result: 研究发现法规要求工程具有区别于其他要求的特殊性，组织通常在组织层面建立独立的法规RE流程，但开发团队往往以临时方式处理合规设计，缺乏系统性方法

Conclusion: AM4RRE模型旨在促进法规要求工程的系统整合，希望引发相关讨论并帮助完善博士研究的最终阶段计划

Abstract: Processing regulations and resulting requirements to achieve regulatory compliance in software engineering (SE) is a developing challenge due to the continuously growing amount, complexity, and expanding scope of regulations. Despite the growing amount of newly suggested regulatory requirements engineering (RE) approaches by the research community, industry remains under pressure to assure their integration into their RE and overall software development life cycle (SDLC) practices to facilitate a seamless and legally valid compliance by design. As of today, we still have limited empirical understanding of how this can be achieved. Such integration should avoid additional burdens and address the demands of legal knowledge intensity, cross-functional communication and consistency between different involved viewpoints. Intermediary results of this doctoral study showed that regulatory RE has peculiarities distinguishing it from the engineering of other requirements. Oftentimes, organizations establish standalone regulatory RE processes on the organizational level. However, software development teams usually approach compliance by design in an ad-hoc manner, rather than in a systematic way. Among other, because of the complexity of the coordination between the involved viewpoints. The goal of this paper is to report and get feedback about the synthesis and future evaluation of our Artefact Model for Regulatory Requirements Engineering (AM4RRE) for a integrated compliance by design. We hope this paper will spark discussions about regulatory RE and help us refine plans for the final stage of the doctoral study.

</details>


### [158] [EmbC-Test: How to Speed Up Embedded Software Testing Using LLMs and RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09497)
*Maximilian Harnot,Sebastian Komarnicki,Michal Polok,Timo Oksanen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于检索增强生成（RAG）的管道，用于嵌入式C软件测试的部分自动化，通过项目特定文档减少幻觉，提高测试生成质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式C软件的手动测试开发耗时费力且难以扩展，随着软件发布周期加快，验证成为嵌入式开发流程的瓶颈，需要自动化解决方案

Method: 采用检索增强生成（RAG）管道，将大型语言模型与项目特定文档（如需求、设计文档、代码库）相结合，通过检索相关上下文来增强测试生成，减少幻觉并提高项目对齐

Result: 工业评估显示：生成的测试100%语法正确，85%通过运行时验证；相比手动测试编写可节省高达66%的测试时间，每小时可生成270个测试

Conclusion: RAG管道能有效自动化嵌入式软件验证过程，显著提高测试效率和质量，具有实际工业应用价值

Abstract: Manual development of automatic tests for embedded C software is a strenuous and time-consuming task that does not scale well. With the accelerating pace of software release cycles, verification increasingly becomes the bottleneck in the embedded development workflow. This paper presents a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline as a solution for partial automation of the verification process. By grounding a large language model in project-specific artifacts, the approach reduces hallucinations and improves project alignment. An industrial evaluation showed that the generated tests are 100 % syntactically correct, with 85 % successfully passing runtime validation. The proposed solution has the potential to save up to 66 % of the testing time compared to manual test writing while generating 270 tests per hour.

</details>


### [159] [Preparing Students for AI-Driven Agile Development: A Project-Based AI Engineering Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09599)
*Andreas Rausch,Stefan Wittek,Tobias Geger,David Inkermann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个将敏捷实践与AI工程整合的项目式AI工程课程，通过案例研究展示学生在真实软件项目中应用AI工具进行敏捷开发，并评估其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI和智能工具正在重塑敏捷软件开发，但当前工程课程仍将敏捷方法与AI能力分开教授且以讲座为主，需要整合的教学方法来培养学生适应AI驱动的敏捷开发环境。

Method: 设计项目式AI工程课程，通过案例研究让二年级本科生团队在7个两周冲刺周期内开发真实软件产品，将AI工具嵌入日常敏捷工程任务（需求澄清、待办事项优化、架构推理、编码支持、测试和文档），并反思人类责任和质量。

Result: 初步结果表明整合方法支持AI辅助工程实践能力的培养，关键观察包括：敏捷教学需要适应工具的快速演变，口头验证对确保基础学习至关重要。

Conclusion: 整合敏捷实践与AI工程的项目式课程能有效培养学生适应AI驱动的敏捷开发环境，论文提供了课程设计原则、案例研究和评估证据，为教育工作者在AI时代设计敏捷项目式课程提供建议。

Abstract: Generative AI and agentic tools are reshaping agile software development, yet many engineering curricula still teach agile methods and AI competencies separately and largely lecture-based. This paper presents a project-based AI Engineering curriculum designed to prepare students for AI-driven agile development by integrating agile practices and AI-enabled engineering throughout the program. We contribute (1) the curriculum concept and guiding principles, (2) a case study of interdisciplinary, AI-enabled agile student projects, and (3) early evidence from a mixed-methods evaluation. In our case study, second-semester bachelor students work in teams over seven two-week sprints on a realistic software product. AI tools are embedded into everyday agile engineering tasks - requirements clarification, backlog refinement, architectural reasoning, coding support, testing, and documentation - paired with reflection on human responsibility and quality. Initial results indicate that the integrated approach supports hands-on competence development in AI-assisted engineering. Key observations highlight the need for agile teaching adaptations due to rapid tool evolution, the critical role of oral verification to ensure foundational learning. We close with lessons learned and recommendations for educators designing agile project-based curricula in the age of AI.

</details>


### [160] [An Empirical Study of Interaction Smells in Multi-Turn Human-LLM Collaborative Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09701)
*Binquan Zhang,Li Zhang,Lin Shi,Song Wang,Yuwei Qian,Linhui Zhao,Fang Liu,An Fu,Yida Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM代码生成中的交互异味问题，建立了首个交互异味分类体系，提出了多智能体框架InCE来改善多轮交互质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码生成中虽然表现出色，但在多轮交互中难以保持上下文一致性，现有基准主要关注最终输出的功能正确性，忽视了交互过程中的潜在质量问题（交互异味）。

Method: 1. 从WildChat和LMSYS-Chat-1M数据集中采样真实用户-LLM交互进行实证研究；2. 通过开放卡片分类建立首个交互异味分类体系；3. 定量评估6个主流LLM的交互异味分布；4. 提出Invariant-aware Constraint Evolution (InCE)多智能体框架，通过显式提取全局不变量和预生成质量审计来改善交互质量。

Result: 1. 建立了包含3个主要类别（用户意图质量、历史指令遵循、历史响应违反）和9个子类别的交互异味分类体系；2. 分析了不同LLM的交互异味分布；3. InCE框架在扩展的WildBench基准上显著提高了任务成功率并有效抑制了交互异味的发生。

Conclusion: 交互异味是LLM代码生成中的重要质量问题，需要专门关注。提出的InCE框架通过多智能体协作和显式约束管理，能够有效改善多轮交互质量，为提升LLM协作编程能力提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized code generation, evolving from static tools into dynamic conversational interfaces that facilitate complex, multi-turn collaborative programming. While LLMs exhibit remarkable proficiency in generating standalone code snippets, they often struggle to maintain contextual consistency during extended interactions, creating significant obstacles in the collaboration process. Existing benchmarks primarily emphasize the functional correctness of the final output, overlooking latent quality issues within the interaction process itself, which we term Interaction Smells. In this paper, we conduct an empirical study on sampled real-word user-LLM interactions from WildChat and LMSYS-Chat-1M datasets to systematically investigate Interaction Smells in human-LLM code generation tasks from the perspectives of phenomena, distribution, and mitigation. First, we establish the first taxonomy of Interaction Smells by manually performing open card sorting on real-world interaction logs. This taxonomy categorizes Interaction Smells into three primary categories, i.e., User Intent Quality, Historical Instruction Compliance, and Historical Response Violation, comprising nine specific subcategories. Next, we quantitatively evaluate six mainstream LLMs (i.e., GPT-4o, DeepSeek-Chat, Gemini 2.5, Qwen2.5-32B, Qwen2.5-72B, and Qwen3-235B-a22b) to analyze the distribution of Interaction Smells across different models. Finally, we propose Invariant-aware Constraint Evolution (InCE), a multi-agent framework designed to improve multi-turn interaction quality through explicit extraction of global invariants and pre-generation quality audits. Experimental results on the extended WildBench benchmark demonstrate that this lightweight mitigation approach significantly improves the Task Success Rate and effectively suppresses the occurrence of Interaction Smells.

</details>


<div id='cs.SC'></div>

# cs.SC [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Avoiding Big Integers: Parallel Multimodular Algebraic Verification of Arithmetic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09501)
*Clemens Hofstadler,Daniela Kaufmann,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 提出基于多项式推理的混合代数验证技术，通过多模推理避免大整数运算，显著提升算术电路验证效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于任意精度算术的算术电路字级验证方法在处理大操作数时计算开销巨大，需要更高效的验证技术

Method: 采用基于多项式推理的混合代数验证技术，结合线性和非线性重写，通过同态映像进行多模推理，并行计算不同素数模下的结果，避免大整数运算

Result: 在TalisMan2.0工具中实现该方法，在乘法器基准测试套件上评估，结果显示混合多模推理相比现有方法有显著改进

Conclusion: 提出的混合多模推理验证方法能有效避免大整数运算，显著提升算术电路验证效率，为大规模算术电路验证提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Word-level verification of arithmetic circuits with large operands typically relies on arbitrary-precision arithmetic, which can lead to significant computational overhead as word sizes grow. In this paper, we present a hybrid algebraic verification technique based on polynomial reasoning that combines linear and nonlinear rewriting. Our approach relies on multimodular reasoning using homomorphic images, where computations are performed in parallel modulo different primes, thereby avoiding any large-integer arithmetic. We implement the proposed method in the verification tool TalisMan2.0 and evaluate it on a suite of multiplier benchmarks. Our results show that hybrid multimodular reasoning significantly improves upon existing approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Clarifying the Compass: A Reflexive Narrative on Entry Barriers into HCI and Aging Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08818)
*Tianyi Li,Jin Wei-Kocsis*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 两位非老龄化研究背景的研究者反思HCI与老龄化研究跨学科合作的障碍，分析技术设计与老年人需求脱节问题，分享通过志愿服务培养同理心的个人经历


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到新兴技术设计与老龄化人群需求之间存在脱节，希望通过跨学科合作弥合这一鸿沟。作为非老龄化研究背景的研究者，他们希望从外部视角识别HCI与老龄化研究领域合作的障碍和缺口

Method: 采用反思性研究方法，分为两个部分：1) 分析技术设计与老年人需求脱节的观察；2) 通过在老年社区志愿服务的个人经历，培养对老年人的同理心和深入理解，并分享反思性思考

Result: 通过志愿服务经历，研究者发展了对老年人需求的更深层次理解和同理心，识别了HCI与老龄化研究跨学科合作的具体障碍，为促进该领域的合作提供了个人视角的见解

Conclusion: 跨学科合作对弥合技术设计与老年人需求鸿沟至关重要，研究者通过亲身参与和反思能够培养必要的同理心，为HCI与老龄化研究的有效整合提供有价值的视角

Abstract: This manuscript presents the perspectives and reflections of two researchers who were not previously engaged in aging research, regarding the gaps and barriers related to interdisciplinary collaboration on HCI and Aging research. The manuscript has two sections. In the first section, the authors discuss their observations on the disconnect between the needs of aging populations and the design of emerging technologies. The second section delves into their personal journey of developing empathy and a deeper understanding of older adults by volunteering in a senior living community, and shares their reflective thoughts on these experiences.

</details>


### [163] [NaviNote: Enabling In-situ Spatial Annotation Authoring to Support Exploration and Navigation for Blind and Low Vision People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08837)
*Ruijia Chen,Yuheng Wu,Charlie Houseago,Filipe Gaspar,Filippo Aleotti,Dorian Gálvez-López,Oliver Johnston,Diego Mazala,Guillermo Garcia-Hernando,Maryam Bandukda,Gabriel Brostow,Jessica Van Brummelen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了NaviNote系统，结合视觉高精度定位和智能体架构，支持盲人和低视力用户通过语音创建注释和导航，显著提升了导航性能和环境理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商业系统未经过评估，GPS定位精度不足（偏差数米），而高精度视觉定位技术为开发更准确、包容的注释系统提供了可能。研究发现盲人和低视力用户不仅需要注释系统，更需要精确的最后几米导航工具。

Method: 1. 首先进行形成性研究（24名BLV参与者），了解高精度技术需求；2. 开发NaviNote系统，结合视觉高精度定位和智能体架构，支持语音注释创建和导航；3. 评估研究（18名BLV参与者）测试系统性能。

Result: NaviNote显著提高了导航性能，支持用户更好地理解和注释周围环境。用户反馈表明系统不仅作为注释工具，还能提供精确的最后几米导航。

Conclusion: 基于视觉的高精度定位与智能体架构结合的系统能有效支持盲人和低视力用户的注释创建和导航需求。研究结果为未来无障碍注释创作系统提供了设计考虑。

Abstract: GPS and smartphones enable users to place location-based annotations, capturing rich environmental context. Previous research demonstrates that blind and low vision (BLV) people can use annotations to explore unfamiliar areas. However, current commercial systems allowing BLV users to create annotations have never been evaluated, and current GPS-based systems can deviate several meters. Motivated by high-accuracy visual positioning technology, we first conducted a formative study with 24 BLV participants to envision a more accurate and inclusive annotation system. Surprisingly, many participants viewed the high-accuracy technology not just as an annotation system but also as a tool for precise last-few-meters navigation. Guided by participant feedback, we developed NaviNote, which combines vision-based high-precision localization with an agentic architecture to enable voice-based annotation authoring and navigation. Evaluating NaviNote with 18 BLV participants showed that it significantly improved navigation performance and supported users in understanding and annotating their surroundings. Based on these findings, we discuss design considerations for future accessible annotation authoring systems.

</details>


### [164] [The Data-Dollars Tradeoff: Privacy Harms vs. Economic Risk in Personalized AI Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08848)
*Alexander Erlei,Tahir Abbas,Kilian Bizer,Ujwal Gadiraju*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 隐私泄露的不确定性（而非确定性风险）显著降低用户对AI个性化服务的采用率，用户愿意为透明度标签支付溢价


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究信息环境如何影响用户对数据泄露威胁的响应，填补隐私担忧如何影响AI采纳的知识空白

Method: 2×3组间实验设计（N=610），比较风险（30%泄露概率）与模糊性（10-50%范围）条件下隐私威胁对AI个性化采纳的影响

Result: 风险条件下AI采纳率无差异（约50%），模糊性条件下隐私威胁显著降低采纳率；用户愿意为隐私披露标签支付溢价；隐私泄露威胁不影响后续与算法的议价行为

Conclusion: 数据泄露的不确定性（而非隐私偏好本身）驱动用户避免使用个性化AI，强调透明度机制的重要性

Abstract: Privacy concerns significantly impact AI adoption, yet little is known about how information environments shape user responses to data leak threats. We conducted a 2 x 3 between-subjects experiment (N=610) examining how risk versus ambiguity about privacy leaks affects the adoption of AI personalization. Participants chose between standard and AI-personalized product baskets, with personalization requiring data sharing that could leak to pricing algorithms. Under risk (30% leak probability), we found no difference in AI adoption between privacy-threatening and neutral conditions (ca. 50% adoption). Under ambiguity (10-50% range), privacy threats significantly reduced adoption compared to neutral conditions. This effect holds for sensitive demographic data as well as anonymized preference data. Users systematically over-bid for privacy disclosure labels, suggesting strong demand for transparency institutions. Notably, privacy leak threats did not affect subsequent bargaining behavior with algorithms. Our findings indicate that ambiguity over data leaks, rather than only privacy preferences per se, drives avoidance behavior among users towards personalized AI.

</details>


### [165] [Investigating the Effects of LLM Use on Critical Thinking Under Time Constraints: Access Timing and Time Availability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08849)
*Jiayin Zhi,Harsh Kumar,Mina Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: LLM对批判性思维的影响取决于时间约束：在时间压力下，早期使用LLM能提升表现；在充足时间下，早期使用LLM反而会损害表现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注LLM对批判性思维的影响，但忽略了时间因素的作用。本研究旨在探究LLM访问时机（早期、持续、晚期、无LLM）和时间可用性（不足vs充足）如何共同影响批判性思维任务表现

Method: 采用被试间实验设计（n=393），参与者需基于多样化文档为真实世界场景做出理性决策。实验操纵两个变量：1）LLM访问时机（早期、持续、晚期、无LLM）；2）时间可用性（不足vs充足）。通过比较不同条件下的表现来检验时间约束的调节作用

Result: 发现时间反转效应：在时间压力下，早期或持续访问LLM能改善表现；但在充足时间下，早期或持续访问LLM反而损害表现。相反，独立开始任务（晚期或无LLM访问）则呈现相反模式

Conclusion: 时间约束从根本上决定了LLM是增强还是削弱批判性思维。在设计LLM支持和评估人机协作时，必须将时间作为核心考量因素

Abstract: The impact of large language models (LLMs) on critical thinking has provoked growing attention, yet this impact on actual performance may not be uniformly negative or positive. Particularly, the role of time -- the temporal context under which an LLM is provided -- remains overlooked. In a between-subjects experiment (n=393), we examined two types of time constraints for a critical thinking task requiring participants to make a reasoned decision for a real-world scenario based on diverse documents: (1) LLM access timing -- an LLM available only at the beginning (early), throughout (continuous), near the end (late), or not at all (no LLM), and (2) time availability -- insufficient or sufficient time for the task. We found a temporal reversal: LLM access from the start (early, continuous) improved performance under time pressure but impaired it with sufficient time, whereas beginning the task independently (late, no LLM) showed the opposite pattern. These findings demonstrate that time constraints fundamentally shape whether an LLM augments or undermines critical thinking, making time a central consideration when designing LLM support and evaluating human-AI collaboration in cognitive tasks.

</details>


### [166] [Unpacking Interpretability: Human-Centered Criteria for Optimal Combinatorial Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08856)
*Dominik Pegler,Frank Jäkel,David Steyrl,Frank Scharnowski,Filip Melinscak*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验发现，在同等最优的装箱方案中，人类偏好受三个可量化结构特性影响：与贪心启发式对齐、箱内简单组合、有序视觉表示


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法支持系统常返回难以理解的最优解，但有效的人机协作需要可解释性。当存在多个同等最优解时，人类需要选择，但缺乏对"什么使一个解比另一个更易理解"的精确解释

Method: 提出实验范式，让参与者在两个同等最优的装箱方案中选择更易理解的方案，量化分析解决方案的结构特性与人类偏好的关系

Result: 人类偏好可靠地追踪三个可量化结构特性：与贪心启发式对齐、简单箱内组合、有序视觉表示。最强关联出现在有序表示和启发式对齐，组合简单性也显示一致关联。反应时间证据混合，注视数据未显示可靠复杂性效应

Conclusion: 研究提供了基于特征的可解释性具体解释，将解决方案结构与人类偏好联系起来。通过识别可操作特性，使可解释性感知的优化和机器解决方案呈现成为可能，并为量化现实世界分配和设计任务中最优性与可解释性之间的权衡提供了路径

Abstract: Algorithmic support systems often return optimal solutions that are hard to understand. Effective human-algorithm collaboration, however, requires interpretability. When machine solutions are equally optimal, humans must select one, but a precise account of what makes one solution more interpretable than another remains missing. To identify structural properties of interpretable machine solutions, we present an experimental paradigm in which participants chose which of two equally optimal solutions for packing items into bins was easier to understand. We show that preferences reliably track three quantifiable properties of solution structure: alignment with a greedy heuristic, simple within-bin composition, and ordered visual representation. The strongest associations were observed for ordered representations and heuristic alignment, with compositional simplicity also showing a consistent association. Reaction-time evidence was mixed, with faster responses observed primarily when heuristic differences were larger, and aggregate webcam-based gaze did not show reliable effects of complexity. These results provide a concrete, feature-based account of interpretability in optimal packing solutions, linking solution structure to human preference. By identifying actionable properties (simple compositions, ordered representation, and heuristic alignment), our findings enable interpretability-aware optimization and presentation of machine solutions, and outline a path to quantify trade-offs between optimality and interpretability in real-world allocation and design tasks.

</details>


### [167] [Touching Emotions, Smelling Shapes: Exploring Tactile, Olfactory and Emotional Cross-sensory Correspondences in Preschool Aged Children](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08889)
*Tegan Roberts-Morgan,Min S. Li,Priscilla Lo,Zhuzhi Fan,Dan Bennett,Oussama Metatla*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究学龄前儿童（2-4岁）的跨感官对应关系，特别是嗅觉-触觉-情感对应，通过游戏化任务发现显著对应关系及背后的关联策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感官整合在学龄前快速发展，跨感官对应（不同感官感知相互影响的系统性方式）是这一过程的关键组成部分，但对此年龄段儿童的跨感官对应关系知之甚少。

Method: 采用游戏化任务研究26名学龄前儿童的嗅觉-触觉-情感对应关系，通过分析揭示支撑这些映射的关联策略。

Result: 发现感官模态之间以及感官模态与情感判断之间存在显著对应关系，进一步分析揭示了支撑这些映射的关联策略。

Conclusion: 贡献了关于早期儿童跨感官对应的实证见解、与学龄前儿童感官输入关联方式一致的设计指南，以及适用于该年龄组的可复制的跨感官认知研究方法。

Abstract: The use of a wide range of sensory modalities is increasingly central to technologies for learning, communication, and affective regulation. During the preschool years, sensory integration develops rapidly, shaping how children perceive and make sense of their environments. A key component of this process is cross-sensory correspondence: the systematic ways in which perceptions in different sensory modalities influence one another. Despite its relevance, little is known about cross-sensory correspondences in preschool-aged children (2-4 years). We present a study with 26 preschoolers examining smell-touch-emotion correspondences through playful tasks. We found significant correspondences both between sensory modalities and between sensory modalities and affective judgements. Further analysis revealed association strategies underpinning these mappings. We contribute empirical insights into cross-sensory correspondences in early childhood, design guidelines that align with how preschoolers relate sensory input, and a replicable method for probing cross-sensory cognition in this age group.

</details>


### [168] [Understanding the Use of a Large Language Model-Powered Guide to Make Virtual Reality Accessible for Blind and Low Vision People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09964)
*Jazmin Collins,Sharon Y Lin,Tianqi Liu,Andrea Stevenson Won,Shiri Azenkot*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究开发了基于大语言模型的AI"视力引导"系统，帮助盲人和低视力用户在VR社交环境中导航和获取信息，并通过用户研究发现用户在不同社交情境下对引导系统的使用方式和态度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着社交虚拟现实（VR）的普及，为盲人和低视力（BLV）用户提供可访问性变得越来越重要。虽然已有研究提出AI"视力引导"来帮助用户在VR中导航和回答问题，但尚未进行用户研究验证其有效性。

Method: 开发了基于大语言模型（LLM）的引导系统，并在虚拟环境中与16名BLV参与者进行研究。研究中使用了同盟者（confederates）扮演其他用户，观察参与者在不同社交情境下如何使用引导系统。

Result: 研究发现：当参与者独自一人时，他们将引导系统视为工具；但在有他人在场时，他们会以更友好的方式对待引导系统，如给系统起昵称、为其错误寻找外观相关的解释理由，并鼓励同盟者与引导系统互动。

Conclusion: 该研究深化了对引导系统作为VR可访问性多功能方法的理解，并为未来引导系统的设计提供了建议。研究表明AI引导系统在社交VR环境中具有潜力，用户会根据社交情境调整与系统的互动方式。

Abstract: As social virtual reality (VR) grows more popular, addressing accessibility for blind and low vision (BLV) users is increasingly critical. Researchers have proposed an AI "sighted guide" to help users navigate VR and answer their questions, but it has not been studied with users. To address this gap, we developed a large language model (LLM)-powered guide and studied its use with 16 BLV participants in virtual environments with confederates posing as other users. We found that when alone, participants treated the guide as a tool, but treated it companionably around others, giving it nicknames, rationalizing its mistakes with its appearance, and encouraging confederate-guide interaction. Our work furthers understanding of guides as a versatile method for VR accessibility and presents design recommendations for future guides.

</details>


### [169] [Design Guidance Towards Addressing Over-Reliance on AI in Sensemaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08903)
*Yihang Zhao,Wenxin Zhang,Amy Rechkemmer,Albert Meroño Peñuela,Elena Simperl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何设计GenAI增强的群体感知工具来支持协作工作和学习中的自主意义建构，避免过度依赖AI指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GenAI系统在支持协作意义建构时倾向于提供明确指令，导致群体被动跟随、产生过度依赖并侵蚀自主意义建构能力。需要设计更好的系统来促进自主意义建构。

Method: 基于对现有群体感知工具系统的文献调研，探索GenAI增强的群体感知工具设计，提出初步设计原则进行讨论。群体感知工具通过可视化协作数据、揭示成员差异来创造认知冲突，触发自主阐述和讨论。

Result: 提出了GenAI增强的群体感知工具的初步设计原则，这些工具通过隐性引导而非显性指令来支持自主意义建构的涌现。

Conclusion: GenAI增强的群体感知工具有潜力通过隐性引导支持协作工作和学习中的自主意义建构，避免过度依赖AI指令的问题，但需要进一步研究和设计原则的完善。

Abstract: Sensemaking in collaborative work and learning is increasingly supported by GenAI systems, however, emerging evidence suggests that poorly designed GenAI systems tend to provide explicit instruction that groups passively follow, fostering over-reliance and eroding autonomous sensemaking. Group awareness tools (GATs) address this challenge through implicit guidance: rather than instructing groups on what to do, GATs externalize observable collaboration data through visualizations that reveal differences between group members to create cognitive conflict, which triggers autonomous elaboration and discussion, thereby implicitly guiding autonomous sensemaking emergence. Drawing on an initial literature search of existing GAT systems, this paper explores the design of GenAI-augmented GATs to support autonomous sensemaking in collaborative work and learning, presenting preliminary design principles for discussion.

</details>


### [170] [Integrating Virtual and Augmented Reality into Public Education: Opportunities and Challenges in Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08970)
*Tanja Kojić,Maurizio Vergari,Giulia-Marielena Benta,Joy Krupinski,Maximilian Warsinke,Sebastian Möller,Jan-Niklas Voigt-Antons*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VR/AR在公共教育语言学习中的潜力与挑战研究：VR提升动机但词汇保留效果不明，AR增强情境学习但个性化有限，需改进界面设计、降低认知负荷并加强基础设施和教师培训


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索VR和AR作为变革性教育工具在公共教育语言学习中的潜力，旨在增强传统教学方法并解决现有教育差距，同时分析技术整合面临的挑战

Method: 基于两项实证研究，调查VR和AR辅助语言学习的机会与挑战，并提出公共部门有效实施的策略

Result: VR能提高学习动机和沉浸感，但对词汇保留的影响不明确，存在技术限制和认知超负荷问题；AR增强情境学习和可访问性，但面临可用性限制和个性化不足的挑战

Conclusion: 为促进有效采用，需改进界面设计、降低认知负荷、增加适应性，并确保足够的基础设施和教师培训；克服这些障碍将实现沉浸式技术在语言教育中的更有效整合

Abstract: Virtual Reality (VR) and Augmented Reality (AR) are emerging as transformative tools in education, offering new possibilities for engagement and immersion. This paper explores their potential in language learning within public education, focusing on their ability to enhance traditional schooling methods and address existing educational gaps. The integration of VR and AR in schools, however, is not without challenges, including usability, technical barriers, and the alignment of these technologies with existing curricula. Drawing on two empirical studies, this work investigates the opportunities and challenges of VR- and AR-assisted language learning and proposes strategies for their effective implementation in the public sector. The findings show that VR increases motivation and immersion but has an unclear impact on vocabulary retention, with technical limitations and cognitive overload identified as key challenges. AR enhances contextual learning and accessibility but faces usability constraints and limited personalization. To facilitate effective adoption, this paper recommends improving interface design, reducing cognitive load, increasing adaptability, and ensuring adequate infrastructure and teacher training. Overcoming these barriers will enable a more effective integration of immersive technologies in language education.

</details>


### [171] [Influence of Interactivity in Shaping User Experience and Social Acceptance of Mobile XR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08973)
*Tanja Kojić,Maurizio Vergari,Maximilian Warsinke,Sebastian Möller,Jan-Niklas Voigt-Antons*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨移动增强现实应用中交互程度对用户体验和社会可接受性的影响，发现两者之间存在复杂关系，强调设计需要平衡交互性、可用性和社会因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AR技术日益普及，理解不同交互程度如何影响用户感知和社会动态对于AR应用的设计和采用至关重要。需要研究AR交互中的身体动作如何影响用户体验和社会可接受性，特别是在公共场合中的舒适度和潜在社会障碍。

Method: 使用两个交互程度显著不同的商业MAR应用（IKEA和Virtlo）进行用户研究，考察与AR内容交互所需的身体动作如何影响用户体验和社会可接受性。

Result: 研究发现交互程度、感知可用性和社会因素之间存在复杂关系。高交互性应用可能带来更好的用户体验，但可能降低社会可接受性，特别是在公共场合。身体动作幅度较大的交互可能引发用户不适和社会障碍。

Conclusion: AR应用设计需要平衡交互性、可用性和社会可接受性。将社会可接受性纳入传统用户体验评估至关重要，这有助于AR技术无缝融入日常环境。研究为未来AR应用开发提供了重要见解，强调了考虑社会影响的重要性。

Abstract: This study investigates the impact of the Degree of Interactivity on User Experience (UX) and social acceptability (SA) in Mobile Augmented Reality (MAR) applications. As AR technologies become more prevalent, understanding how varying levels of interactivity influence both user perception and social dynamics is crucial for their design and adoption. Two commercially available MAR applications, IKEA and Virtlo, which differ significantly in their interactivity levels, were used to conduct a user study. The study examines how body movements required for interaction with AR content affect both UX and SA, shedding light on users' comfort levels and potential social barriers in public settings. The findings suggest a complex relationship between interactivity, perceived usability, and social considerations, emphasizing the need for a balanced design approach. This research provides valuable insights into the development of future AR applications by addressing not only usability but also the broader social implications of AR interactions. By integrating social acceptability into traditional UX evaluations, this study highlights its significance in ensuring the seamless integration of AR technologies into everyday environments.

</details>


### [172] [Dishonesty Tendencies in Testing Scenarios Among Students with Virtual Reality and Computer-Mediated Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08974)
*Tanja Kojić,Alina Dovhalevska,Maurizio Vergari,Sebastian Möller,Jan-Niklas Voigt-Antons*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究比较了虚拟现实(VR)和笔记本电脑环境下学生在考试中的欺骗行为，发现两种环境下的作弊程度完全相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR系统在电子学习领域具有创新潜力，能够建立完整的虚拟校园。虽然VR技术能提供比传统计算机媒介更强的学生互动体验，但学生经常在考试中采取欺骗行为。本研究旨在探索学生在VR环境和现实环境中的欺骗行为差异。

Method: 研究邀请了22名志愿者参与实验，每次实验包括两名参与者和一名监考人员。学生需要参加两次测试：一次在VR环境中，一次在笔记本电脑上。测试模拟真实的在线考试场景，目标是尽可能获得高分。研究过程中和结束后，参与者需要填写问卷以收集额外数据。

Result: 研究结果显示，在VR环境和笔记本电脑环境中发生的作弊行为数量完全相同，表明技术环境的变化并未影响学生的欺骗行为程度。

Conclusion: 尽管VR技术提供了更强的互动体验，但在防止考试作弊方面与传统计算机环境效果相同。这表明单纯的技术环境改变不足以减少学术不端行为，需要其他干预措施来应对学生的欺骗行为。

Abstract: Virtual reality (VR) systems have the potential to be an innovation in the field of e-learning. Starting with fully functional e-classes, VR technologies can be used to build entire e-campuses. The power of VR is that it allows for stronger contact with students than computer-mediated technology. Deceptive behaviour, both verbal and nonverbal, refers to intentional activities designed to deceive others. Students often engage in dishonest practices to make progress. Whether it is cheating on an exam, copying another student's essay, or inflating their GPA, the motivation for cheating is rarely simply a lack of preparation. Even though some may see academic dishonesty as an asset, the reality is that it can have major consequences. This poster demonstrates the findings from a study of students' deceitful behaviour during a test in VR and in real-life situations. For this user study, 22 volunteers were invited to participate, with each experiment involving exactly two participants and the examiner present in the room. Students were invited to take two tests: one in VR and one on a laptop. Their goal was to score as many points as possible by simulating a real-world online exam. Participants were requested to complete questionnaires during and after each experiment, which assisted in collecting additional data for this study. The results indicate that the amount of cheating that happened in VR and on a laptop was exactly the same.

</details>


### [173] ["Who wants to be nagged by AI?": Investigating the Effects of Agreeableness on Older Adults' Perception of LLM-Based Voice Assistants' Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09012)
*Niharika Mathur,Hasibur Rahman,Smit Desai*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨了语音助手（VA）的宜人性如何影响老年人对解释的感知，发现在常规场景中高宜人性助手更受信任，但在紧急情况下清晰度比温暖更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的语音助手越来越多地支持老年人居家养老，助手的宜人性如何影响老年人对解释的感知仍缺乏深入研究。需要探索在不同家庭场景（常规与紧急）中，助手的宜人性特质如何塑造老年人对解释的信任、同理心和喜爱度等感知。

Method: 研究采用实验方法，对70名参与者进行考察，比较不同宜人性水平（高vs低）的语音助手在常规和紧急家庭场景中提供的解释。评估了两种解释类型：实时环境解释和历史基于解释。测量了参与者对助手的信任度、同理心、喜爱度、感知智力等维度。

Result: 高宜人性助手在常规场景中被认为更值得信任、更具同理心和更受欢迎，但这些优势在紧急情况下减弱，此时清晰度比温暖更重要。宜人性不影响感知智力，表明社交语气与能力是可分离维度。实时环境解释优于历史基于解释。宜人性高的老年人对低宜人性助手的惩罚更强烈。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，AI可解释性需要超越"一刀切"的方法，平衡个性特质、情境和受众特征。在紧急情况下应优先考虑清晰度而非温暖，而常规场景中宜人性特质能增强信任和接受度。设计语音助手时应考虑用户个性与情境的交互作用。

Abstract: LLM-based voice assistants (VAs) increasingly support older adults aging in place, yet how an assistant's agreeableness shapes explanation perception remains underexplored. We conducted a study(N=70) examining how VA agreeableness influences older adults' perceptions of explanations across routine and emergency home scenarios. High-agreeableness assistants were perceived as more trustworthy, empathetic, and likable, but these benefits diminished in emergencies where clarity outweighed warmth. Agreeableness did not affect perceived intelligence, suggesting social tone and competence are separable dimensions. Real-time environmental explanations outperformed history-based ones, and agreeable older adults penalized low-agreeableness assistants more strongly. These findings show the need to move beyond a one-size-fits-all approach to AI explainability, while balancing personality, context, and audience.

</details>


### [174] [Tracing Everyday AI Literacy Discussions at Scale: How Online Creative Communities Make Sense of Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09055)
*Haidan Liu,Poorvi Bhatia,Nicholas Vincent,Parmit Chilana*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过分析Reddit上12.2万条创意社区对话，研究发现AI素养在创意实践中是动态、实践驱动且事件响应的，而非静态概念框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI素养框架多为自上而下的专家驱动模式，忽视了创意社区中素养如何有机形成。随着生成式AI重塑创意实践，理解社区中的AI素养发展变得日益紧迫

Method: 对80个创意导向的Reddit子版块进行为期三年的大规模分析，涵盖12.2万条对话，识别AI素养相关讨论的持续主题，并追踪话语如何随重大AI事件变化

Result: 识别出AI素养讨论的四个一致主题；发现创作者主要将AI素养框定为如何有效使用工具，强调实践和任务技能；AI能力和伦理讨论仅在重大事件期间激增；AI素养呈现动态、实践驱动和事件响应特性

Conclusion: AI素养在创意社区中是动态、实践驱动且事件响应的，而非静态或纯概念性的。这为研究者、设计者和政策制定者开发学习资源、社区支持和政策提供了重要启示

Abstract: Developing AI literacy is increasingly urgent as generative AI reshapes creative practice. Yet most AI literacy frameworks are top-down and expert-driven, overlooking how literacy emerges organically in creative communities. To address this gap, we performed a large-scale analysis of 122k Reddit conversations from 80 creative-oriented subreddits over a three-year period. Our analysis identified four consistent themes in AI literacy-related discussions, and we further traced how discourse shifted alongside major AI events. Surprisingly, creators primarily frame AI literacy around how to use tools effectively, foregrounding practice and task skills, while discussions of AI capabilities and ethics surge only around high-profile events. Our findings suggest that AI literacy is dynamic, practice-driven, and event-responsive rather than static or purely conceptual. This study provides insights for researchers, designers, and policymakers to develop learning resources, community support, and policies that better promote AI literacy in creative communities.

</details>


### [175] [A Text-Native Interface for Generative Video Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09072)
*Xingyu Bruce Liu,Mira Dontcheva,Dingzeyu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Doki是一个文本原生的生成式视频创作界面，通过单一文档中的文本写作实现视频制作，使视频创作与自然文本写作过程对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频创作需要学习专业复杂工具，而文本写作是人人都会的基本技能。Doki旨在让视频创作像写文本一样自然，降低创作门槛。

Method: 设计文本优先的界面，在单一文档中通过文本定义资产、构建场景、创建镜头、细化编辑和添加音频，提出文本优先设计原则，并通过实际案例展示功能。

Result: 通过为期一周的部署研究，评估了不同视频创作经验参与者的实际使用情况，证明了Doki作为生成式视频界面的有效性和可访问性。

Conclusion: Doki代表了生成式视频界面的根本性转变，提供了一种强大且易用的视觉故事创作新方式。

Abstract: Everyone can write their stories in freeform text format -- it's something we all learn in school. Yet storytelling via video requires one to learn specialized and complicated tools. In this paper, we introduce Doki, a text-native interface for generative video authoring, aligning video creation with the natural process of text writing. In Doki, writing text is the primary interaction: within a single document, users define assets, structure scenes, create shots, refine edits, and add audio. We articulate the design principles of this text-first approach and demonstrate Doki's capabilities through a series of examples. To evaluate its real-world use, we conducted a week-long deployment study with participants of varying expertise in video authoring. This work contributes a fundamental shift in generative video interfaces, demonstrating a powerful and accessible new way to craft visual stories.

</details>


### [176] [The Richest Paradigm You're Not Using: Commercial Videogames at the Intersection of Human-Computer Interaction and Cognitive Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09753)
*Jaap Munneke,Jennifer E. Corbett*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 商业电子游戏可作为认知科学与HCI交叉领域的研究环境，通过游戏内置的认知需求替代传统实验操控，使用屏幕录制、眼动追踪等工具研究感知、注意和执行功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学依赖实验室范式存在生态效度问题，HCI关注复杂交互环境但较少探究认知机制。商业电子游戏恰好位于两领域交叉点，提供认知需求真实、持续且有意义的天然研究环境。

Method: 提出"可供性-认知映射框架"作为游戏选择和实验设计的系统基础，推荐使用屏幕录制、眼动追踪和行为计时等最小观察工具包，在商业游戏中研究感知、注意和执行功能。

Result: 论证了商业电子游戏作为研究环境的可行性：游戏设计具有认知需求，能激励玩家参与，玩家行为具有一致性支持系统比较，游戏可供性结构替代了传统实验操控。

Conclusion: 商业电子游戏是认知科学与HCI交叉领域未被充分利用的研究环境，通过可供性-认知映射框架和最小观察工具包，可在游戏环境中有效研究认知过程，提高研究的生态效度。

Abstract: Synthesizing from Corbett and Munneke (2025), who demonstrated that questions originating in human-computer interaction (HCI) and game design can be answered through the theoretical toolkit of cognitive science, this perspective argues that commercial videogames represent a largely underutilized research environment at the intersection of these two fields. Cognitive science has long relied on carefully controlled laboratory paradigms to study perception, attention, and executive functioning, raising persistent questions about ecological validity. HCI, by contrast, has spent decades developing methods for studying behavior in rich, complex, interactive environments, but has been less concerned with what that behavior reveals about underlying cognitive mechanisms. Commercial videogames sit precisely at this intersection. They are cognitively demanding by design, motivating by nature, and consistent enough across players to support systematic behavioral comparison. The affordance structure of a game does the work that experimental manipulations typically require of the researcher, instantiating cognitive demands that are genuine, sustained, and meaningful to the player. We argue that perception, attention, and executive functioning can be meaningfully studied within commercial games using a minimal observational toolkit of screen recording, eye tracking, and behavioral timing. We propose an affordance-cognition mapping framework as a systematic basis for game selection and research design and offer practical methodological recommendations for researchers wishing to work in this space.

</details>
